# OMC-1 DESIGNER READING PACK — FULL (compiled from primary records)

Compiled 2026-09-29 from the primary records listed in §15 (Source coverage). Read this together with `Project/PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS.md` (rev. 6); it replaces reading the primary records themselves. It is not a summary: every test is given with its setup, method, the combination of design choices it ran under, its result as the source states it, and its conclusion. Where two sources disagree, both figures are kept and the disagreement is marked **[SOURCES DISAGREE]**. Estimates are marked as the sources mark them ("entropy", "estimate", "coded-rate estimate", "real code lengths"). Numbers are per plane Y/Cb/Cr unless the source gives only one plane (then the source's plane is named, and "luma-only" is stated where the source was luma-only).

All paths are relative to `/home/user/fromscratch/OMC_CLOUD/Project/` unless absolute. "Ledger S5.nnn" = `Codec/Current/LEDGER_SANDBOX_v2.md` entry S5.nnn.

Conventions used throughout:
- **today / codec** = OMC's REAL decodes (v537 binaries, read-only) unless the source says "offline model of today" (a numpy model) — those are different baselines and are labelled.
- **NEG** = VMAF-NEG (`Subagents/shared_tools/negscore.sh REF DIST W H FMT DEPTH NFRAMES`); "a (b)" = mean (worst frame).
- **g2 / g3** = generation 2 / 3 (re-encode of the previous generation's decode with identical rules).
- **oob / OOR** = out-of-range samples.
- **cell** = clip × rate point. Cell names: dng720 (dng 1280×720), dng1080 (dng 1920×1080), cf_gfx / gfx (448×256 graphics), spot = spotrobotL, floor = floorballgameL, hwy = highwaydriveL, volley = volleyballgameL, city = cityalley, cut24 / ext / ext10 / extFULL = synthetic rail extremes.
- Rates in bpp; "@0.5" = 0.5 bpp.

Contents
- §0 How the work was organised (who did what, in what order)
- §1 Binding owner rulings and requirements (with where and when each was made)
- §2 Method, instruments, footage
- §3 Background measurements on TODAY's codec that shaped the from-scratch work (§3.0 what today's codec is; SA11 reviews/tests; §3.8 SA9's legality steps W–Z3, the hold design and memo 010)
- §4 Expert answers to memo 010 and the coordinator's own from-scratch design (DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH) — falsified
- §5 Vertical-causal family tests (SA11: S, V, E, SM rounds)
- §6 SA12 "CLP-1" (5 rounds + unreported round 6)
- §7 SA13 "BSC-1" (2 rounds)
- §8 SA14 "NEST" (2 rounds + unreported round 3)
- §9 Consolidation, SA15 "CPP-LL" (2 rounds)
- §10 SA16 "CQP-1" (Fable 5.1 trial)
- §11 SA17 "CPV-1" + ideation partner SA17P
- §12 SA18 + ideation partners SA18P and SA18Q (STOPPED 19:45; final snapshot 19:50)
- §13 Cross-designer cause → effect
- §14 Open problems
- Appendix A Raw result lines (SA13 t-results, SA14 round-3 outputs)
- §15 Source coverage

## §1 Binding owner rulings and requirements

(PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS.md rev. 6 governs everything; these are added on top. Where a ruling has a ledger date it is given.)

### 1.1 Current-stage goals, in order (S5.354, S5.363 and refinement; BRIEF_SA18 §2)
1. **No seam or smudge.** No row position may be special. A smudge is an area slightly too dark or too light, grouped along a grid.
2. **Legal by construction, at zero cost in bits and quality.** Legality may never harm the image: it must move samples toward the source, never away.
3. **Byte-exact through CBR and baseband hops for unlimited generations, with only the image crossing.** Includes a mid-stream join and recovery after packet loss.
4. **Efficiency at least today's:** no noticeable bit increase vs today's OMC, judged on VMAF-NEG first, including the worst frame. Half of JPEG XS's bitrate (R = 2R) is a later bonus, not the bar now.
- History of this ruling: S5.354 (owner, 2026-09-27, on the vertical-causal route): "If the tests come back suggesting that three of the four issues are resolved using this method [seam/smudge, legality, generation exactness], let's proceed — let's worry about #4 [compression] later" (compression deferred, not dropped). S5.363 (owner): "By half bitrate, do you mean R=2R? That's not the primary goal here. The main goal is to reach the current list of four (at least the first three) … more efficiency is a bonus, not a requirement at this stage" → 1–3 required, 4 strongly wanted, R=2R deferred; the coordinator's reviews S5.361/S5.362 had wrongly weighted A1 (half-rate) and were corrected with SA12, SA13, SA14. S5.363 refinement: no NOTICEABLE bitrate increase vs today (guard), efficiency gains welcome, R=2R not the acceptance bar. S5.355 note: "The subagent's goal is to accomplish all Project Constraints" (every clause at once: half-rate mandate by eye, exact CBR, ramp, A4, A5, formats, C1–C8, Section G; DESIGN.md must map clause by clause) — later ordered by S5.363.

### 1.2 Artifacts
- No visible artifact of any kind, judged by eye: no seams, smudges, flattened texture, flicker in still areas, blocks or steps (BRIEF_SA18 §2).
- S5.344 (owner, "sigh. all disqualifying artifacts." — on the two named risks: seam line if the display blend is removed; strip-level quality step from an escalation ladder): design only by construction against disqualifiers; an escalation ladder (per-slice step change) violates zero-visible-steps and is NOT a design element; dense rail content is an OPEN problem stated plainly, never papered over with a visible fallback.
- S5.345 (owner): "'The only flaw' is the definition of smudges … a HUGE disqualifier … those issues are currently NOT handled, since we have smudges." → the display seam blend shifts the level of the rows either side of every slice boundary = the SMUDGE; the slice-boundary treatment (XSL cross-slice term + display blend) joins the replacement block. S5.345 note (owner): "test things to see if they actually produce what you fear, unless you already have an exact test".
- S5.357 (owner): "Did you specifically look for smudges — you lumped seams and smudges together and then only commented on seams." SEAM (row-phase error) and SMUDGE (level-map groupings, decode − source per LL-support block, steady-state frames, smudgegroups.py / artifactmap.py) are different artifacts; the coordinator had conflated them.
- S5.369 (owner ruling on design-created artifacts, on SA14's first-row excess and the coordinator's "one-line hold" direction): "This is an unforgivable artifact — the same type … as we've been working to eliminate. Creating a new one is exactly what the assignment said not to do — don't solve a problem by creating a new one somewhere else. … Try to patch that new problem is again eliminating the benefit of this design from scratch project. The whole point with designing from scratch is to prevent patching symptoms." Consequences: (1) SA14 item (d) (one-line hold) WITHDRAWN (a patch; coordinator error); (2) SA12 round-6 item 1 (re-phase slices to read the final row above = SA14's shifted causal slices) WITHDRAWN, and SA12's intra error continuation dropped (symptom treatment); (3) rule for all designers and reviews: an artifact a design creates disqualifies the DESIGN CHOICE that produces it; the designer goes back to that choice and redesigns so no row position is special by construction, within XS latency parity, < 1 ms incl. conversion, A5, no blend/mirror, no bit/dB cost — or restarts that part. Pass = identical per-row error statistics at every row phase, intra and inter, Y/Cb/Cr.
- Still areas must show zero change (short pack §1.2, citing today's 60–77 % of samples changing per frame, ledger S5.335; see §3.3, §3.4).
- No rung, floor or flat fallback slice (S5.361 review: "guard terminal flat strip = rung (disqualifier, must be impossible by construction)"; S5.371 round-2 item 1: guard firing = design-created visible region → must be impossible by construction).
- Owner memory rules also cited in the records: zero visible steps (any per-region parameter = a continuous field both ends derive); flatness bar: zero flat textured blocks at ≥ 0.5 bpp up to 4K and ≥ 1.0 bpp above 4K, every cell and plane (no design and not today's codec meets it); colour cast from lifting rounding is a disqualifier (the owner caught a +1-code cast once).

### 1.3 Legality and exactness
- Legality at zero cost in bits and quality, and "never away" (goal 2). S5.384 addendum (owner): "If we design something that doesn't work, then the design is the problem." No rule-relaxation question on never-away; go further up the root chain (averaged output, legality placement, representation). S5.384 addendum 2 (owner): "Just because they came up with two ideas, it doesn't mean those two ideas are the extent of the options" → widen the search (legality stage, transmitted description, transform structure, reading rule) before concluding.
- "Open" or "structural" is not an acceptable end state for goals 1–4 or A5; either solve at the root or say plainly the design fails (BRIEF_SA18 §2).
- Short pack §1.6 wording: "Requirements are never relaxed." "'Structural', 'open' or 'the price of' is not an acceptable end state for goals 1–4."
- Baseband and CBR hops pass ONLY the image; no hidden step the next encoder must undo (S5.346; short pack §1.5).

### 1.4 Latency, hardware, work
- Latency: never noticeably longer than JPEG XS excluding conversion (XS ≈ 32 lines), always under 1 ms including conversion, deterministic, 720p to 8K. S5.371 addendum (owner, 2026-09-28 ~09:00): 720p50 + 3:1 conversion at 0.991 ms is ACCEPTABLE ("always been tight"); improve only if free.
- Hardware: comfortably fits a Xilinx Zynq UltraScale+ ZU7EV-class FPGA; no extra parallel hardware to hide work.
- 8K memory: S5.371 addendum (owner challenged "1.65× memory traffic fits 8K"): the coordinator's relay was wrong — SA15 R11 counted external DDR bandwidth only (171 vs 103 Gbit/s at 8K60 4:2:2; 42 % of two x64 DDR4-3200); on-chip memory, the binding 8K limit (v537 `DDR_WINDOW_CACHE.md`: 8K 4:2:2 10-bit URAM 24.25/27 Mbit only with a narrowed store; 8K 4:4:4 12-bit does not fit), was not accounted, and the 7-bit per-coefficient step-memory state likely adds to it → full URAM/BRAM accounting enc+dec per format; if it no longer fits where today fits, redesign the per-coefficient state at the root, no bigger part. S5.371 addendum 2 (owner): "8K must fit. That's not an option." The 8K two-channel DDR setup is today's documented v537 setup (4320p60 two x64 DDR4-3200 channels OR one with no other master), not new → same memory subsystem as today at every format incl. single-channel 8K, on-chip and DDR, worst-case margin; redesign step-memory state / two-frame motion search at the root if over.
- Short pack §1.3: report on-chip AND external memory, "with the same method as that document" (`.work/v537/docs/DDR_WINDOW_CACHE.md`); external = two x64 DDR4-3200 channels, OR one channel with no other master.
- One decision per slice, like JPEG XS. No retry loops, no search, no iteration to convergence; at most one predetermined re-choice (BRIEF_SA18). "Parallel lanes that each code the slice count as trial coding" (short pack §1.3; coordinator intervention on SA16, S5.374). S5.350 (owner): no design that repeats work many times ("not a codec, a medieval painting").

### 1.5 Loss recovery (A5)
- S5.378 (owner corrections, 2026-09-29 10:20): (1) 15-frame recovery is not a win (JPEG XS = 1 frame; ≥ 4 frames highly visible) → bar: recovery < 4 frames. (2) Primary refresh = ON-DEMAND via the return path (owner ruling 2026-09-04, S5.37: bidirectional encoder↔decoder allowed, per-slice loss flags, refresh inside the fixed CBR budget, capped per frame/receiver; Task C measured heal at RT + 1, crawl halved, +0.4…2.1 NEG at low rates); scheduled wave only as one-way fallback. Short-pack detail of the S5.37 ruling: encoder↔decoder bidirectional communication allowed if it causes no network problem; the return path carries only per-slice loss/mismatch flags; a refreshed slice is intra INSIDE its fixed CBR budget (no added bandwidth); the encoder caps refreshes per frame and per receiver; Task C: heal at round trip + 1 frames whatever the loss size, ants tail halved, +0.4…2.1 NEG at low rates, no cell worse than −0.05 (ledger around lines 905–925 and 1510). JPEG XS recovers in 1 frame; ≥ 4 frames is highly visible. Coordinator error recorded: SA15–SA17 had been briefed with "no back channel" as primary. v537 still defaults to R = 8 (refresh_r default 8).
- S5.378 addendum 2: return-path links recover in < 4 frames via on-demand refresh; one-way links (satellite, multicast) use the scheduled refresh cycle as fallback (cycle length to be chosen from measured NEG cost at 8/12/16). Addendum 3: long refresh cycles on satellite/one-way links are likely unusable (damage trails too long) — an OPEN product problem, parked until the current four goals are solved; do not treat the 8/12/16 fallback as a solution. Addendum 4: on one-way links the owner prefers lower NEG/PSNR over trailing damage → include cycles 2/3/4 and all-intra in the (low-priority) fallback measurement. Addendum 5: do not design in a long refresh wave for one-way links; one-way mode = short recovery (few frames … all-intra) at a quality cost; drop mechanisms that exist only for a long wave. S5.380 addendum (owner): one-way links recover in < 4 frames (refresh cycle 2–3), NOT all-intra.
- BRIEF_SA18 §2b (binding): return-path links recover in UNDER 4 frames via on-demand refresh (primary); one-way: a refresh cycle giving recovery under 4 frames (SA17: cycle 2 works, cycle 3 gives 4 frames), NOT all-intra; no long-wave machinery. (BRIEF_SA18 §2 also still carries the older line "Loss recovery without a back channel: complete recovery within a small, bounded, documented number of frames (A5)"; §2b supersedes it.)
- Motion vectors must be TRANSMITTED: decoder-derived vectors (Design A) failed A5 and were deleted (S5.29); the coordinator's DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH wrongly copied "decoder-derived" from expert A (S5.349 item 8).

### 1.6 Formats, IP, measurement
- All formats: 8/10/12-bit, 4:2:0/4:2:2/4:4:4, full and limited range, SDR and HDR.
- Only open-standard, royalty-free, expired or own-work techniques; designers do not raise legal questions (the coordinator handles them). Flags the coordinator raised for the owner/legal team, not the designer: continuous vertical 5/3 + slices as rate units + "JPEG XS-shaped decision" = the JPEG XS architecture (S5.349 item 7); SA15's XS-like architecture (S5.371).
- RULE (owner 2026-09-27, S5.358): primary quality measurement = VMAF-NEG, secondary = per-plane PSNR; the eye decides. The coordinator had been reporting PSNR only — corrected. BRIEF_SA18 §2b: VMAF-NEG first for EVERY decision, PSNR Y/Cb/Cr second, always at REAL code lengths (entropy proxies mislead: SA15, SA16, SA17 all show it).
- Every quality statement and every render covers Y, Cb AND Cr side by side (~70 % of problems are chroma); luma-only results are rejected unread. S5.368 addendum (owner reminder "never only luma — always chroma"): SA14 round-2 smudge groups, grille |err|, worst-frame figures were luma-only and its renders held NO Cb/Cr maps; the coordinator's review had not caught it → every number and render per plane with legends; review checklist item added: reject any luma-only result before judging it. S5.368 addendum 2 (owner): shared tools stay unchanged; chroma is obtained by running them per plane (`--plane Y/Cb/Cr`); no settings hook.
- An efficiency number without artifact checks is not evidence (S5.355 note, owner: "HVBC too was more efficient than today's codec. But it also had way more artifacts at every bpp … because it allowed crap to go through." — the coordinator had called SA12's interim "promising" too early). An ENTROPY ESTIMATE is not evidence of efficiency (SA15's lead disappeared at real code lengths); use real code lengths from static tables trained on footage disjoint from the test cells.
- No compressed footage: soccer, soccer2 are compressed broadcast; officewalk is excluded (illegal source).

### 1.7 Design method (owner)
- S5.346 (owner): "what all the experts said is what you need to take to heart: don't patch things. Even when I tell you not to, you still try to tinker. Go to the very beginning, look at things from scratch, and build something that doesn't require patching. As long as we patch, nothing will work." → all work on the patched sandbox trees STOPPED (rtree2/3/4 frozen as measurement history; T1/T2 halted, partial results kept; only T3 finished).
- S5.330 (owner, before S5.346): "we need to look at larger changes if everything we test keeps failing. If a larger chunk of the codec needs to be replaced, then we need to do that, rather than making patches." → decision to replace the LEGALITY + GENERATION-EXACTNESS block as one design (details §3.8), with the owner's sequence: record check → design document → adversarial design review → build → adversarial build review (all formats/depths/chroma/rates, open standards) → eye checks (full-frame colour level + |diff|, unmarked + _grid) → owner; owner screens: no seams, no dB, no bits, no artifacts, both latency bars, ZU7EV comfortable, royalty-free.
- S5.331 (owner): "any tests you want to run need to happen after you've reviewed documentation." The coordinator's Step W brief had been sent before the documentation check — recorded as a process error.
- S5.351 (owner): "test a few more things before writing back"; "what causes the need for the smoothness step? keeping it is patching."
- S5.352 (owner method, binding): "If a perfect solution breaks patch X, ask why it breaks X and why X exists; if X exists because of another problem, ask why that problem exists — patching it or nipping it in the bud?" (root-cause chain).
- S5.355 notes (owner): "Does the designer know that it's building an HVBC artifact?" and "Don't tell it to stop explicitly. It should just know it's going down that path. If it knows a better way than HVBC, great, but it needs to be aware." (awareness only; design choices are the designer's own).
- S5.369: a design-created artifact disqualifies the design choice (see 1.2).
- S5.384 (owner concern: SA18 copied SA17's model and began repairing CPV-1 = patching and lost innovation): earlier designs are evidence, not templates; write 2–3 genuinely different own architectures before choosing; the previous model is a measuring instrument only. Coordinator error recorded: MEMO_SA17_FINDINGS §5 had framed SA17's parts as "the platform that now works" — reworded to "shown achievable — evidence, not a template".
- S5.384 addenda: "If we design something that doesn't work, then the design is the problem"; "two ideas are not the extent of the options".
- BRIEF_SA18 work rules: "You design; you do not build a product. Be conservative with effort and tokens: grep first, then read only what matters, and keep experiments small and decisive"; the records LEDGER_SANDBOX_v2.md / LEDGER_v5_3_5.md are to be grepped, "do not read them whole"; BRIEF_SA18 opening: "Six earlier designers (SA12–SA15 and SA17 on Opus, SA16 on Fable 5.1) worked on this exact assignment; SA17 was the closest. None passed."
- Before proposing any element, grep the records and memos; if it was tried and failed, drop it or explain precisely why the failure does not apply (all briefs).
- Prior attempts: OMC (today's codec, `.work/v537`, READ-ONLY) failed on smudges and seams along the slice grid, flattened texture, flicker in still areas, and illegal pictures whose clipping broke generation exactness and whose repair added artifacts, plus search-heavy work and a bitrate short of the half-rate goal (S5.355); HVBC (`.work/hvbc`, `Issues_to_fix`) failed on a 16×16 tile grid and waxy flat patches at every bpp — use only as a list of failures.

### 1.8 Other owner rulings recorded in this period
- S5.371 addendum 3 (owner, 2026-09-28): the a7 compressed reference store is to be examined once the four current goals are truly solved, before anything else; extra bits on fast motion is dangerous — fast motion is OMC's main chance to prove its bitrate value vs JPEG XS → the a7 gate includes no noticeable bit increase on the four motion cells (separately, worst frame). Not sent to SA15.
- S5.372 addendum 6: the owner caught a coordinator inconsistency (promised "finish round 2, then stall rule if hard problems open", then proposed a round 3) → no round 3.
- S5.390 (owner, 2026-09-29, on SA18): still-area refinement "events" must not look like pulsating refreshes — report per-event change counts/amplitudes Y/Cb/Cr, time series (each event smaller, ≤ 3 per sample, then zero), spatial uniformity maps, renders either side of an event; a visible pulse disqualifies; root question why refinement must come in discrete events. Addendum: "If we truly update only when something changes, then refreshes on schedule would not be needed" → no scheduled / still-age refinement events; update a region only on change (source changed, or decoded-vs-source error above a threshold), encoder rule, decoder unaffected; catch-up exactly once, no pulse; loss-recovery refresh stays as ruled (on-demand; one-way cycle 2). Addendum 2: motion quality (no big drop on motion) is an important avenue, queued for the three-agent big-picture phase, not now. (Detail and SA18's response: §12.1, §12.2.)
- S5.391 (owner, 2026-09-29 19:15, on multiple references): "I can see it benefiting PREDICTION but not in pulling most of a frame out of a store ... we are still working at no noticeable latency from JPEG XS. So prediction, yes; using it as a library, absolutely no." → multi-reference PREDICTION queued as a big-picture research question (it would need the owner's revision of C2 "exactly one previous frame"; to be judged on bits saved on motion cells vs recovery, join, memory and latency); a frame LIBRARY / long-term store = absolutely no.
- S5.393 (OWNER RULING on C2, 2026-09-29): C2's one-frame limit was written for SIZE → multi-frame PREDICTION is PERMITTED if the reference frames are compressed and nothing else is damaged (A5 < 4 frames with no damage hiding in older references, mid-stream join, generation exactness, XS latency parity, 8K memory ≤ today, quality); frame LIBRARY / long-term store still OUT; PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS.md not edited; queued with a7 for the big-picture phase. Addendum (trio rule): no meaningful progress soon → ideas round, documentation update, shut all three, new group of three; S5.394: the coordinator judges IMMEDIATE progress; no ideas round for the SA18 trio.

## §2 Method, instruments, footage (as given to designers; BRIEF_SA18 §4, short pack §2)

- Sandbox: own folder under `Subagents/`; read anything, change nothing outside. Tests: small numpy or C, FULL frames only (never crop). Footage at another size must be Lanczos-resampled, never cropped (floor720 = Lanczos 720p of floorballgameL).
- Footage: raw .yuv under `.work/arms/`; motion cells floorballgameL, highwaydriveL, spotrobotL, volleyballgameL; also dng720, dng1080, cf_gfx; rail extremes cut24 (0↔1023 plates butting onto 0…895 ramps, 10-bit) and ext (generators in SA13's and SA15's sandboxes; `SA7_legality_v2/arms/cut24.yuv` 256×64, `ext_10_422_l0.yuv` = extFULL 512×128); soccer, soccer2, officewalk excluded.
- Today's real decodes: `Subagents/SA7_legality_v2/out/DM/*_a0.d.yuv` (ptree5 base, cross-slice term on, gamut repair on, 12 frames; dng720 @0.5, dng1080 @0.5, spot @1.0; exact CBR) and `Subagents/SA15_design/out/today/` (floor/volley/hwy, decoded by SA15 with read-only v537 binaries).
- Tables: train on disjoint clips (cityalley, bosphorus, readysetgo, trafficlightsL, winterdriveL); floorballtrainL and highwayviewL resemble test cells; SA15 found training without them no worse.
- Owner's tools, UNMODIFIED: `Subagents/shared_tools/smudgegroups.py` (owner setting thr 6 / density 0.4, groups ≥ 8 blocks; tool default thr 12 / 0.5), `artifactmap.py` (`--plane Y`, `--plane Cb`, `--plane Cr`), `flatplane.py`, `rowphase.py`; `.work/v537/harness/levelmap.py`; `SA7_legality_v2/notes/gridmap.py`; ants instrument `.work/v537/tests/ants.py`; `texstat.py`.
- Renders: colour decode + per-plane level maps and |decode − source| maps with a colour LEGEND, unmarked and with the slice grid; a one-row feature magnified to ≥ 4 output pixels (8×8-mean maps cannot show a one-row step — S5.367 review).
- Jobs: nice -n 19; check exit codes; /tmp is RAM (delete large .yuv); ≤ ~6 parallel jobs of ~2.6 GB (SA15 crashed at 7–10); run long batches detached so they survive a session stop; finish a queue before changing code unless there is a correctness bug.
- RESUME.md in the sandbox, updated after every finished item (quota can end a session); required contents (BRIEF_SA18 §4, short pack §2): state, item status/table with numbers, rejected ideas with reasons, running jobs, open problems, next steps.
- Before trusting a test bench, reproduce a known result exactly (e.g. SA17's dng720 @0.5 NEG 88.97 (88.10)) and one gen-2-identical run (short pack §2; reason given there: "SA15 found model bugs late"; SA18 did so, §12).
- Deliverable (BRIEF_SA18 §5): DESIGN.md, written incrementally: (1) full encode/decode path (transform/prediction, quantisation, exact-CBR rate control, temporal, entropy, slices/packets, loss, legality, resolution conversion); (2) legality and exactness proof sketch + measurements incl. join and loss; (3) why none of the artifacts of OMC, HVBC or earlier designers can occur, with per-row-phase statistics; (4) worst-case work per slice as a closed formula vs JPEG XS, FPGA fit 720p–8K, on-chip and DDR memory vs today, latency per format; (5) efficiency vs today's real decodes per plane, NEG first incl. worst frame, at matched REAL coded rates; (6) IP provenance; (7) prior failures checked; (8) risks. Final message: short summary with every figure per plane and the weakest point.
- JPEG XS reference NEG (SVT build, recorded by SA12, S5.364) at 0.5/1/2 bpp: dng720 72.1/89.3/94.8; spot 87.7/96.6/98.8.

## §3 Background measurements on TODAY's codec (SA11 and SA9, 2026-09-27, before and during the from-scratch turn)

These are read-only documentation reviews and instrument runs on today's codec (v537 and the patched sandbox trees rtree3/rtree4). They define the artifacts the from-scratch designs must not have.

### 3.0 What today's codec (OMC v537) is — as described to the experts (memo 010 §2) and in the short pack §1.7
- Slices: each frame cut into horizontal slices of 16 rows (8 at 720p); each slice has a bit budget; a causal BANK lets unspent bits flow to later slices of the same frame, wire cap 2× a slice's nominal budget.
- Transform: integer lifting wavelet, 5/3 (reversible JPEG 2000 filter) with an integer 9/7-like filter (9/7-M) at the two finest levels, vertical and horizontal, several levels (the asymmetric 2V×5H structure — 2 vertical, 5 horizontal levels — per §4.2/§5); a CROSS-SLICE (XSL) term links each slice's top to the last rows of the slice above; a small display-side BLEND smooths the two rows either side of each slice boundary and the next encoder undoes it exactly before re-encoding.
- Temporal: per band, intra (coefficients) or inter (difference from the forward transform of the motion-compensated previous decoded frame); motion vectors — memo 010 said "derived from previously decoded frames", but OMC's vectors are encoder-derived from committed history and TRANSMITTED (§1.5, §4.3 item 8); rolling intra refresh: every 8th frame per slice, staggered (R = 8).
- Plan: per slice (profile, Q, number of refinement steps, optional partial chunks) → a power-of-two step per band; rate control tries plans until the slice fits.
- Quantiser: dead-zone scalar, power-of-two steps, 9/16 dead zone on finer bands, small texture bias; reconstruction at fixed points of each cell.
- Entropy: static tANS tables per band.
- Decoder: tANS decode, dequantise, add prediction, inverse transform, clip every sample to the legal range, then the boundary blend. Hops: the generation LOCK (candidate walk + lock_verify) re-derives the plan at the next encoder (§3.7), plus the repair engine (gamut_strict) at the first encoder.
- Short pack §1.7: "A slice-based integer wavelet with temporal prediction and static tANS", which failed on smudges/seams along the 8/16-row grid (bottom row mirrored; the blend added to hide the seam shifts those rows' level — "The blend IS the smudge"), flattened texture, still-area flicker, and illegal pictures (overshoot → decoder clip → next encoder cannot reproduce; every fix added artifacts or enormous repeated work).

### 3.1 Seam / blend / smudge documentation review (SA11_plan/seam_R.txt; ledger S5.347)
Abbreviations in the source: LSv2 = LEDGER_SANDBOX_v2; L53 = LEDGER_v5_3; T5 = `.work/v537/docs/TEMPORAL_T5.md`; XSL = `.work/v537/docs/XSL.md`; BS = BITSTREAM.md; SL12 = SESSION_LEDGER_2026-08-12; HV = HVBC CONSOLIDATED_FINDINGS_TILES_2026-07-17.

**How slices are joined today, and why.**
- 1.1 Slices are coded independently (slice_h lines); rows 0 and sh−1 carry 30–60 % excess quantisation-noise energy; the eye integrates it into a horizontal line at every join; "the incumbent has none" (the incumbent = JPEG XS; XSL.md "Why it exists"); BITSTREAM.md ~872–874 quotes the band-fix memo §21–26: "eye gate passed with this fix".
- 1.2 Cross-slice (XSL) transform term: slice k's vertical level-1 lifting takes d[−1] = r15 − 2 r14 + r13 of slice k−1's COMMITTED reconstruction (was a copy of d[0]); computed per slice by xsl_prep (rtree4 codec.c:9802), fed to prediction forward, reconstruct_slice inverse (both ends) and source forward. Live in the shipped build; setting it to 0 costs −0.17…−0.46 dB mean, −3.7 dB worst frame (spotrobotL @1.0), chroma ≤ 0.03 (S5.206). The d[−1] term feeds the level-1 update (S5.202).
- 1.3 Boundary edit / display blend, level 3 (minor 9, shipped v4.14 default): row 0 blended toward (prev r15 + r1)/2, prev r15 toward (r14 + r0)/2, cap ±lim, applied to reference AND display. Cap (lim) normative, rate-derived: 8 codes (10-bit) below 0.75 bpp on coded samples, 4 at/above; ×((maxv+1)>>10) by depth; 4:4:4 threshold 1.125 bpp; 0 at 8-bit (F-14). Eye-chosen (SL12 PART V: cap 4/8/16 sweep @0.5 seam +1.23/+0.67/+0.40, @1.0 +0.00/−0.35).
- 1.4 T5 form (current): reversible two-step lifting cascade: step 1 row15 += clamp(div(row0 − row14), ±lim); step 2 row0 += clamp(div(row15' − row1), ±lim); rows 14 and 1 never written; unconditional in the wide domain. LOOPFREE (candidate L, OMC_XSL_LOOPFREE=1, shipped): the interior edit is DISPLAY-ONLY (loop gain 9.6 → 1.9 %/frame @2.0, 4.9 → 0.2 % @0.5, 72–90 % of the seam repair retained); verified loopfree = 1 at runtime (S5.202). CASC (four-row cascade) default 0.
- 1.5 Refresh barriers (A5): a slice intra-refreshed this frame takes no cross-slice terms and no edit; slice k skips the retro edit of k−1 when k−1 is refreshed; without them a lost slice's divergence "decay stalls at ~10 codes and creeps one slice per cycle". Barrier DISPLAY blend: raw boundaries get the same cascade on the EMITTED picture only (step excess 3.1 → 6.6 codes without it, 3.3 with it). H0 measured it: with zero residuals only displayed row 0 / last row next to a refreshed slice change (350/880/1,188 samples).
- 1.6 Un-blend at the next encoder: omc_enc_frame applies omc_xsl_unblend to every input frame (exact inverse of the interior cascade + barrier display blend, bottom-up), detector-free; on masters it pre-distorts boundary rows within ±lim which the decoder's forward edit cancels; 2-line lookahead. Measured on masters: the blend still removes 54–70 % of seam step excess (cine @0.5 +1.69 vs +4.36 no-edit codes; @1.0 +0.79 vs +2.64; gfx444 @0.5 +1.35 vs +2.95; @1.0 +0.77 vs +1.88; source ~+0.2). Always un-blending first-generation material loses ~1/3 of the repair (beach @0.5 +2.903 → +4.873).
- 1.7 Owner rulings: T5 ~446 "XSL must remain on: its seam blend is essential at and below 0.5 bpp; cannot be removed"; S5.344 the display seam blend stays; S5.345 CORRECTION: the blend = the SMUDGE; the slice-boundary treatment joins the replacement.
- 1.8 Legality interaction: the blend is non-convex — 340 of 343 out-of-range blend writes on dng720 @0.5 have every operand in range; bound ±lim, two rows in sixteen (S5.233); committed = clip(blend(clip(recon))) adopted for R1. The four-row cascade + outer clip is not one-to-one at rail samples (reference differs by −1…−6 codes where the displayed value is identical; slice k+1's term reads rows the cascade later edits, S5.296). Cross-slice propagation: slice k−1's clipped rows shift slice k's top-row coefficients by ~1 code (S5.269: XSL off → 532 unaffected off-lattice coefficients fall to 109). ICL → XSL → bank: extLIM Cb −14.40 dB on slice 13 (S5.341/S5.342).
- 1.9 Smudge / grille records (720p, 8-row slices): S3.13: owner's method adopted (smudgegroups.py: level map minus source's, |level| > 6, density 0.4, groups ≥ 8 blocks); 720p @0.5 f2: 14 luma groups / 247 blocks (grille rows 650–687 one 88-block group), 5 Cb groups / 69 blocks; artifactmap defaults (|level| > 12, 0.5) found NO group; the legality clamp does NOT remove the visible smudges (blotch deep blocks 105 → 13 only); grille/text smudges = texture loss inside 8-row slices at 0.5 bpp. S3.21: grille luma |err| 39.7 @0.5 sh8, 38.0 sh16, 28.7 @0.75, 22.5 @1.0; HP energy dec/src 0.70/0.96/0.99/0.96; "rate deficit on that part of the picture". S3.22: black class new since v4 (dark blocks < −20 on f0: v4.14 sh16 21, sh8 42, v5.3 72, +step 98). S5.90: 720p flatness = sect.80 fill-reach rule at sh 8 (reach 64: 3.66/3.57 % Y; reach 0: 0.08 %; 16-row 0.11 %). S5.91: reach 0 fixes FLATNESS but NOT SMUDGES (8-row f11 6 groups / 226 blocks, largest 177, rows 654–675; reach 0 4 / 198, largest 138; 16-row 0 groups every plane). S5.99: per-frame smudge blocks f2..f11 167 150 148 120 62 61 58 44 78 226; not the refresh wave; --refresh 1 gives 2,748–2,856 blocks/frame (12–45× worse); tracks source motion r = +0.78; carrier = inter residual size that 8-row geometry (8 → 4 → 2 vertical samples) cannot represent. S5.102: the 720p grille = three artifacts: TEAR (fill ON × repair × refresh slice × exact CBR; slice 83 Q 9, |err| 55.1, level −15.4 codes full width), SMUDGE with fill OFF (refresh slice starved: Q 8, 0 steps, |err| 25.6 vs 15–17), FLATNESS at both rates. S3.10/S3.11: per-row error within the slice (720p sh8 @0.5): 14.7 15.9 14.3 16.9 13.5 17.4 15.8 19.3; odd rows +8–15 %, last row +23–30 %; display-side arms leave boundary-row error unchanged.

**Attempts to remove the seam line / smudges — failed or limited (F1–F17).**
- F1 XSL level 3 (in-loop, non-invertible blend): generation loss beach 3.0 bpp sh8 −8.17 dB over 6 gens (no edit −0.30); lock fires 0/270 slices vs 73/270 without the edit. Replaced in T5.
- F2 XSL levels 4–6 (strength from emptiness / seam measurement, recomputed per frame): level 6 @1.0 +2.06 ants-tail points and +0.20 box boil vs level 3; removed in T5.
- F3 XSL level 7 / T5 cascade + mandatory un-blend: seam repair kept (level 7 @0.5 sh16 city +6.978 vs level 3 +6.831 step excess) but needs the un-blend; on masters loses ~1/3 of the repair. Identified by the owner as the smudge carrier (S5.345).
- F4 Candidate K (OMC_XSL_CDIV, chroma divisor 2): FALSIFIED — relocates the seam from row phase 0 to 1: cityalley Cb phase-0 step 4.13 → 2.98, phase-1 3.27 → 3.85, total grid-locked error +68 %; pitch worse on chroma 18/20 cells.
- F5 OMC_XSL_SPREAD (split two-step ramp): pitch up to −61 % (dng chroma) but BREAKS A4 (8 of 12 chains fail pixels-gen2; G-T5-XSL2/XSL3a fail) — writes rows 14/1 that the inverse reads.
- F6 OMC_XSL_CASC (four-row cascade): invertible, passes A4 11/11, but RELOCATES the artifact into the G7 class: dng 1080p blocks > 40: 3 → 68 (casc 1), 61, 64; 720p 63 → 101 / 63 / 35; default 0.
- F7 Display-side arms: pitch statistic falls up to 40 %, boundary-row error unchanged (last row +23–30 % in all 18 cells).
- F8 OMC_VEXT (linear continuation replacing the whole-sample mirror at the slice bottom, both vertical levels): removes the replication (Cb 0.673 → 0.101) but PITCH 0.151 → 0.219 / 0.301, −3.9 NEG, row-phase evenness 19.5 → 45.6 % — rejected; later "WORSE on the error-profile face on all three cells (+49/+42/+48 % at strength 2)" (S5.103).
- F9 OMC_FILLROWS (fill only the last N rows): PITCH 0.151 → 0.315 / 0.343 / 0.295 — creates a slice-pitch texture modulation.
- F10 Grain fill as the cure: luma pitch 0.151 → 0.039 at full amplitude, but at the shipped quarter amplitude 0.083 (3× XS @0.5 on dng, 8× on cityalley) — headline withdrawn; fill trades pitch/flatness against crawl and G7 blotch; fill now off by default.
- F11 Normative last-row rung (finer quantiser on the last row of vertical highpass bands): last-row excess +23.1 → +5.3 % (dng422), +22.8 → +3.9 (dng720) but BREAKS the generation lock (committed shift read at ~30 sites) and repair passes +24–68 % (S5.103).
- F12 16-row slices at 720p: clears grille smudges (0 groups) and flatness but latency 1.139 ms with conversion > 1 ms A2 bar — refused (S5.89, S5.91); S3.21: 16-row restores HP energy 0.70 → 0.96 but not pattern (corr 0.66) and worsens dark speckle. SL12 PART V: seam excess at sh16 lower than sh8 in every row (city @0.5 f0 +10.32 vs +12.71) — height not the cause of the cold-frame banding.
- F13 Fill reach 0 at sh8: fixes flatness (3.66 → 0.08 %), not smudges (226 → 198 blocks); blotch n > 40 +20 %.
- F14 Refresh period / all-intra against the 720p smudge: --refresh 1 is 12–45× worse.
- F15 Blend-aware in-cell legaliser (Step Y): moves clipping from outer to inner, total lower only on 2 cells; not adopted (S5.341).
- F16 Cross-slice term removed (d1m = 0): −0.17…−0.46 dB mean, −3.7 dB worst; seam level worse (dng720 row-15 bias −0.344 → −0.452; cf_gfx row 13 −0.002 → −0.532) (S5.206).
- F17 HVBC boundary failures: pixel-domain boundary blend REJECTED by eye twice ("blending two correct-but-different colours makes a third wrong one"); oracle arc of interval-bounded decoder reconstructions: v1 too soft, v2 facet creases, v4/v5 hard edges, v7 multigrid "a blurry canvas" (diffusion falsified), v8 crosshatch/lines; display gradient-fill / ramp filter (interval-bounded, display-only) ease borders but cannot remove the checkerboard; mode-boundary sig-mask coordination REJECTED; spanning transform through gradient tiles: spec as written 2.3× → ~20× boundary energy; quantiser checkerboard (Mechanism C) untouched by every boundary fix, near-lossless step 1 only at 7.2× bits, forward dither +206 % bpp. HVBC died on 16×16 boundaries. [Addition from ledger S5.331, the same HVBC §10 arc in full: v1 too soft, v2 facet creases, v4/v5 hard edges, **v6 a clamp bug (cast +5.5)**, v7 multigrid "a blurry canvas" (diffusion falsified as a category), v8 crosshatch/lines.]
- NOT FOUND in the records: overlapping / extended slices (no entry). Self-contained slices = XSL level 0 / off (v4.14 archive default). Experts A/B/C listed "a cross-slice term computed from decoded rows, or self-contained slices" — a suggestion, not measured.

**Invertible from the decoded picture alone (no hidden undo)?** YES, inside the transform, no undo: the cross-slice d[−1] term; XSL off / level 0 self-contained slices; 16-row slices; OMC_VEXT. ONLY WITH AN UNDO STEP (next encoder's mandatory omc_xsl_unblend): level 7 / T5 cascade, LOOPFREE display-only interior edit, barrier display blend, OMC_XSL_CASC, candidate K — the decoded picture is the BLENDED one; recovering the coded reconstruction needs the un-blend, which pre-distorts masters, is not one-to-one at rail samples with the outer clip (S5.296) and is non-convex (S5.233). NO: XSL level 3 and levels 4–6 (row moved toward a target computed from its own value; "cannot be undone"); OMC_XSL_SPREAD; HVBC pixel-domain blend and display-only filters. Not a transform question: last-row rung (quantiser), fill / FILLROWS / fill reach.

**What causes the seam line without a blend.** The vertical 5/3 whole-sample mirror at the slice's last pair degenerates the last predict to a first difference: x[last] = H[last] + x[last−1]; when H[last] quantises to 0 (common at 0.5 bpp, commoner on chroma) the last row is an exact copy; two vertical levels compound it (bottom quarter of the slice collapses to one row). Duplicate-row fraction rows 13–14 (1080p sh16 @0.5): Y 0.532 / Cb 0.671 vs interior 0.12 / 0.10, source 0.029 / 0.027; tracks slice height (moves to phase 7 at sh 8); Cb seam step 12.17 vs 5.7 interior. §78.10 later removed the replication (phases 13/14 0.223 → 0.020) but pitch rose. Bands: vertical highpass LH1 (b7), HH1 (b9) at level 1, b4/b6 at level 2; odd rows = vertical highpass rows, the first zeroed at 0.5 bpp. Per-row error 720p sh8 @0.5 f2: Y 14.7 15.9 14.3 16.9 13.5 17.4 15.8 19.3 (odd +12 %, last row +30 %); cf_gfx sh8 last +23 %; 1080p sh16 last +30 %; Cb 720p 19.1 … 23.7. Rate dependence without the blend (seam step as multiple of interior step): city 1.824 / 1.269 / 1.067 / 1.013 and beach 1.705 / 1.294 / 1.048 / 1.021 at 0.5/1.0/2.0/3.0 bpp. No-edit seam excess: +4.36 codes @0.5, +2.64 @1.0 (cine), +2.95 / +1.88 (gfx444), source ~+0.2. XSL off vs level 3, seam SPATIAL excess: +8.86 vs +2.59 @0.5, +3.68 vs +0.82 @1.0. Cold intra frame 3–14× worse than steady (city @0.5 f0 +12.71 sh8 / +10.32 sh16 vs f5 +7.37 / +5.01). Slice-pitch periodicity (pitch.py, dng @0.5, no fill-cure): v5.2 shipped 0.083/0.093/0.078 vs XS @0.5 0.027/0.034/0.033 and source 0.020/0.009/0.008; cityalley 0.214 vs XS 0.026. DC at the seam is NOT the problem (luma seam DC 0.97 of interior; XS 1.32) — what remains is an 8–11 % texture modulation phase-locked to the slice. 720p 8-row: smudge carrier = inter residual size the 8-row geometry cannot represent (r = +0.78 with source motion); refresh slice starvation.

### 3.2 T3 seam test on today's build (SA9; ledger S5.348)
Setup: out/seam_T3.txt, pT3/t3.log, renders out/pT3/renders/ (104 PNGs + ×4 nearest-neighbour copies 96 PNGs); harness rtree3/notes/t3.sh, t3_rp.py; OMC_R1=1, ICL K=4, gamut-strict 0, 0.5 bpp, 4:2:2 10-bit, 6 frames; T1/T2 stopped with no numbers, the one source edit reverted, rtree3 codec.c 31deb436…. The blend is display-only here (decode with/without it differs only on seam rows 0 and sh−1, ≤ 8 codes, frames 0 and 5). Caveat: the "off" arm still carries the encoder's un-blend pre-distortion of the input seam rows.
Result, mean |decode − source| Y (interior / last row on / last row off / row 0 on / row 0 off): 720p f0 15.91 / 19.56 / 21.00 / 14.88 / 15.16; 720p f5 11.55 / 14.17 / 15.29 / 10.61 / 10.81; 1080p f5 12.68 / 15.20 / 16.21 / 12.02 / 12.24 → the LAST ROW of every slice carries +2.5…+3.7 codes excess error even WITH the blend, +1…+1.4 more without it; row 0 at or below the interior; chroma same pattern, smaller; signed mean at the last row −0.2…−0.25 codes more negative without the blend. Conclusion: the excess lives on the mirrored last row of each slice; the blend only partly masks it; the root is the slice-boundary mirror. Renders: `Subagents/SA8_r1lock/out/pT3/renders/` (and x4/).

### 3.3 Temporal stability (still-area churn) — SA11 Part A documentation review (SA11_plan/temporal_A.txt)
Trigger: S5.334 / W6: among SOURCE-static samples ~92 % of decoded samples change frame to frame (dng720 @0.5 Y 222,705/243,073; cf_gfx @0.5 Y 17,753/19,009). Metric note: prior records measure the "ants tail" = P(|frame-to-frame diff| > 6 codes) over source-flat-AND-static 16×16 blocks (tests/ants.py) or "boil" = mean |diff|; W6 counts ANY change (≥ 1 code) — not comparable without a histogram.
- A1 (REPORT.md 18.5): a blind viewer identified temporal boil in source-still areas as the tell; boil: source 1.526, XS@2 1.417, OMC 1.651 codes/frame. Falsified there: fill innocent (1.643); amplitude fixes no effect; "Refresh period irrelevant (R16/R1 = 1.64)"; dead zone HELPS (off: 2.071). Mechanism: lattice reconstruction amplifies near-threshold survivors (0.6-step value reconstructs at a full step), re-rolled per frame.
  [A1 detail: boil OMC 8 % above source, 17 % above XS.]
- A2: the bar is XS's SUB-source calm (XS tail 7.7 % vs source 15.3 %).

- A4 (TEMPORAL_T5 12.23): "ants came back" in T5; still720_444_8 (frame 0 ×8) @1.0: shipped Y 7.54 / Cb 6.13 / Cr 5.53 %; all-intra R=1: 0.50/0.56/0.54; refresh 64 halves the tail ("roughly half the absolute level is the rolling intra refresh"); DEADZONE OFF triples it (8.68 → 26.00 Y). All-intra rejected as a lever: costs NEG 3.3 @0.5 on cineA21. Mechanism: detail coefficient near the zero/one boundary carried across by sub-code wobble. FIX shipped (OMC_CALM default on): calm positions (source-LL gradient < 24<<(d−8), |coef| < 3<<(d−7)) with q = 1 and |coef| < step coded as zero; runs on intra and inter slices; gates G-T5-CALM1a/b/c, CALM2a/b. All-intra rejected as a lever because "refresh is a resilience parameter, A5".
- A5: grain fill OFF by default (14/16 cells better on ants AND NEG); static tile normative whenever --fill runs; still1080 @0.5 shipped tail Y 0.08 % (source 0).
- A6 (LEDGER_v5_3_5 B2.0–B2.3): frozen masters: 99.5 % of non-refresh slices change (Q, prof, n_steps) EVERY FRAME on a bit-static source (558/561 pairs) — plan search re-runs each frame, near-tie winner wanders; REPRESENTATION DEGENERACY. Plan hysteresis level 1 was OFF in every normal encode (wiring gap). Level 1 as default FALSIFIED: NEG −0.55 mean, cf_gfx@0.5 −7.13. Shapes 2–4 falsified (near-tie hold, small-slack cap, step ratchet); "representation degeneracy (adjacent triples encode near-identical shift sets)". ADOPTED shape 5 = STATIC-SLICE HOLD (hold previous plan iff profile matches and est(old) ≤ budget × 35 %; OMC_PLAN_HYST=3). Non-refreshed slices hold a ~15 % churn floor that never converges; refresh-wave slices flicker at ~50 %; derived MVs all zero on frozen content; XSL rows NOT the carrier.
- A7: frozen dng tail 25.08 → 14.94 (0.5), 23.60 → 16.22 (1.0), 18.49 → 17.26 (2.0) Y after the hold. With plan stable and fill off, refresh-wave slices ~41 % pair-tail — "the intra and inter stationary points differ; each refresh yanks the picture between the two fixtures"; Q5's intra skip structurally unreachable on refresh slices. Pulse instrument, frozen dng@1.0 120f: fixed 75/91/91 %, crawl 3/0/0 %, pulsing 20/8/7 % period 8 = the REFRESH QUALITY RAMP (v5.4 frozen dng@1.0, 120 frames: "inter lineage accumulates quality over R−1 frames, the intra refresh resets it"); Q5's intra skip q == quant(pcoef) is unreachable on refresh slices ("A5 resync forbids pcoef there"); disposition = Task C "ramp invisibility" (refresh boost §48, need-keyed refresh scheduling §39.5). Grid constancy at the refresh FALSIFIED (engaged on 79 % of refresh events, moved nothing; refresh-held slices still change 62 % of pixels by > 6 codes), reverted.
- A8: chroma fill repaint at refresh; refresh boost (OMC_RBOOST) was DEAD CODE since it shipped (guard on cfg.refresh_r == 0); fixed, default 50; Cb pulsing 1596 → 169. Crawl battery: frozen_dng @0.5 Y/Cb/Cr 12.85/18.92/18.19 %, @1.0 12.04/11.37/11.91, @2.0 10.41/9.44/10.47; frozen_readysetgo @0.5 6.65/6.76/9.71; cityalley @0.5 3.92/2.77/3.02.
- A9: OMC_PLAN_HYST=3, OMC_PLAN_STATIC=35; OMC_FILLGHYS FALSIFIED; "no refresh flicker at any setting (pulses ≤ 0.17 dB)".
- A10: XSL level 6 "recomputed every frame" +2.06 ants-tail points vs level 3.
- B (bitrate side): B1 (S5.20) static content still gains +0.9..+3 NEG at 1.0, +0.5..+1.4 at 2.0 (graphics +3 at 1.0); "frozen" = upper bound; proposed per-region still/moving split with cheapest no-change coding — not built. B2 (S5.144) predicting from previous SOURCE instead of committed frame saves 12–19 % inter bits @0.5 on the near-static dng control (+13.89 %); 60–72 % of the saving in LH1+HL1 = REFERENCE NOISE; reference noise switches 7–30 % of bands inter↔intra ("mode flips"). B3 (S5.152) source-searched vectors do not help; stale-vector theory falsified. B4 V23 closed. B5 per-block mode FALSIFIED (0.1–1.8 % of residual bits). B6 sub-slice scheduled refresh = paper idea, flicker risk.
- C (HVBC): C1 HOLD-STILL / churn-skip (adopted 2026-07-14): predicted tiles whose SOURCE did not change (mean |src − prev_src| < 1.5) and whose prediction meets the quality floor code an EXACT zero residual; @1.5: cow bits −13.5 % / flicker −19 %, fence −9.6/−13 %, pans never qualify; encoder-only; luma only. C2 hold-still RE-CONFIRMED (no-churn arm +13.2 % bits AND visibly noisier); MV hysteresis falsified. C3 any decoder-side shaping must be a continuous deterministic function of the decoded picture; "no threshold can flicker frame-to-frame; hold-still guarantees static areas identical". C4 encoder-only step changes must leave reconstruction points untouched.
- D (rulings constraining any fix): D1 refresh R = 8 is a resilience parameter, "not available as an ants knob"; D2 no opt-in trade-offs; D3 zero visible steps (decoder-side thresholds = flicker risk); D4 plan hysteresis level 2 forbidden (froze signalled state, broke locks, −1.00 NEG), any hold must remain a generation fixed point; D5 NEG is not a gate — ants tail / pulse instruments + eyes are.
- Mechanisms in today's codec that move a static area (M1–M13): M1 rolling intra refresh (slice s intra on frame f when fidx8 % R == s % R, R = 8; refresh boost +50 % budget); M2 fresh rate control every frame (plan wander; static-slice hold level 3; slices whose residual cost > 35 % of budget replan every frame); M3 inter residual re-coded every frame (on a static source the residual = the codec's own quantisation error, re-quantised and re-sent every frame; no hold-still rule in OMC; Q5 skip only drops coefficients with q already 0 or == quant(pcoef)); M4 per-band mode decision recomputed each frame (reference noise flips 7–30 % of bands); M5 dead zone / calm kill; M6 causal bit banking (earlier slice spend moves later budgets → cascade); M7 OMC_ALLOC per-slice cap from the previous frame's energy record; M8 targeted partial / PBAND / PLAN-SD; M9 XSL seam blend (slice k uses slice k−1's committed rows); M10 derived motion (zero on frozen content); M11 gamut repair (default ON) and DCFB; M12 grain fill (off by default); M13 transform support spill-over (level-5 LL supports hundreds of samples).
- Still open (per the review): B2.5 frozen source should converge to zero — ~15 % churn floor; refresh ramp pulse period 8 (Task C, not closed); S5.20 still-region coding never built; HVBC hold-still never ported (the only measured mechanism that cut both bits −9..−13.5 % and flicker −13..−19 % on static content); no record measured all changes (≥ 1 code) on a frozen input at current defaults → Part B.
- Further mechanism and record detail (temporal_A.txt): M3 Q5 skip (codec.c 1720–1730, on by default OMC_Q5FLAG=16) only drops coefficients whose q is already 0 (inter) or = quant(pcoef) (intra) — reconstruction-preserving, rate only, never holds; M4 BMODE = bits_inter < bits_intra (codec.c 10586) recomputed each frame from noisy costs, a band flipping intra↔inter "moves between the two stationary points"; M8 targeted partial / PBAND / PLAN-SD chunk selections derived from the quantised LL (follow LL changes), spc depth clamp toward the previous slice (12646); M10 derived motion (derive_mv 4970) zero on frozen content (A6: MV loop exonerated), on real clips moving neighbours' vectors reach static samples; M11 DCFB default 0 (codec.c 3441); M12 grain fill off by default, when on the tile is static but its amplitude follows the plan shift; M13 (S5.317 f5) level-5 LL supports hundreds of samples → static samples near motion change even with perfect coding. B2 = SA2 I2; B4 (WORK_QUEUE V23) "temporal first (creates bits)", V23 closed; A4 mechanism "each crossing = a full-step move"; A6 frozen masters `arms/frozen_*_1920x1080_422_10.yuv` (source tail 0). Open item also: "No record measures ALL changes (≥ 1 code) or bits split by refresh vs inter slice on a frozen input at the current defaults" (→ Part B).

Not converged: remaining change = rolling refresh (period 8) and its seam rows.
2. Stage dump, inter slice 5: f1→f2, f2→f3, f6→f7 (no refresh neighbour): every stage 0 changed incl. DISPLAYED → exact hold. Every inter frame: pre-clip == ref and post-clip == ref (with a zero residual the inverse reproduces the reference exactly; the XSL term cancels; clip does nothing; gamut repair ON/OFF identical). f3→f4 (slice 4 above refreshed): pcoef changes (gfx 242, dng 352, spot 1,006 coefficients — the XSL term) but pre/post-clip/committed 0; DISPLAYED changes on ROW 0 only (gfx 350, dng 880, spot 1,188) = refresh-barrier display blend. f4→f5 (slice 5 intra): pre-clip 2,195 / 6,629 / 21,990 samples differ from the reference = 61 / 74 / 72 % of the slice — the intra re-encode does not reproduce the inter picture. f5→f6 (slice 6 below refreshed): DISPLAYED last row only (gfx 248, dng 428, spot 733). Displayed vs committed: constant offset 2,048 (bias) + constant set of differing samples per slice (gfx 687, dng 1,879, spot 2,331). pcoef = forward transform of the integer motion-compensated previous FINAL picture, not quantised, no bias.
3. Bits with forced zero residual: cf_gfx inter 28 slices 4,284 bits every frame (153 bits/slice) vs 8,740–34,869 normal; refresh 5,592–10,731; frame 9,876–15,015 (normal 18,159–40,461). dng720 inter 79 slices 12,087 (153/slice) vs 96,778–344,357; refresh 57,452–87,190; frame 69,386–99,277 (normal 175,453–401,796). spot @1.0 inter 60 slices 9,180 vs 476,766–1,634,698; refresh 279,651–414,797; frame 288,678–423,824 (normal 811,357–1,914,349). Gamut OFF same inter bits; totals within 0.3 %. Quality Y dB vs source f7: H0 29.31 / 34.23 / 44.14 vs normal 31.48 / 36.00 / 45.90 (gfx/dng/spot) — zero residual also removes the inter refinement of frames 1–3.
4. Stages that must change for an exact hold: (i) the residual decision on inter slices (any non-zero index re-codes quantisation noise every frame); (ii) the rolling intra refresh (re-encode differs from the held picture on 61–74 % of the refreshed slice); (iii) the refresh-barrier display blend (0.9–3.7 % of inter-row samples, display only). NOT involved: prediction, pcoef, inverse transform, XSL cancellation, clip, gamut repair.

### 3.7 Plan class instrument U (SA11_plan/plan_U.txt, u13_raw.txt, u4_sum.txt, u4_best.txt) — today's generation lock
Vocabulary of today's generation lock (as used in plan_U.txt, u13_raw.txt and ledger S5.326–S5.342): GENERATION LOCK = the next encoder's procedure that re-derives gen 1's plan from the decoded picture; CANDIDATE WALK = candidates (plan = prof, Q, ns [refinement steps], k [partial chunk]; "triple" = prof,Q,ns) ranked by a flag-free bit ESTIMATE, cheapest first; LOCK_VERIFY = the per-candidate lattice check (PREREJECT = its early coefficient test; "fails at prereject p2 b5 i55" = plane 2, band 5, coefficient 55); SITES = samples gen 1 clipped (out of range before the clip); AFFECTED ("aff", naff) = coefficients whose value the clip perturbed (their transform support covers a site); δ* = the excursions lost at the sites, from gen 1's delta dump; pred(δ*) = the predicate (count of mismatching samples when the candidate is decoded with δ* restored; 0 = exact); bits* = the candidate's bits with δ*; DIRECT SOLVE = the fixed-path δ estimate (table weights + Gauss-Seidel, S5.326); p2 = predicate after the census direct solve (3 passes); CENSUS = SA8's run-3 gen-1/gen-2 streams and dumps (SA8_r1lock/out/pP/run3); class B = the SOLVE class (gen 1's plan ranks first but the direct solve does not close); PLAN class = gen 1's plan not ranked first; "B5.5 overspend" = the source's label for a lock emitting an exact plan with more bits than gen 1 (spot f0 s44, +1,017); R1 = the recovery lock switch OMC_R1; rtree3 / rtree4 = the patched sandbox trees (rtree4 = rsync of rtree3 for SA11).
Tree rtree4 = rsync of rtree3 at codec.c md5 d2afb79a… (pre-U source kept as SA11_plan/codec_pre_U.c); after U codec.c 822d5925…, omc_enc 51c3175c…, omc_dec c50fb09f…; diff sa11_U.diff (418 lines). Switches default off: OMC_R1_PLANU, OMC_U_FORCE (gen-1 plan override), OMC_U_STOPAFTER. Off-path identity 6/6 after each build; neutrality instrumented g2 == uninstrumented g2 on 4/4 cells and == census g2 streams 4/4; R1U output byte-identical after the U4 build. Env on every g2: OMC_R1=1 OMC_R1_STAT=1 OMC_R1_REUSE=1, --gamut-strict 0. Inputs: census gen-1 streams, delta dumps, EPLAN dumps (SA8_r1lock/out/pP/run3); U4 gen-1 re-runs reproduce the census gen-1 streams byte for byte (6/6 prefixes); seed never pinned (drivers rtree4/notes/u.sh, u4.sh assert it); off-path identity checked after both builds (SA11_plan/ident, ident2). Discriminator: ranking key recomputed equals the walk's stored key on 12,780/12,780 candidates (351 slices); cost tables recomputed equal the snapshot on 351/351 (tabmis 0). Slice sets: PLAN = 8 brief slices (gfx_05 f0 s15, s26; gfx8_05 f0 s15, s26; f1 s1, f4 s4; spot_10 f0 s3; d7x8_05 f4 s42); C_site = 200 random (seed 11) site slices with gen 1's plan at rank 0 (80/15/105 from gfx8_05/spot_10/d7x8_05); C_nosite = 50; M = all census slices with gen 1's plan at rank > 0 (59 site, 34 no-site).
- U1 (full tables u13_raw.txt; per-candidate lines run1/<cell>.g2.err "R1U CAND", R1VFAIL before a failing candidate): every plan-class slice: gen 1's plan partial k = 0, no targeted/PBAND selection, mode word XOR 0 on every candidate except d7x8 f4 s42. gfx s15 (3 sites, 1,208 aff; nlcand 151; gen 1 0,10,13,0 at rank 26): ranks 0–9, 20–25 fail lock_verify at the same first failing coefficient (prereject p2 (Cr) b5 i55 val −256 shift 11 recon 0 diff 256, NOT affected); ranks 10–19 (0,11,34..42 and 1,11,30) verify, pred(delta*) 2,198, bits* 1,295, one step coarser on luma bands 0.1, 0.2 (28/28 and 56/56 coefficients affected) plus empty chroma bands; rank 26 = gen 1 pred 0 bits* 1,318; ranks 27–35 equivalent. gfx s26 (6 sites, 912 aff; nlcand 88; gen 1 at rank 10): ranks 0–9 verify, pred 866, bits* 1,438; the one live difference band 0.3 luma shift 7 vs 6 (58/112 affected); rank 10 = gen 1 pred 0, bits* 1,469. gfx8 f1 s1 (20 sites, 1,540 aff; nlcand 270; gen 1 at rank 37; intra): ranks 0–29 verify, pred(delta*) 1,110 (r0–1), 1,106, 1,023, 375, 297, 907, 700 (coarser plans along the refine path 0,10,1..13 and profiles 1–3); rank 0 (0,10,1,0) one step coarser on luma bands 0.4–0.9 (116/224, 116/224, 116/224, 344/896, 344/896, 344/896 affected); first exact rank 30 (0,10,14,0) bits* 1,418 = gen 1. gfx8 f4 s4 (9 sites, 1,844 aff; nlcand 82; gen 1 at rank 8): ranks 0–4 (0,10,5..9) pred 595, bits* 1,344 (band 0.6 (142 aff) and 0.7 (404 aff) one step coarser); ranks 5–7 pred 159, bits* 1,413; rank 8 = gen 1 pred 0, bits* 1,516. spot_10 f0 s3: NOT a plan-class slice (gen 1's plan 0,5,10,0 is rank 0, derived plan identical, nlcand 22, verifies, pred 0, bits* 30,323); what fails is the solve (census direct solve under rank 0 leaves pred 344 (p2), R ≥ 64 183 vs 8 at delta*; class B). d7x8 f4 s42 (no sites; nlcand 335; gen 1 0,7,3,0, mode word 267878399, at rank 1): rank 0 = 0,7,2,0 fails lock_verify at p0 b0 (LL) i8 val −263 shift 2 diff −1; rank 1 = gen 1 verifies, derived mode word XOR 131072 (bit 17 = p1 b7 inter vs intra), bits* 5,016 vs 5,015 (+1).
- u13_raw per-slice header facts (sites / affected / candidates / gen-1 plan / rank / emitted at g2 / bits / budget_hard): d7x8_05 f4 s42 0 / 0 / 335 / 0,7,3,0 / 1 / 0,7,3,0 / 5,015 / 9,856; gfx8_05 (= gfx_05) f0 s15 3 / 1,208 / 151 / 0,10,13,0 / 26 / 0,10,13,0 / 1,318 / 1,456; f0 s26 6 / 912 / 88 / 0,10,22,0 / 10 / 0,10,22,0 / 1,469 / 1,512; gfx8_05 f1 s1 20 / 1,540 / 270 / 0,10,21,0 / 37 / **0,10,14,0** (gen 2 emitted an EQUIVALENT plan, not gen 1's triple) / 1,418 / 1,736; f4 s4 9 / 1,844 / 82 / 0,10,13,0 / 8 / 0,10,13,0 / 1,516 / 1,616; spot_10 f0 s3 **66 / 3,620** / 22 / 0,5,10,0 / 0 / 0,5,10,0 / 30,323 / 30,512. plan_U slice set M "includes the plan slices' neighbours; most still lock because an equivalent plan ranks first".
- U2: the ranking key = flag-free bit ESTIMATE, cheapest first; the cost tables include affected (clip-perturbed) coefficients, the lattice admission SKIPS them, so a band whose non-affected coefficients happen to lie on a coarser lattice admits the coarser shift; nothing in the key tests exactness; the walk then takes the cheapest VERIFIED and exact candidate by actual bits. Per-band key difference rank 0 − gen 1 (KEYB lines): s15 −97 (−88 in affected bands 0.1: 24 vs 60 and 0.2: 24 vs 76, both 100 % affected; −9 in band 2.5; keyE −9); s26 −34 (band 0.3: 114 vs 148, 58/112 affected; keyE −7: 15 vs 22); f1 s1 −615 all in affected bands 0.4–0.8 (48/142, 53/137, 14/136, 14/151, 14/192; keyE 0 on every band); f4 s4 −171 (0.6: 22 vs 91, 0.7: 14 vs 116; keyE 0); d7x8 s42 −38 in band 0.5 (585 vs 623), no sites (not affected-driven; rank 0 mode-word XOR 131104 = bits 17 and 5, p1 b7 and p0 b5). Key source lines (rtree4 codec.c after U): cost tables from band_scan over ALL coefficients incl. affected (10577); lattice admission skips affected (10661/10664); key adds bc[m][p][b].bits[s] (10988; m = consistent mode, cheaper if both, 10982; step band fan_base 10965 + fan_d·k/nch 11006); key = tk (11034); walk = argmin key, first on ties (11181); then cheapest VERIFIED and exact by actual bits (11388); gen 1's rate control Q loop 12273, refine loop 12289 (12273–12303). → on the 4 gfx site slices the preference for rank 0 is the bits term of bands containing affected coefficients (86–100 % of the gap).
- U3 definitions (plan_U.txt): set S per slice = verifying candidates among ranks 0..max(39, gen-1 rank) (|S| per plan slice: d7x8 s42 38; gfx s15 21; s26 40; f1 s1 40; f4 s4 40; spot s3 22). Per feature, gen 1's plan is "first" (strictly best), "tie_equiv" (best, tied only with plans exact at delta* with equal bits* = equivalent), "tie_nonequiv" (tied with a non-equivalent plan) or "demoted" (some candidate strictly better). Features: (a) R2 = sum |lattice residue| over affected coefficients with step ≥ 64 after the census direct solve (3 passes); (b) p2 = the predicate after that solve; (c) bits2 = trial bits with the solved delta (c_bitsstar = bits* with delta* gives identical classes in every class); (d) fineness = sum of band shifts (finest = ascending, coarsest = descending; also band-size weighted: d_finestN = d_finest in every class, d_coarsestN = "coarsest(wtd)"); (e) keyE = key with affected coefficients set to 0; x_Rstar and two combined rules.
- U3 (features tested to rank gen 1's plan first on every plan-class slice without demoting it on controls; first/tie-equivalent/tie-nonequivalent/demoted over PLAN(8), C_site(200), C_nosite(50), M_site(59)): (a) R2 0/3/4/1, 1/0/124/75, 0/0/50/0, 0/0/37/22; (b) p2 0/3/5/0, 0/0/197/3, 0/0/50/0, 0/0/59/0; (c) bits2 2/0/0/6, 175/24/0/1, 45/5/0/0, 9/47/0/3; (d) finest 1/0/0/7, 0/0/0/200, 0/0/0/50, 0/0/0/59; coarsest 2/0/0/6, 170/3/12/15, 49/0/1/0, 9/0/0/50; coarsest(wtd) 2/0/0/6, 197/0/0/3, 50/0/0/0, 9/0/0/50; (e) keyE 2/0/4/2, 159/19/22/0, 45/5/0/0, 9/39/10/1; key (today) 2/0/0/6, 176/22/2/0, 45/5/0/0, 9/47/0/3; combined exact-after-solve then bits2 5/3/0/0, 174/24/0/2, 45/5/0/0, 9/50/0/0; combined R2 then bits2 4/3/0/1, 109/15/0/76, 45/5/0/0, 3/34/0/22; x_Rstar 0/3/4/1, 0/0/167/33, 0/0/50/0, 0/0/48/11. Per-feature answers (plan_U): (a) R2 ties non-equivalent plans on 4/8 and demotes spot s3 ("R2 at gen 1 183 vs 0 on 1,5,9 and 0,5,19/21"; spot s3 a_R2 = 13 better / 8 ties), demotes 75/200 controls; (b) p2 = 0 on many non-equivalent plans: ties 5/8, demotes 3/200 controls (spot slices where the direct solve fails under gen 1's plan); (c) demotes 6/8 plan slices (cheaper non-exact rank 0) and 1/200 controls; (d) NO either direction; (e) demotes 2/8 (s26 −7, d7x8 s42), ties non-equivalent 4/8, 0/200 controls demoted, 22 non-equivalent ties. Each was forced at gen 1 (OMC_U_FORCE), decoded, and gen 2 run to the slice with the instrument (u4_sum.txt, runs u4/, log u4.log). ### 3.8 The pre-turn legality block and the hold (ledger S5.330–S5.342, 2026-09-27; SA9 on rtree3, SA11 on rtree4; coordinator = Fable)
Context: S5.327 (owner) had set exact miss counts over the full test set as the only reporting form and a completeness-by-construction self-check (≤ 1 predetermined re-choice per slice); S5.328 (SA9 Step T) falsified index capping as a free re-choice; S5.329 proposed the "in-cell legaliser + recovery" candidate and ordered Step W.
- **S5.330 block replacement (owner ruling quoted in §1.7).** Parts to REMOVE: the output clip as the legaliser; the plan-search generation lock (candidate walk, lock_verify/trial walk, ~50 entropy-pass equivalents per slice); R1's search; the repair engine (gamut_strict); MINBITS/RESJOINT/ONESEARCH/CAP and every other patch switch. Parts of the NEW block, each measured alone: (1) in-cell legaliser at both ends (Step W, S5.329); (2) a directly computed plan — the next encoder derives gen 1's plan from the picture in one pass (Step U; the lattice read is rank-0-correct on 91–100 % already, S5.326); (3) the fixed-path recovery (rank-0 plan, table weights, Gauss-Seidel, ≤ 2 corrections; S5.326) for the small residue only; (4) SDI-correct rails per format (S5.326 S3). To be built as a CLEAN replacement in a NEW sandbox tree copied from the shipped codec (not layered on rtree2/3), design document DESIGN_CORE.md written by the coordinator once W and U report. (Superseded by S5.346 "don't patch" and DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH, §4.2.)
- **S5.331 doc check for W (done after the brief — process error).** HVBC CONSOLIDATED_FINDINGS_TILES_2026-07-17 §10: decoder-side reconstruction inside the quantisation intervals was used as a smoothing/deblocking post-filter over the whole image — v1 too soft, v2 facet creases, v4/v5 hard edges, v6 a clamp bug (cast +5.5), v7 multigrid "a blurry canvas" (diffusion falsified as a category), v8 crosshatch/lines; HVBC standing rules: no global smoothness filters, no synthesized texture, "gradients must be a CONTINUOUS deterministic function of the decoded picture (no threshold can flicker frame-to-frame; hold-still guarantees static areas identical)". Old OMC rule "any legal fix must leave reconstruction points untouched" (Issues_to_fix/20260811 second-agent ledger §6.1) was about ENCODER-only step changes causing decoder mismatch; W changes the reconstruction at both ends identically, so it does not apply. Verdict: W (only out-of-range samples and their footprint, toward the legal range and the source) is not the falsified category but inherits two risks: (a) the out-of-range trigger is a threshold → temporal flicker; (b) local softening/creasing near rail edges. Two added gates: 8-frame temporal hold-still (static areas byte-identical) and full-frame colour level + |diff| renders (unmarked + _grid) vs plain clip on gfx, dng720, spot, cut24. No adoption without both.
- **S5.332 Step U (§3.7) — coordinator reading:** "the PLAN class has the SAME root as the solve class — the clip destroys the coefficient evidence in bands where all coefficients are affected, so the lattice read admits coarser shifts there. Both classes therefore vanish if the decoded picture stays inside its transmitted cells (no clip-destroyed evidence) — Step W's in-cell legaliser is the decisive test for BOTH; the self-check re-choice route (S5.327) is closed (no free re-choice, U4 + S5.328)."
- **S5.333 Step W — in-cell legaliser (SA9; out/icl_W.txt, pW/run1/w_sum.txt, pW/w5.log, pW/w67.log; renders pW/w67/renders/ 156 PNGs, not looked at by eye then).**
  - W1 method: OMC_ICL / OMC_ICL_K in rtree3 reconstruct_slice at BOTH ends, after the inverse and before any clip; per round: clip → WHOLE-slice forward → clamp each coefficient into its transmitted cell → inverse; then the existing clip. Cell model: round-to-nearest, 9/16 dead zone on bands ≥ 4, NO texture bias, pcoef on inter bands; cells filled at encoder emission, decoder and in the R1 predicate. OMC_ICL unset 6/6 identical; rt = 0 on all 64 gen-1 runs.
  - W2 (K = 4 vs plain clip, R1 set): real cells stream byte-identical, quality +0.000…+0.001 dB (worst slice-plane −0.001, cf_gfx s20); sites / max |δ|: cf_gfx 146 → 12 / 259 → 8; dng720 418 → 81 / 113 → 8; spot @1.0 528 → 44 / 43 → 4; volley 1,001 → 72 / 111 → 8. Synthetic: cut24 @0.5 4,874 → 4,619; @2.0 9,577 → 2,563; extFULL 25,956 → 3,242; extLIM 16,465 → 1,479; stream changes on cut24 and extLIM (legalised rows feed the next slice's XSL term); extLIM worst slice-plane −14.40 dB (Cb) at every K; cut24 +0.02…+0.52 dB. With R1 UNSET the stream changes on every cell (worst −0.98…−14.4 dB; recorded, not the configuration of interest).
  - W3 gen-2 fixed path with ICL: coverage FALLS on every real cell (cf_gfx 93.8 → 84.4 %, dng720 100 → 96.7 %, spot 98.5 → 94.1–95.6 %, volley 100 → 97.1 % at K = 1; volley K = 2/4 gen 2 timed out at 900 s); cut24 @0.5: 7 of 8 slices have NO lock candidate at gen 2 ("the legalised picture is off the coefficient lattice"); cut24 @2.0 and extrema 0 %.
  - W4 work (whole-slice forward): real cells 1.00 / 1.07–1.41 slice-passes mean at K = 1 / 4, worst 4; cut24 2 / 8; extrema ≈ 1 / 2–2.4, worst 5; no local figure. W5: six small arms generation-exact at K = 1 and 4 (moved 0, g2 = g3, oob 0, guard 0, all site slices r1locked).
  - W6 hold-still (8 frames @0.5, K = 4): extra changing static samples ICL − clip: dng720 Y −3, Cb 0, Cr 0; cf_gfx Y −3, Cb +11 (up to +8 at f4→5, ~0.1 % of static Cb), Cr −4 (a small chroma flicker finding); and the plain codec already changes 93–94 % of source-static samples frame to frame — the origin of the temporal-stability finding (§3.3, S5.334).
  - Coordinator diagnosis then: one defect — the cell model does not match the real quantiser (no texture bias; partial chunks/targeted partial not modelled; XSL and seam-blend state) — REFUTED by W′. Theory check stated: if every coefficient stays in its true cell and the final clip is 0, gen 2 needs no recovery (T(P1) = c′ ∈ cells ⇒ Q = q₁).
- **S5.334–S5.335 temporal stability** (owner: "very bad — affects visuals and bitrate; a huge setback") → SA11 Part A/B (§3.3, §3.4). Coordinator fixed-point view (S5.335): a still region converges only if (i) inter slices reproduce the previous displayed picture EXACTLY (zero residual ⇒ recon = reference, by the bijection) and (ii) the refresh of an unchanged region with an unchanged plan reproduces the previous refresh's intra picture (then the inter lineage in between is exactly held, so the refresh changes nothing). H0 = the discriminator (§3.5); then DESIGN_HOLD.md → adversarial design review → build (sandbox). Priority: alongside legality (owner).
- **S5.336 DESIGN_HOLD.md (`Subagents/HOLD/`, coordinator)**, written after H0: H1 exact source-equality static detection; H2 zero-residual hold; H3 a static refresh reuses its previous refresh plan (monotone finer allowed) so the intra picture repeats; H4 quality from the budget freed by held slices funding refresh slices, gate ≥ today's plateau; H5 the blend follows; H6 generations consistent; stage 2 chunk-level holds.
- **S5.337 adversarial review of DESIGN_HOLD (read-only reviewer; verdict NOT to be built as written; stage 1 only after test D1 + changes; stage 2 not workable).**
  - BLOCKER-until-measured H4: held static slices freeze at their last intra refresh; the freed budget is capped — wire cap 2× per slice in the bitstream spec (codec.c 9923–9927; raising it costs latency, OMC_ODCAP rejected), allocator 1.5× + boost 0.5× (10038–10063), refresh slices already at ~1.35–1.57×, slice 0 at 1× (the bank flows downward only, 9918) → H0's −1.76…−2.17 dB (frozen intra vs today's settled quality) likely cannot be recovered; still content is where the temporal gain lives (S5.20) → the hold would forfeit it.
  - MAJOR: exact source equality never fires on camera footage (3.5–4.1 % static samples, isolated in sensor noise; no full-width slice qualifies); HVBC's gains came from a threshold (mean change < 1.5, quality floor, luma only, codec.py 1383–1400); scope = slates, bars, black, letterbox, freeze-frames, graphics; the W6 alarm on real content is not addressed.
  - MAJOR: no quality check of what is frozen (a graphic animating in freezes its last blurred frame until the refresh) → reviewer's MONOTONE-REFINEMENT rule: while static and before its first refresh, accept a non-zero residual only if it STRICTLY reduces the slice's absolute error vs the source (integer, monotone → terminates in an exact hold). MAJOR: pop when motion starts in a held slice.
  - BLOCKER stage 2: chunk-level holds = held/refined steps on the chunk grid (HVBC 16×16 failure; wavelet supports span hundreds of samples) → stage 2 dropped. MAJOR: previous-frame store at 8K ≈ 80–143 Gbit/s DDR → replace by a 64-bit per-slice-per-plane checksum on input (≈ 13 KB on chip at 8K). MAJOR: H3 = B2c "grid constancy at the refresh" (codec.c 12352–12373), falsified in B2.5 because inter refinement moved the picture; its static-cycle flag (13578) would be cleared by H2 slices; budget depends on motion above → static refresh quality steps with unrelated motion → reserve the static refresh budget, drop or once-only "strictly finer".
  - BLOCKER: cut to a still / stream start: every slice holds cut-frame intra quality until its own refresh → venetian-blind sharpening over 8 frames, violating the rev. 3 ramp rules → monotone-refinement rule until the first static refresh. MAJOR: the lock must win over H3 at gen ≥ 2; H0 evidence was taken with OMC_R1 unset — re-prove "display-only blend" and "seam cancels" with R1 on and ICL on.
  - Latency unchanged; IP low (conditional replenishment, Mounts 1969 / H.261 1990, expired; no legal question for stage 1; keep docs free of other codecs' skip-mode references). Gates to add: 24–120 frames, pulse instrument, per-slice-row quality incl. slice 0, flatness and level maps with slice grid on both codecs, ramp cases, motion start/stop, coupling, R1 + ICL, generation chains at different refresh phase and rate, A5 loss, 4:4:4 12-bit, full range, HDR PQ, 8K (seam blend off > 8192 wide, codec.c 2862), slice heights 8/16/32, XS@2R.
  - Coordinator reading: the structural conflict is resilience refresh vs refined quality vs no-change; D1 decides whether a 2×-funded refresh can reach settled quality (if not, the exact hold is dead in this form); the real-content alarm needs the monotone-refinement rule on ALL inter slices, measured, not assumed. Next: D1 + M1 on SA11 (§3.6).
  - Addendum (reviewer, on the owner's legality point): the hold's main beneficiaries (static graphics, bars, slates, super-white text, full-range graphics) are rail-heavy content.
