## §4 Expert answers (memo 010) and the coordinator's from-scratch design — FALSIFIED

### 4.1 Expert answers to memo 010 (ledger S5.343; `Communication/202600927/`: start_here.md = "Memo 011" expert A; vibe.md = expert B; gemini.md = expert C; perplexity.pdf = expert D)
**What memo 010 asked (ledger S5.339, `MEMO_TO_CODEC_EXPERT_from_Fable_010.md`, Project root; the owner sent it to SEVERAL experts, answers delivered together, digested before any DESIGN_CORE — S5.339 note).** Self-contained: (§1) requirements all at once — legal range full and limited (10-bit 4…1019, 8-bit 1…254, 12-bit 16…4079), 8/10/12-bit now, 16-bit later, 4:2:2/4:4:4 required, 4:2:0/4:0:0 supported, SDR BT.709 and HDR BT.2020 PQ/HLG, 720p–8K; generation byte-exactness for unlimited generations through a CBR hop and a baseband hop, only the image crossing; no visible artifact (only smooth softening of already-soft regions); no quality and no bit cost for legality or exactness ("today we are at about 1.33× of that goal, not 2×"); exact CBR incl. first frames and cuts; latency (never noticeably longer than JPEG XS excluding conversion, < 1 ms including, constant, no unit overrunning its slot); ZU7EV-class with large margin, shifts/adds/lookups only, no per-pixel multipliers, no adaptive arithmetic coding, no parallel hardware to hide work; IP stated once; every feature kept (one previous decoded frame, rolling refresh, conversion, cuts, formats). (§2) the codec as it works today (see §3.0). (§3) the measured problem: overshoot sparse on natural content (100–1,100 samples per 720p–1080p frame, isolated, mostly two finest levels) and dense on rail content (thousands to tens of thousands, coupled); the clip destroys information; the plan does not repeat by itself → the lock (candidates ranked by estimated bits, lattice check, cheapest exact; ~50 entropy-pass equivalents per slice vs a design budget of about one pass + ≤ 2 packs); clipped samples break the lock. (§4) every attempt, measured: (1) repair engine — legal and lock-exact but artifacts, unbounded passes; (2) index capping — halves violations, a fix breaks its neighbour through the update step, 0.8–21 dB worst slice, remaining overshoots larger; (3) δ recovery at the next encoder — works sparse, up to ~190,000 slice-inverse equivalents or never finishes on dense rails, several exact δ, order-dependent; (4) fixed-work direct solve (top-ranked plan, table weights, Gauss-Seidel, one check) — 91–100 % natural in ~2–15 slice-pass equivalents, 0 % dense rails, misses 1–9 % on graphics where the top plan is wrong; (5) gen-1 plan re-choice — no equal-or-cheaper alternative quality-neutral (−0.08 to −1 dB luma); (6) capping + recovery worse than recovery alone; (7) embedded bit-plane — +1–17 % luma bits with static contexts, its in-cell projection stalled 1–3 codes short on rail plates, the independent designer found no route meeting all requirements; (8) decoder-side in-cell legaliser W/W′ — K = 4 every coefficient in its true cell (checked with the encoder's own quantiser), quality = plain clip, no bits change, final-clip samples 0 on graphics/720p frames, 7–81 on others, thousands on dense synthetic rails, 0.3–2.7 % of coefficients off their lattice points so the lattice lock rejects them. (§5) NON-leading request: the right way to build input → numbers → legal picture so every decision is reproduced from the image alone; four questions: (1) which decisions must be canonical and how to structure rate control and quantisation for it without bit/quality cost; (2) legality without destroying what the next encoder needs, incl. content exactly at black/white with dense edges; (3) worst-case work per slice in passes and whether it fits "one decision per slice, like JPEG XS"; (4) where the history went astray. "Please do not answer legal or licensing questions." (Memo §2.3 stated "Motion vectors are derived from previously decoded frames" — the wording experts A and B repeated; see §1.5 and §4.3 item 8: OMC's vectors are transmitted.)

- **A (start_here.md, titled "Memo 011"; the expert's own text):**
  - Summary: "The clip is the only operator in the pipeline that is neither invertible nor computable from the decoded picture." Three changes: transform with no update step; quantiser closed-loop, interval-aware, with self-identifying reconstruction points; encoder order — "the first encoder's last act is to run the second encoder's procedure on its own reconstruction and emit what that returns". Then "the lock disappears, the legaliser disappears, the δ search disappears".
  - §1 exactness = E(D(E(x))) = E(x); two conditions: (1) the decoded picture must lie in the set the codec can describe (clip(T⁻¹(R(I))) is in general outside the image of D); (2) E canonical — step per band, intra/inter per band, refinement and chunk boundaries, refresh phase, bank state recomputable from y or pinned by a fixed tie-break. "The work so far went mostly into condition 2 (the lock) while condition 1 was broken."
  - §2.1–2.2 legality inside the synthesis: in an inverse step odd = d + P(even) the decoder knows P(even), so d ∈ [lo − P, hi − P]; reconstruct r = clamp(R(q), interval); the first encoder emits q* = Q(clamp(R(q), interval)), r* = clamp(R(q*), interval) — one step suffices, provable per coefficient with no dependence on neighbours ("which is what index capping lacked"). A's quality/bit claim: "its quality equals the plain clip measured today: the interval is exactly the set of d values giving a legal pixel, so the clamp lands where the clip would have landed. No bits change because the index alphabet and the tANS tables are untouched."
  - §2.3 needs a predict-only transform: the 5/3 update (even = a − U(d)) makes the even pixel depend on the high band while d's interval depends on the even pixel — the cycle behind "fix at one sample breaks its neighbour" (capping) and the K-round legaliser's slow convergence on dense content ("the legaliser is in fact the correct projection (onto cell ∩ range); the cycle is what stops it from finishing"). Predict-only (interpolating (N,0) family): reconstruct L coarsest-up (odd rows = LH + P(even rows), clamped into [lo, hi]; coarsest LL interval [lo, hi]), then H clamped into [lo − Pcol(L), hi − Pcol(L)]; dense rail plates handled by the same per-coefficient clamp, "no coupling for density to amplify"; two finest levels → (4,0) or (6,0). A's expectation: predict-only has an aliased low band and lower coding gain at low rates; "At contribution rates … I expect the loss to be small (a few tenths of a dB at most, possibly nil once the predictor length is chosen), but I have no measurement and this should be the first experiment. If it fails the 2× target, I have no bounded-iteration proof for any transform with an update step" (measured later: §4.4/§5.1, −2.4…−4.8 dB). A declares the cross-slice term COMPATIBLE (rows above final before the slice starts) and the display blend COMPATIBLE "as long as it remains an exactly invertible integer blend of legal values, which it is today" (A.10 keeps the blend).
  - §3.1 steps read off the picture: r(q, Δ) = sign(q)(|q| + β)Δ, β = m/2ᵏ, m odd, fixed per band → trailing zeros of 2ᵏ·r = those of Δ; any nonzero non-clamped coefficient reveals Δ by count-trailing-zeros; texture bias survives as the choice of m; disjointness needed across Δ (odd-numerator dyadic β with Δ ≥ 2ᵏ; finer Δ via a small per-Δ table checked offline); clamped coefficients excluded (equal to own interval boundary; a coincidental lattice point on the boundary is excluded harmlessly). Chunk syntax rule: "A chunk boundary is the first coefficient whose signature shows the coarser step. If the syntax cannot be recovered this way, simplify the syntax rather than adding a search."
  - §3.2 tie-breaks: all-zero or all-clamped band → fixed total order (coarsest step, then fixed mode order); the first encoder adopts it inline in its coarse-to-fine pass and requantises that band before finer ones (≤ 1 requantisation per band; "a coarser step on an already-empty band costs fewer bits, never more"). §3.3 intra/inter: intra if non-clamped nonzero coefficients share one signature, inter if c − pred does; fixed order if both; the first encoder may choose from the source by any heuristic, then emits what the identification rule returns on its own reconstruction; "Motion vectors are already decoder-derived" (premise from memo 010's wording; wrong for OMC, §4.3 item 8).
  - §3.4 sequence state by convention: frame 1 intra, bank empty, refresh phase = f(frame index). Mid-stream: identical pictures once every slice has been refreshed, identical bytes once the refresh phase coincides; "if byte identity from a mid-stream start is required, pin the phase to something both sides observe (frame count modulo 8 from the first frame both have seen)" (rejected in §4.3 item 4).
  - §3.5 / A.9 encoder order per slice: (1) forward transform; open-loop plan choice from source coefficients with estimated bits from the static tables "as today"; (2) ONE closed-loop pass coarse-to-fine band by band (quantise, reconstruct with interval clamps, re-quantise at clamped sites, check signature determinacy, apply tie-breaks) giving indices and y; (3) pack — "If the closed-loop indices exceed the budget (… rare), step Q one notch coarser and repeat step 2 once; the bank absorbs the rest" (the "one notch coarser" attacked in §4.3 item 4). Second encoder: same forward pass, recognises an already-canonical slice (every band consistent, every non-boundary coefficient on-lattice) and packs; on fresh source or loss damage it falls through to steps 1–3; "A false positive on fresh source is harmless".
  - §4 worst case: decoder one inverse with a compare-and-clamp per sample; fresh encode one forward (open-loop estimate) + one closed-loop quantise/reconstruct pass + ≤ 2 packs; hop encode one forward with inline signature inference + one pack (+ optionally one verify decode); shifts, adds, compares, count-trailing-zeros; no content-dependent iteration; latency unchanged ("the closed-loop pass replaces the plain quantise … reconstruction is already computed for temporal prediction").
  - §5 astray: (1) legality as post-processing (attempt 3 inverts a many-to-one map, unbounded on dense content); (2) plan as search — "The lattice-point test was also the wrong test after attempt 8: the legaliser had already produced in-cell coefficients that would have re-quantised correctly, and the lock rejected them for being off-point"; (3) open-loop quantisation (attempt 5 searched alternative plans instead of re-quantising within the plan); (4) the update step (9/7-like at the two finest levels = strongest coupling where overshoot lives); (5) the embedded bit-plane attempt right instinct, wrong layer, "paying 1–17 % of bits for a property that reconstruction-point signatures give at zero cost". Kept: slice structure, bank, static tANS, "decoder-side motion derivation", rolling refresh.
  - §6 measure first, in order: (1) predict-only (4,0)/(6,0) vs current, luma dB at equal bits — "decides whether the design is viable within the 2× target"; (2) interval-clamped closed-loop quantisation on the dense rail set: samples out of range at the final stage (should be 0) and generation-2 byte identity; (3) signature inference success rate on natural and graphics content, and frequency of tie-break bands.
  - Appendix A (for the hardware team): A.1 lo/hi example 4 and 1019 (10-bit SDI); predictors (Deslauriers–Dubuc) (2,0) P = (e[i]+e[i+1]+1)>>1; (4,0) P = (9·(e[i]+e[i+1]) − (e[i−1]+e[i+2]) + 8)>>4; (6,0) P = (150·(e[i]+e[i+1]) − 25·(e[i−1]+e[i+2]) + 3·(e[i−2]+e[i+3]) + 128)>>8; shift-add 9v = (v<<3)+v, 150v = (v<<7)+(v<<4)+(v<<2)+(v<<1), 25v = (v<<4)+(v<<3)+v, 3v = (v<<1)+v; suggested (6,0) or (4,0) at the finest level, (4,0) at the second, (2,0) coarser, "to be settled by measurement 1". A.2 forward L = e, H = o − P(e). A.3 inverse Hrec = clamp(Hval, lo_H − p, hi_H − p), o = Hrec + p. A.4 2-D per level, horizontal first: stage 1 Lcol from LL (legal) + LH_rec = clamp(LH_val, lo − Pcol, hi − Pcol); stage 2 Hmin/Hmax = lo/hi − Prow(Lcol row); stage 3 HL_rec = clamp(HL_val, Hmin, Hmax), HH_rec = clamp(HH_val, Hmin − Pcol(even Hcol rows), Hmax − Pcol(…)); stage 4 odd columns = Hcol + pr (legal by construction); "no cycle at any stage". A.5 coarsest first; coarsest LL reconstructed as clamp(LL_val, lo, hi); within a 16-row slice vertical recursion 16 → 8 → 4 → 2. A.6 Q(c) = 0 if |c| < z·Δ, else sign(c)·(floor((|c| − z·Δ)/Δ) + 1) (z = 9/16 on finer bands today); R(q, Δ) = sign(q)·((|q| + β)·Δ); Δ = 1 << ctz(|c| << k); for Δ < 2ᵏ a per-band disjoint table. A.7 closed loop per coefficient: q = Q(c); r = clamp(R(q,Δ) + pred, Hmin, Hmax) − pred; q* = Q(r); r* = clamp(R(q*,Δ) + pred, Hmin, Hmax); emit q*; proof as §2.2. A.8 canonical inference per band: clamped iff c equals Hmin or Hmax; signatures of c (intra) and c − pred_inter; mode = fixed order over consistent modes; Δ = 1 << sig or tie-break Δ (coarsest allowed by the plan syntax); indices = Q(c − pred_mode); any inconsistent band → slice treated as fresh source (A.7 after an open-loop plan). A.9 order as §3.5 (LL/LH first, then HL/HH, per band A.7 then A.8, redo A.7 once if the inference differs; step 4 "If over budget: step Q one notch coarser, repeat step 3 once, pack"). A.10 decoder: tANS decode, dequantise, add prediction, inverse with clamps, "Display-side slice-boundary blend (unchanged)"; "No pixel-domain clip remains anywhere in the chain."
- **B (vibe.md; the expert's own text):**
  - §0 one idea: decode output a fixed point (y = D(E(x)) ⇒ E(y) = E(x), D(E(y)) = y); (1) encoder reconstruction = decoder output bit for bit incl. the legality step, used as reference, rate-control statistics input and plan re-decision test; (2) every decision a computable function of the decoded picture. History in one sentence: decisions depended on pre-quantisation source data, then "enormous fixed work" was spent rediscovering them.
  - §1.1 canonical set: plan, mode flags, rate-control state, legality handling, motion vectors ("yours already are — keep this"), refresh schedule (frame-counter), slice-boundary handling, entropy tables; free inputs = configuration, CBR budget, frame index. "Canonical is not the same as 'deterministic'": a deterministic function of the source is useless; test = run the decision at gen 1 on the reconstruction and at gen 2 on y — "the inputs must be equal objects".
  - §1.2 rate control feed-forward: budget = nominal (config) + causal bank of ACTUAL prior-slice bits with the 2× cap — canonical by induction (gen 2's earlier slices byte-identical); first frames exact automatically; deterministic stuffing (tANS flush + fixed filler). Plan by LUT (bits[index, band] from the static tANS tables, "the property JPEG XS exploits"): quantise once at the finest candidate step; power-of-two steps + fixed 9/16 dead zone → lattices nest, coarser indices by shifts and a constant add; fixed threshold rule (cheapest fitting, fixed tie-break); worst case one transform + one quantise/estimate pass (fusable) + one entropy pass. Critical subtlety: y's coefficients lie inside cells, not at lattice points, so plan choices can disagree near decision boundaries → the encoder computes the plan from its own (projected) reconstruction: quantise provisionally, reconstruct and legalise, recompute the plan from the reconstruction, compare (one comparison on ordinary content; bounded ladder otherwise). §1.3 mode decision from reconstruction-side costs; motion vectors "derive from decoded frames — keep that exactly as it is".
  - §2 stages: A prediction from the previous PROJECTED decoded frame; B forward lifting as today ("do not abandon it"), quantise at the finest step, coarser by shifts; C plan by LUT sums; D reconstruction IS the decoder — in-cell projection at both ends (repeat K times, K fixed e.g. 4, early exit: clip → forward → snap coefficients into transmitted cells → inverse; final clip); argument: cell box ∩ convex preimage of the legal box, alternating projection converges when the intersection is non-empty; zero bits, quality = plain clip, adds/shifts/compares; E canonical re-decision and verification from the projected reconstruction's coefficients (replaces the 50-pass lock with one comparison in steady state); F static tANS + deterministic flush/stuffing. Boundary blend: "Delete it" — a display blend the next encoder undoes is "a hidden metadata channel across your baseband hop"; the cross-slice lifting term should be a function of previously decoded rows, exactly invertible; if that cannot be made seamless, fallback = self-contained slices (fixed lifting initialisation at the slice top) "and accept the one-row cost at the boundary".
  - §3 rails: 3.1 "The dead zone does most of the work" (sparse overshoot 100–1,100 samples/frame from the two finest levels; coarser or equal-step dead zone on the finest bands suppresses nearly all of the sparse case); 3.2 the projection handles the coupled case when a solution exists; 3.3 ESCALATION LADDER when the intersection is empty: coarsen the offending bands (two finest levels) by one power of two per rung, re-project, fixed depth ("a handful of rungs"), unsignalled — gen 2 recomputes the coarse plan from the reconstruction; claimed no bits/quality violation because it triggers only where a legal decode at the finer step is impossible (ringing around flat plates quantised to zero). 3.4 at gen ≥ 2 projection round 1 is the identity (idempotence check "verified in silicon, every frame, for free"; gen-2 projection cost = one round); the 0.3–2.7 % off-lattice coefficients are "a defect of your lock", not of the projection: "Cell membership is sufficient; lattice-point membership is a strictly stronger, unnecessary demand" → replace the lattice test with an in-cell test ("a sign check and a shift per coefficient").
  - §4 work: steady state 1 fused forward/quantise/LUT pass + plan comparison + inverse/projection (typically 1–2 rounds; gen ≥ 2 exactly 1) + entropy encode; worst case (rails, gen 1 only) + ladder, each rung one re-quantise-by-shift + one projection (2K passes), depth 4–6; "cheaper even in its worst case" than ~50 entropy passes per slice.
  - §5 astray: (1) clip as decoder-only afterthought (architectural mistake); (2) plan from source then rediscovered; (3) lattice-point lock ("the single cheapest fix in the history"); (4) rate control by trial encodes; (5) boundary blend "a patch on a patch"; (6) attempts 1–3 search for information after destroying it — δ recovery "explodes to 190,000 slice-inverses on rails and finds several valid δ — the problem was never well-posed"; (7) the bit-plane variant abandoned for the wrong reasons (1–17 % from static contexts and fixed order, treatable; not recommended over the plan path; plan-free fallback direction = canonical adaptive-to-static contexts + projection).
  - §6 build: keep transform, static tANS, MV derivation, refresh schedule, integer lifting, slice size; delete blend, lock, repair engine, capping, δ recovery; feed-forward budget + one-pass LUT plan decided from the reconstruction; in-cell projection at both ends; bounded invisible ladder; in-cell test.
  - §7 verify first, in order: (1) in-cell test on the attempt-8 corpus — "hours of work, not weeks"; (2) wire reference frames and plan statistics to the projected reconstruction, re-measure quality and generation stability; (3) LUT plan selection and its fit accuracy vs actual entropy output; (4) the ladder on the dense rail corpus: termination depth, gen-2 exactness through escalated plans, eye check that escalated rail slices show flat rails; (5) stress canonicality at scene cuts, first frames, forced-intra slices, bank-saturated frames. Request: measure generation 3 and 10, both hop types, "in the raw byte counts".
- **C (gemini.md; 4 short sections):** §1 canonical plan: the rate control must be "mathematically idempotent"; the first encoder runs a bounded analysis-by-synthesis loop (plan → inverse + spatial clip → rate control on the clipped reconstruction → if a different plan results, adjust the transmitted plan to the stable state). §2 legality: the clip after an inverse wavelet pushes coefficients off the dead-zone lattice; index capping "severely degraded quality (costing 0.8–21 dB)"; enforce legality "directly within the transform stage" by "applying clipping to the intermediate spatial representations during the update and predict steps of the lifting scheme" (non-linear but invertible lifting) — guarantees the final synthesis never exceeds the legal range (e.g. 10-bit 4…1019), no post-transform clip. §3 work: today's lock ≈ 50 entropy-pass equivalents; rail recovery up to 190,000 slice-inverse equivalents; gen ≥ 2 becomes one pass (forward, canonical plan, static tANS) "aligned with the standard set by JPEG XS". §4 flaws: (a) order of decisions — decoder-side clip; (b) rate-control fragility — the causal bank (2× cap) + the 9/16 dead zone ("inherently sensitive to small perturbations") make one clipped pixel alter later slices' budgets at the next generation → "canonical plan reproduction mathematically impossible without the unbounded searches".
- **D (perplexity.pdf; the recorded text is D's SECOND reply):** the file opens with the owner's pushback — "it's already in my prompt, attached. also, your job is not to provide research projects but A SOLUTION. if you need to provide multiple solutions because you are not sure which works, that's fine. But you cannot simply make this a research project" — and D's acknowledgement that it "should have read [the attachment] before replying" (the first reply had not used the memo). D's solution: a "canonical reconstructed-image codec" — the legal pixels themselves are the stable representation; every transmitted decision a deterministic function of those pixels and a specified decoded reference state.
  - Boundary of the claim: guarantees legality, generation stability, bounded work and exact size under a specified rate envelope; "I cannot honestly assert, without implementing and viewing it, that it also matches JPEG XS at half its bitrate with no visible artifact or quality cost."
  - Build the stable image first: per component a fixed NESTED set of legal integer sample values per precision level (finest = every legal integer), fixed tie-breaking, idempotent (re-quantising an output returns it); code those samples by reversible prediction from reconstructed neighbours and, where appropriate, the one previous decoded frame. Core constraint: "The prediction determines an efficiently coded residual, but it must not shift the set of sample values that the encoder is permitted to reconstruct. Otherwise, the next encoder can see the same pixel but place it in a different prediction-relative quantisation cell." Settles the dense black/white edge case (a legal rail value is a permissible reconstruction value; no clipped excursion to infer; no coupled sweeps); removes the decoder-side legaliser and the lattice lock. "Do not put a lossy inverse wavelet after this canonicalisation"; a transform may remain only as an exactly reversible coder of differences between already chosen legal pixels (invertibility alone does not make lossily quantised coefficients reconstruct to legal canonical pixels). Cited patterns: causal reconstructed-neighbour prediction and in-loop quantisation as established low-complexity patterns (its reference [4] = ISO/IEC 14495-1:1999, JPEG-LS), "this particular canonical-pixel construction is my proposed application of them, not a demonstrated OMC-1 result".
  - Canonical-rule table: reconstructed values from nested legal alphabets; predictors from reconstructed pixels and a specified previous decoded frame, deterministic arithmetic and tie-breaks; inter/intra and refresh by a fixed published schedule or a deterministic rule on decoded state, not an original-picture cost comparison; entropy syntax one fixed traversal/context/table/termination/padding per reconstructed image and state; slice boundaries: reset or define every dependency, "Remove a display-side blend that requires a subsequent encoder to undo a hidden pre-display image"; rate allocation reproducible from decoded data or fixed, never chosen from source trial encodes.
  - "The rate-allocation rule is the hard part": two precision plans reconstructing to the same pixels cannot be told apart; two byte streams decoding to the same image and state cannot both be reproduced → "the syntax must admit exactly one encoder-chosen representation per reachable decoded image and state … not a job for a 50-pass plan search". First implementation: fixed format-and-position-derived slice budgets and precision schedules, causal residuals coded once, canonical padding; a later short fixed-list local choice only "if each choice has a disjoint set of attainable decoded images and its choice can be recovered with bounded logic from those images", not claimed free without measurement.
  - Work and rate: one causal reconstruction-and-symbol pass + one entropy-pack pass per slice + fixed control/padding; comparisons/shifts/adds/lookups; ZU7EV fit still depends on throughput, memory traffic, format, entropy implementation. Limits: "no honest one-pass guarantee that arbitrary slice content will fit a prescribed narrow budget while also forbidding any additional quality loss"; an overflow-safe fallback to a coarser legal alphabet stays stable and bounded "but it can visibly worsen difficult content"; a fallback to exact samples can exceed the budget; D's engineering choice: define the operating bitrate and an admissible source/quality envelope, reserve a worst-case bound, test full frames incl. first frames, cuts and dense legal-limit graphics — "If the stipulated bitrate cannot fund the fallback precision required by that criterion, I would report a failed requirement rather than conceal it behind padding, extra passes, or a nominal 'one-pass' rate controller."
  - Where it went wrong: (1) order of operations (lossy transform numbers → illegal pixels → clip → ask the next encoder to reconstruct the lost decision); ICL "still leaves coefficients away from the points expected by the lock; that result is consistent with the architectural diagnosis, not a reason to run a larger lock"; (2) plan recovery as a substitute for canonical rate control ("a faster plan search cannot recover information that is not there"); (3) the cross-slice term and the reversible-undo display blend create extra state that must be uniquely reconstructible at a baseband hop → remove from the correctness path, handle seams through the predictor and reconstruction design. Bottom line: build and evaluate the canonical legal-pixel causal-prediction prototype with fixed reproducible allocation first; half-XS-rate quality "an empirical pass/fail criterion".
- **Coordinator synthesis (S5.343):** CONSENSUS (A–D): legality inside the reconstruction loop, same at both ends, no pixel-domain clip after a lossy inverse; every decision canonical from the reconstruction, the first encoder emits what the canonical procedure returns on its own reconstruction; the lattice-point lock is the wrong test (A: signatures; B: in-cell) — Steps X/Z (cell-read, mode-aware) already confirm: roots 94–100 %; the display blend is suspect (B, D remove; A compatible) — X4 measured it as the ONLY source of dng720's residual (81 samples). DISAGREEMENT on the transform: A requires predict-only — the record answers it: DESIGN3 predict-only FALSIFIED at ~3 dB at every rate (S5.212, −3.67 dB), and the owner forbids any dB loss → A's structural premise out; A's longer (4,0)/(6,0) predictors had never been measured (DESIGN3 used 2-tap convex predictors), but the loss is attributed to the missing update (aliased low band), so recovery to 0 dB implausible; A's other elements (self-identifying reconstruction points, one-step closed-loop requantisation, canonical emission order) are transform-independent and usable. B keeps the transform and turns ICL into the core with a bounded ladder. D is below the efficiency needed (1.33× vs 2× today). DESIGN CANDIDATE then: transform kept; ICL projection as THE reconstruction at both ends; lock replaced by the canonical rule (mode-aware cell read + one predicate), gen 1 runs that rule on its own reconstruction and emits it; bounded escalation ladder; seam handling moved into the invertible transform path; optional self-identifying points. Candidate detail: ICL projection "K fixed, early exit; encoder references/statistics on it"; ladder "(the owner's eye decides any quality effect; bits only fall)"; seam handling "blend removed or made legal-safe — seam visibility judged by eye". First measurements then planned: T1 = "how often the canonical rule on the gen-1 reconstruction differs from the open-loop plan; bits and quality of adopting it; then gen 2, gen 3 and gen 10 byte identity, both hops, on the suite"; T2 = escalation ladder on residual slices: "natural spot/volley residuals and cut24/extrema: rungs used, residual → 0, bits, quality, renders". → S5.344 coordinator decision: (1) the display seam blend STAYS ("it prevents the seam line; removing it is out"), its legality leak (post-blend out-of-range samples on seam rows, e.g. 81 per dng720 frame) to be handled by δ recovery on those few sparse samples (no picture change); (2) the escalation ladder is NOT a design element (per-slice step = visible step); T2 still runs as a MEASUREMENT of the dense-rail residual only; (3) dense rail content an OPEN problem; design core at that point = ICL + gen-1 canonical self-check + mode-aware cell-read lock + δ recovery for the sparse residual. → S5.345 REVERSED (1): the blend is the smudge; "δ recovery would only make generations reproduce the smudge exactly, not remove it"; target: "slices joined INSIDE the invertible transform with neither a seam line nor a blend, legality and exactness included"; ordered a documentation review before any design (XSL.md, BITSTREAM.md 815–870, SESSION_LEDGER 2026-08-12 PART XXII, S5.233, smudge ledger entries, HVBC boundary failures as falsified list) = §3.1. S5.345 note (owner "test things …"): T2 ladder renders restored; T3 (blend off vs on, today's build, renders + row-phase table) ordered = §3.2. S5.346: stop patching; T1/T2 halted (no numbers), T3 finished.

### 4.2 DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH (coordinator, `Subagents/CORE/DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH.md`, 2026-09-27, ledger S5.346–S5.347; status DRAFT, nothing built)
Starting point: the requirements (PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS rev. 6 and owner rulings: no seams, no dB, no bits, no artifacts, both latency bars, ZU7EV comfortably, royalty-free, video-only hops, rebuild not patch), not today's codec.
- §0 One principle: decoded picture a fixed point E(D(E(x))) = E(x): (a) ONE reconstruction pipeline shared by encoder and decoder, legality included; (b) every encoder decision a function of data the next encoder also has (reconstruction, previous decoded frame, frame index, configuration), and the first encoder emits what the canonical procedure returns on its own reconstruction; nothing recovered, searched, repaired or undone. (Consensus of all four experts; "our own measurements S5.338–S5.342 confirm the cell-level version". Method: where evidence is missing, the design names ONE decisive test for that choice, not a tuning experiment.)
- §1 Slices = rate and packet units, not transform boundaries: today each 16-row (8 at 720p) slice is transformed alone; the bottom-edge mirror is the root of the seam line (duplicate-row fraction 0.53 luma vs 0.03 in source; seam step 1.82× interior at 0.5 bpp); every seam fix since patched that mirror, and the blend is the smudge. DECISION: vertical transform runs CONTINUOUSLY down the frame, line-based, no slice-boundary extension (Chrysafis & Ortega 1999 line-based wavelet schedule). Cost to decide: vertical lookahead (LATENCY.md: T = slice_h lines + one slice period + 2 lines). DECISIVE TEST D1: lookahead rows for 2 and 3 vertical levels and latency per format (720p50 8-row tightest: today 0.500 ms codec-only, 0.806 ms with conversion) vs both bars; coding gain vs today; seam statistic.
  [§1 detail: the seam fixes that patched the mirror were "cross-slice term, two-row blend, fills, extra rows, finer last-row quantiser"; with 2 vertical levels (the asymmetric 2V×5H structure already recorded) the lookahead is "a handful of rows"; D1 pass conditions: (i) exact lookahead rows for 2 and 3 vertical levels, 5/3 and the chosen update form, latency per format vs both bars (720p50 8-row tightest: today 0.500 ms codec-only, 0.806 ms with conversion); (ii) coding gain vs today at equal bits — "a continuous transform should gain, not lose"; (iii) seam statistic (row-phase error, duplicate-row fraction) "must equal the interior".]
- §2 Transform and legality inside the synthesis: expert A's clamp [lo − P, hi − P] needs an ACYCLIC synthesis; the symmetric 5/3 update creates a cycle. DESIGN CHOICE: keep an update but ONE-SIDED (past-only taps, e.g. H[i−2], H[i−3]) so the synthesis stays a DAG; vertically also causal. OPEN POINT flagged: across LEVELS the multi-level processing order must be written out and checked for cycles. DECISIVE TEST D2: coding gain of the one-sided update vs today's 5/3 + 9/7-M at equal bits, per plane, 0.5/1.0/2.0 bpp; pass = no loss; shift-add constants.
  [§2 named D2-failure case (stated in the design, one line; it is the object of review items 2, 3, 6, 8 in §4.3): symmetric transform + the in-cell projection as the DEFINED reconstruction at both ends (expert B; W/W′/X/Z: "in-cell exact on 100 % of coefficients, quality = clip, zero bits"); its open limit, dense rail content (projection stalls), "would remain an open problem, never covered by a visible fallback". §2 also: the causal vertical one-sided update "shortens the lookahead of §1".]
  [§1 further text: "line-based wavelet coding is already recorded in DESIGN.md as JPEG 2000-family practice" (bears on §4.3 item 7); consequences stated: "no seam by construction; no cross-slice term; no display blend; nothing for the next encoder to undo". Header: evidence used = the ledgers (by S-number), seam_R.txt, the four expert answers (Communication/202600927/, S5.343).]
  [§2 detail: expert A removes the update, "but our record answers that: predict-only costs ~3 dB (DESIGN3, S5.212) — out". Cycle mechanism: the symmetric update even = L − U(H[i−1], H[i]) uses the very H values whose intervals depend on it; "H[j]'s interval depends on even[j] and even[j+1]"; with past-only taps (H[i−2], H[i−3]) the order "even[i] → H[i−1] clamp …" is a DAG in each direction. "The update keeps a low-pass/anti-alias role; whether a one-sided update keeps the coding gain of the symmetric one is unknown." OPEN POINT: with an update the low band is not raw samples, so across LEVELS a level-ℓ even sample's interval depends on level-ℓ H values while L is produced by the coarser level's synthesis; the multi-level processing order (coarse-to-fine vs line-interleaved streaming) "must be written out and checked for cycles BEFORE D2 is worth running". D2 = coding gain of "the best of a small family of past-only taps, horizontal and vertical" vs today's 5/3 + 9/7-M at equal bits, 0.5/1.0/2.0 bpp, per plane; pass = no loss (owner rule); every predictor/update constant in shift-add form.]
- §3 Quantiser: dead-zone scalar, power-of-two steps; at clamped sites the first encoder emits q* = Q(clamp(R(q))), r* = clamp(R(q*)); self-identifying reconstruction points r(q, Δ) = sign(q)(|q| + β)Δ, β = m/2ᵏ odd → plan READ from the picture. DECISIVE TEST D3: quality/bits neutrality of odd-dyadic offsets (in place of today's texture bias), disjointness table for small Δ.
  [§3 detail: at a clamped site the next encoder's Q returns q* (expert A §2.2, proved per coefficient, no neighbour dependence once §2 is acyclic); Δ read from any non-clamped nonzero coefficient by count-trailing-zeros, clamped coefficients (equal to their interval bound) excluded — "no candidate list, no ranking (the plan class of S5.332 had exactly this cause: bands whose evidence the clip destroyed)"; D3 "must be neutral".]
- §4 Canonical decisions: budget = nominal + causal bank of ACTUAL bits of earlier slices, capped, deterministic stuffing; plan one pass over nested power-of-two lattices with static tANS code-length tables as cost LUT, fixed threshold rule, fixed tie-break; gen 1 applies the plan-READING rule to its own reconstruction, unreadable band → canonical tie-break (coarsest step); intra/inter per band from signatures of c and c − pred; motion vectors "derived from previous decoded frames (keep; canonical already)" [later found WRONG, §4.3 item 8]; sequence state by convention (frame 1 intra, bank empty, refresh phase from frame index); mid-stream start identical pictures after one refresh cycle; exactly one representation per reachable picture and state (expert D).
  [§4 wording (the statements the review in §4.3 item 4 and item 7 attacks): budget = nominal per-slice budget + causal bank of ACTUAL bits spent by earlier slices of the same frame, capped — "canonical by induction — at generation 2 the earlier slices are byte-identical, so the bank is identical"; deterministic stuffing to the exact size. Plan: "quantise once at the finest candidate step per band; coarser candidates' indices follow by shifts (nested power-of-two lattices); costs from the static tANS code-length tables; a fixed threshold rule picks the cheapest plan that fits, fixed tie-break (expert B; the JPEG XS-shaped decision)"; the first encoder applies the plan-READING rule to its own reconstruction; an unreadable band (all-zero or all-clamped) adopts the canonical tie-break "(coarsest step — never more bits) before finer bands". Intra/inter per band from signatures of c and c − pred; "fixed order when both are consistent (expert A §3.3)". Mid-stream start: "identical pictures after one refresh cycle, identical bytes once the phase matches (to be stated in the spec)". Syntax: exactly one representation per reachable decoded picture and state (expert D).]
- §5 Temporal: prediction from the previous committed legal frame, one reference (C2); decoder-derived vectors; rolling intra refresh by frame index (A5). §6 Static tANS per band, tables double as the rate LUT. §7 Removed entirely: generation lock and candidate walk; repair engine (gamut_strict); R1 and every search/recovery; K-round legaliser (if D2 passes); index capping; XSL cross-slice term; display seam blend and un-blend; pixel-domain clip; every patch switch. §8 Work: decoder one inverse + one compare-and-clamp per sample; first encoder one forward fused with quantise-and-cost, one closed-loop quantise/reconstruct pass, ≤ 2 packs; no content-dependent iteration. §9 Test order D1 → D2 → D3 → clean prototype (new sandbox) gated on legality (0 oob, no clip), g2/g3/g10 identity through both hops on the full format/depth/chroma/rate set incl. cut24/extrema/graphics/720p, quality and bits vs today, work per slice, renders. §10 IP provenance: integer lifting (Sweldens 1996); interpolating predictors (Deslauriers–Dubuc 1989); line-based wavelet schedule (Chrysafis & Ortega 1999); dead-zone scalar quantisation; interval clamping, one-step requantisation, odd-dyadic points, one-sided update, canonical tie-breaks (own work / generic); static tANS (cleared); rate control by code-length tables (cleared as "rate control" in the S5.37 IP review). Method chains on record: lock ← plan chosen from the source; clip damage ← overshoot ← quantised ringing; blend (smudge) ← seam ← slice-bottom mirror ← slices transformed independently ← slices as transform units; update step ← aliasing ← coarse band made by subsampling and re-split many levels.
  [§5/§8/§9 detail: "the refresh must now also be canonical (it is: a slice refreshed at frame t is intra by rule)"; §8: an encoder at a hop runs the same work and "the canonical rules reproduce the stream"; latency = today's model + the §1 lookahead rows; §9: decisive tests "each decides a design choice; none is a patch"; the prototype gate includes full-frame colour renders for the owner's eye (no seam, no smudge, no step). File RULE (owner 2026-09-27): "every test, table, gate and summary reports Y, Cb and Cr side by side — never luma-only (PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS C5); D1–D3 and all gates judged per plane; renders include chroma" — review item 8 ("D1/D3 planes unnamed") is a breach of this rule stated in the same file.]

### 4.3 Adversarial review of DESIGN_FROM_SCRATCH (ledger S5.349; read-only; scripts `Subagents/CORE/review/`: dag1d.py, dag1d_b.py, dag1d_c.py, dag2d.py, cycle_show.py, order_chk.py, cgain.py, cgain_chk.py, cgain2d.py, vdelay.py, vdelay2.py, lossfoot.py; paper evidence). No report file exists in that folder; the report is the ledger entry.
VERDICT: §2 FAILS — REDESIGN; §1 only as an extended D1; §3/§4 need changes.
1. (BLOCKER) One-sided update: acyclic in 1-D for lags ≥ 2, but ACROSS LEVELS the minimum acyclic lag is 2^(L−ℓ)+1 (2 levels (3,2); 3 levels (5,3,2); 5 horizontal levels 17 at the finest, 20 with 9/7-M); explicit 2-level cycle for taps H[i−2], H[i−3]; future-only updates cyclic; 2-D stage order: predict-only acyclic, symmetric updates cyclic (4,064 self-loops), vertical (3,2) with horizontal predict-only or lags (20,20,5,3,2) acyclic. Coding gain, AR(1) ρ = 0.95: the optimal weight at any acyclic lag is 0.00 = predict-only (1/2/5 levels: symmetric 6.29/8.60/9.60, predict-only 5.57/7.39/7.91 dB). Real frames vs today (Y/Cb/Cr): predict-only dng −2.39/−3.06/−2.96, cityalley −2.30/−2.51/−2.55; expert A (6,0)/(4,0) dng −2.79/−3.62/−3.50; acyclic one-sided with 5/3-style weights dng −3.87/−4.83/−4.68, cityalley −3.67/−4.01/−4.07 → legality-in-synthesis = predict-only in disguise (DESIGN3, S5.212) — FALSIFIED under the zero-dB rule, chroma worst.
2. Continuous vertical transform: added lookahead 5/3 2V 9 lines, 3V 21, 9/7-M 2V 21, one-sided/predict-only 2V 3; latency 720p50 5/3 2V 0.667 ms codec-only, ~0.94 ms with conversion (2 lines margin), 4:2:0 chroma 0.833 / 1.11 ms FAIL, 3V FAIL (1.5 ms), 1080p 40 lines vs today 34 (XS ~32); MAJOR packets (slice k's clamps depend on slice k+1 → rate/bank cycle or a one-period wait); BLOCKER for an in-cell projection run on a continuous transform (K × D lines ≈ 1 ms at 720p); MAJOR memory (+~14 rows state at 8K 4:2:2, ~3–4 Mbit; decoder already 24.25/27 Mb URAM) → breaks the ZU7EV margin; BLOCKER A5: one lost packet damages rows −3…+12 around the slice, rolling refresh no longer isolates (XSL.md refresh-barrier drift "crept one slice per refresh cycle"); MAJOR motion seams in the residual; MAJOR per-slice Δ is still a strip-level step.
3. Odd-dyadic points: one-step fixed point sound (even when step > legal window); Δ = 1 cannot self-identify; Δ < 2^k needs tables pushing points up to ~0.44Δ off the centroid (~3× within-cell MSE at Δ = 2); β = ±1/4 ≈ +75 % distortion on nonzero coefficients, ±1/16 ≈ +5 %; the new Q must become normative (C8); all-clamped-band tie-break must keep clamped values clamped; with an in-cell projection 24–54 % of coefficients are off-point on cut24/extrema → signatures fail.
4. Canonical decisions: bank canonical by induction ✓; intra/inter "fixed order" can exceed the budget → cheapest consistent by LUT then tie-break; "never more bits" unproven; "one notch coarser" overflow = the banned ladder (S5.344); MID-STREAM hop: the refresh phase is not in the image (S5.235, 61.6 % moved at g2) → never force the phase at hops, read intra/inter from signatures; plan syntax unspecified (BITSTREAM.md:641).
5. Legality: RCT/RGB 4:4:4 legality is cross-plane (no per-coefficient clamp) — design silent; conversion path (polyphase negative lobes) needs its own clip.
6. Removals: XSL's −0.17…−0.46 dB (−3.7 worst) recovered only by a continuous SYMMETRIC 5/3; refresh barriers still needed; pixel clip still needed for RCT/conversion; coefficient saturation (BITSTREAM 4.2a) must stay.
7. IP: continuous vertical 5/3 with ≤ 2 vertical levels + slices as rate units + "JPEG XS-shaped decision" = the JPEG XS architecture → source to pre-XS art or do not build; odd-dyadic points resemble QIM (Chen–Wornell 1998, expired).
8. Repeated/ignored: DESIGN3 (in disguise); DECODER-DERIVED MOTION VECTORS — WRONG in the design (copied from expert A): OMC's vectors are encoder-derived from committed history and TRANSMITTED; decoder-derived was Design A, falsified on A5 and deleted (S5.29) — coordinator error; S5.105(4)/S5.156/S5.226 not reconciled; S5.176; XSL refresh-barrier evidence; S5.344 ladder ban; S5.338–S5.342 / S5.70 on the in-cell projection; HVBC region steps; F12 (720p latency). Chroma-blind items: D1/D3 planes unnamed; 4:2:0 absent (doubles vertical lookahead); chroma rails and the extLIM Cb case not gated.
- Coordinator conclusion (S5.349): both new pillars falsified on paper — legality inside the synthesis needs predict-only (−2.4…−4.8 dB, chroma worst), and the continuous vertical transform breaks A5, the 8K memory margin and 720p/4:2:0 latency and sits on the XS architecture. OPEN: (a) dense rail content (projection does not finish in fixed work on cut24/extrema); (b) the seam/smudge on slice edges as a separate design problem.

### 4.4 Follow-ups (S5.350–S5.352)
- S5.350: owner asked whether the 2.4–4.8 dB is truly from removing the smoothing step, or whether legality can be built into the rebuild with a (rebuilt) smoothing step. Recorded answer: within lifting-with-per-coefficient-clamps the loss is structural (the update creates cycles; acyclic updates have optimal weight 0), but no proof exists for ALL invertible multiresolution transforms — the open question. Owner: no design that repeats work many times ("not a codec, a medieval painting"). Memo 012 (`Project/MEMO_TO_CODEC_EXPERT_from_Fable_012.md`) written with measured results since memo 010 (in-cell projection exact 100 % of coefficients, quality = clip, 0 bits; mode-aware cell-read lock 94–100 %; blend = the only residual source on dng720 and = the smudge; predict-only / one-sided update −2.4…−4.8 dB chroma worst; continuous vertical transform fails 720p 4:2:0 latency, 8K memory, A5, motion seams, XS resemblance; motion vectors transmitted, correcting a premise) and three non-leading questions: (1) an exactly invertible integer multiresolution transform with 5/3-class anti-aliasing (chroma too) whose synthesis is legal by construction in one fixed-work pass with gen 2 recovering the same coefficients — or why not; (2) dense rail content legal + exact in small fixed work, no visible change, no bits; (3) joining 8/16-row rate-controlled, loss-contained slices with no seam and no level-shifting blend, within latency/memory/A5, no hidden undo.
- S5.351: owner: "test a few more things before writing back"; "what causes the need for the smoothness step? keeping it is patching." Coordinator answer recorded: the update exists because the coarse band is built by subsampling and then RE-DECOMPOSED level after level; without anti-aliasing, detail folds into the coarse bands and costs bits at every coarser level — the update is a patch on deep dyadic re-splitting. Candidate to measure: shallow predict-only (1–2 levels) + CAUSAL spatial prediction of the remaining coarse band (own predictors; NOT the LOCO-I/JPEG-LS MED predictor). Memo 012 HELD until measured. SA11 tasks S1–S4 (+S5, below).
- S5.352 (owner method, binding; §1.7). Chains written: lock ← plan unrepeatable ← plan chosen from the source (root: decision input); clip damage ← overshoot ← quantised ringing + unconstrained reconstruction ← one-sweep legality blocked by the update cycle ← update needed for aliasing ← deep re-splitting of a subsampled coarse band (root: decomposition choice); blend/smudge ← seam ← slice-bottom mirror ← vertical transform needs rows below within a latency-bounded slice (root: vertical transform choice). Two roots coincide in the VERTICAL direction → candidate: vertical CAUSAL prediction (rows predicted only from finished rows above; no rows below → no mirror, no seam, no blend; each row final before the next → legal by construction; no deep vertical re-splitting → no vertical update). Added to SA11's test as S5.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## §5 Vertical-causal family — offline tests (SA11, 2026-09-27; ledger S5.353–S5.358)

Common setup for S and V (SA11_plan/structure_S.txt, vcausal_V.txt; notes/struct_s.py, dpcm1.c, struct_sum.py, vcausal.py, vc_sum.py; numpy, no codec build): 10 intra cells, 4:2:2 10-bit full frames: cf_gfx 448×256 f0,f5 (slice 8 rows); dng 1280×720 f0,f5 (8); dng 1920×1080 f0,f5 (16); cityalley 1920×1080 f0,f5 (16); spotrobotL 1920×1080 f0,f5 (16). All frames INTRA. Structure as `CORE/review/cgain2d.py`: V1,H1,V2,H2 then H3,H4,H5 horizontal-only on LL2 (2 vertical, 5 horizontal). BASELINE = model of today: vertical 5/3 with update at V1,V2; horizontal 9/7-M-like at H1,H2 = (4,0) predictor (−1,9,9,−1)/16 + 5/3 update (reviewer's approximation, not bit-exact 9/7-M); 5/3 at H3–H5; per slice (8/16 rows), whole-sample symmetric extension at slice and frame edges, FLOAT lifting. Quantiser dead zone 9/16 (q = sign·floor(|c|/D + 7/16), recon q·D); D = 2^round(Qf − log2 √w_b) per band (w_b = synthesis energy of a unit impulse), Qf swept in 0.5 steps; coarse band step × 2^k, k ∈ {−3..0}, best mean of the three plane PSNRs (baseline picked k = −1 almost everywhere). Rate = zeroth-order entropy per band per frame (DPCM residuals one histogram per plane); bpp = bits of all three planes / luma pixels. PSNR per plane on integer-rounded [0,1023]-clipped output; values at 0.5/1.0/2.0 by linear interpolation of PSNR in log2(bpp). Deltas: mean over 10 cells / worst cell, dB Y/Cb/Cr.

### 5.2 Round V — band-domain vertical-causal (S5.356; vcausal_V.txt)
Structure: each row transformed horizontally, 5 levels (the baseline's horizontal filters); each coefficient predicted from the SAME band's reconstructed coefficient row above (closed loop in the coefficient domain); residual quantised per band (dead zone 9/16, power-of-two steps from the 1-D synthesis weights, coarse offset k chosen as for the baseline); rows run top to bottom through the whole frame (slice boundaries only matter for the per-slice predictor choice).

- Ops/sample and look-back: baseline 12.3 forward (+ same inverse), whole slice before the vertical transform. V_co / V_zero: encoder horizontal forward 10.5 + subtract 1 + quantise ~3 + dequantise/add 2 = 16.5 + horizontal inverse 10.5 for output = 27; decoder 2 + 10.5 = 12.5; look-back 1 coefficient row. V_avg3 +4 per sample. canS +1.1 (16-row) / +2.3 (8-row), look-back 2 rows; canR +18, 2 rows; sig encoder +18, decoder as V_co + 2 bits per band per slice. S5a/S5aC decoder 7.8–13.8 (+2 compares for the clamp), encoder 15.6–21.6; look-back 1 row.

### 5.3 Round E — integer legality + generation exactness (S5.356, S5.358; exact_E.txt; notes/exact_e.py; exE/)
Frames: frame 0 of cf_gfx 448×256, dng 1280×720, dng 1920×1080, spotrobotL 1920×1080 (4:2:2 10-bit), and synthetic SA7_legality_v2/arms/cut24.yuv (256×64) and ext_10_422_l0.yuv (extFULL, 512×128). PIPELINE (S5a, INTEGER): row predictor p = (a + 2b + c + 2) >> 2 of the reconstructed row above (row 0: 512); residual e = x − p; horizontal integer 5/3 lifting with update, 5 levels (h = o − floor((e0+e1)/2), l = e + floor((h_−1 + h + 2)/4)), symmetric extension; dead-zone 9/16, power-of-two steps D ≥ 1 per band from the 1-D synthesis weights (coarse × 2^−1); recon q·D; one Qf per frame (carried in the stream). Gen 1 encodes the source; gen 2 the gen-1 decode with identical rules; gen 3 the gen-2 decode. Variants: E1 per-pixel clamp after the horizontal inverse; E2K2 / E2K4 = E1 + 2 / 4 rounds of 1-D in-cell projection per row (clamp → forward → clamp every coefficient into its quantiser cell → inverse → clamp) at both ends; E2qK4 = E2K4 decoder + the encoder emits q* = Q(F(r − p)) of its own E2K4 decode r, re-decoded, every row; E3 = S5b (1 level horizontal integer predict-only + closed-loop left DPCM on the coarse half + final pixel clamp). Operating points = sweep Qf nearest each target (actual bpp shown); dB vs the float baseline of structure_S interpolated at exactly 0.5/1.0/2.0 on the gen-1 sweep.

- Reading (source): E1/E2 exact where the clamp does not fire on a row whose result feeds later rows (dng720, dng1080 all rates; gfx 1.0/2.0); where it fires gen 2 differs and a single differing index propagates to every row below through the row predictor; gen 2→3 still moves. E2qK4 gen 2 = gen 3 = gen 1 on all four natural cells at all rates; cut24 19 luma / 2,617 at g2 then 0; extFULL 3 / 538 then 0; cost vs E2K4 −0.00…−0.02 dB natural, cut24 −0.2…−1.2 chroma, extFULL luma −0.43. E3 exact except dng720 @0.5/1.0 (1–3 luma idx, 12,675–12,738 px) and spot @1.0 (1 / 7,048), but −1.4…−5.4 dB. E1/E2 vs baseline natural: −0.39…−1.17 luma, −0.42…−1.43 chroma (dng, gfx); spot −0.46…−0.70 luma / −0.94…−1.89 chroma. DATA GEN 2 LACKS: none (every decision from reconstructed data + the Qf carried in the stream); gen-1 mismatches come from the clamp: gen 1 quantised the unclamped residual of the SOURCE, gen 2 the residual of the clamped decode; E2q removes exactly that by emitting the index of the decode.
- First reading's extFULL explanation (later refuted by the row-0 follow-up): the −62…−91 dB rows are "the 99-cap artefact plus a real chroma failure (row 0 predicted from 512 on a rail-valued plane; E3 at Qf 11 collapses)"; baseline codes both extFULL chroma planes exactly (99.00 cap, 0.45 bpp: 14.14/99.00/99.00) vs pipeline 14.99/37.56/37.56 (E1, 1.16 bpp), 14.57/37.56/37.56 (E2qK4), 9.11/3.01/3.01 (E3, 0.43 bpp); cut24 pipeline 17.29/20.12/20.12 (E1) vs baseline 17.89–18.13/16.03–16.06. "DATA GEN 2 LACKS: none" — every decision is computed from reconstructed data (steps from Qf and width; E2 cells from the emitted q).
- Ops/sample (estimates): E1 decoder up3 4 + dequantise/inverse 6.8 + add 1 + clamp 2 = 13.8; encoder + forward 6.8 + subtract 1 + quantise 3 = 24.6. E2 + per round forward 6.8 + cell clamp 4 + inverse 6.8 + add/clamp 3 = 20.6 at BOTH ends (K=2 +41, K=4 +82). E2qK4 decoder = E2K4 (96); encoder ~230. E3 12.5 (encoder ~2×). Baseline 12.3 + 12.3. Look-back 1 row, no row below.

### 5.4 Round SM — smudge test of the vertical-causal pipeline (S5.358; smudge_SM.txt; notes/temporal_sm.py, sm_eval.sh, sm_drift.py; smudge/)
Why the test was run (ledger S5.357): the coordinator had checked only the SEAM (row-phase mean |error|) and conflated it with the smudge; the specific risk of the vertical-causal structure named for the test: "errors carried row to row downward (E1 showed one index spreading through the slice) → level drift = smudge". Queued spec: temporal offline pipeline ≥ 16 frames (inter residual, staggered refresh), dng720 @0.5, dng1080 @0.5, spot @1.0; smudgegroups.py per plane on frames 8–15 vs the offline baseline and today's real decodes if present; level-map renders frames 8 and 12 per plane (SA11_plan/smudge/renders/).
Definition (binding): SMUDGE = grouping of red/blue in the LEVEL MAP (decode level minus source level per LL-support block), steady-state frames only (≥ 8), smudgegroups.py at the owner's S5.91 setting (thr 6 / dens 0.4) and the tool default (thr 12 / 0.5), --sh = slice height; renders with levelmap.py (--plane all, unmarked) and gridmap.py (unmarked + _grid). Nothing re-implemented.
Temporal offline coding: slices of sh rows (8 at 720p, 16 at 1080p); slice s intra on frame f when f = 0 or f mod 8 = s mod 8 (staggered period 8, as today); other slices code the residual vs the reconstructed previous frame, ZERO MOTION. Arms: vc = integer vertical-causal E2qK4 (intra row predictor (a + 2b + c + 2) >> 2, frame row 0: 512; inter row predictor = co-located reconstructed row of the previous frame; horizontal integer 5/3 × 5; clamp [0,1023]); base = float offline model of today's 2-D structure (struct_s.py), same slices/refresh/quantiser; codec = today's real decodes SA7_legality_v2/out/DM/*_a0.d.yuv (ptree5 base, cross-slice term on, gamut repair on, 12 frames, exact CBR). Frames: dng 12-frame sources (steady 8–11); spot 16 frames coded (steady 8–15; codec decode has 12 → 8–11). Rates (Qf sweep, power-of-two steps give coarse rate points): dng720 vc q6.5 all-frame 0.510 / steady 0.457 / f0 0.772; base q6.7 0.409 / 0.359 / 0.703; base q6.6 0.664 / 0.611 / 0.959; dng1080 vc q6.75 0.584 / 0.547 / 0.694; base q6.6 0.551 / 0.518 / 0.696; spot vc q5.5 0.862 / 0.854 / 0.718; base q5.5 0.843 / 0.824 / 0.917; codec 0.500 / 0.500 / 1.000.
- Steady-state PSNR Y/Cb/Cr: dng720 vc 35.34/35.40/36.51 (0.457) | base 35.28/35.78/36.96 (0.359) | base 36.51/36.07/37.22 (0.611) | codec 35.35/36.12/37.15 (0.5); dng1080 vc 34.91/33.93/35.11 (0.547) | base 35.67/34.49/35.69 (0.518) | codec 35.36/34.69/35.76 (0.5); spot vc 40.99/41.75/44.32 (0.854) | base 42.90/43.80/47.00 (0.824) | codec 42.23/44.82/48.18 (1.0) → vertical-causal CHROMA clearly worse on motion content.
- smudgegroups (groups/frame / blocks/frame, largest): thr 6/0.4: dng720 f8–11 vc Y 1,1,0,0 / 8,23,0,0 (largest 23), Cb 0, Cr 0; base(0.36) Y 6,4,4,6 / 78,69,64,104 (31); base(0.61) 0; codec Y 2,0,5,4 / 58,0,82,91 (57), Cb/Cr 0; dng1080 f8–11 all arms 0; spot vc 0, base 0; codec f8–11 Y 0,2,2,2 / 0,29,44,36 (28), Cb/Cr 0. thr 12/0.5: 0 everywhere except codec spot Y f10 1 group / 17 blocks, f11 1 / 8. Full output smudge/ev_<cell>_<arm>.txt; boxed PNGs smudge/sg/.
- LEVEL DRIFT (mean SIGNED error by row within the slice, steady frames; and by eighth of frame height): vc: a UNIFORM POSITIVE LEVEL OFFSET of +0.9…+1.2 codes on all three planes at every row phase (last − first row −0.11…+0.05) and every eighth of the height, already on frame 0 (dng720 f0 Y +0.9…+1.1, Cb +0.8…+1.1, Cr +0.8…+1.1; spot f0 +0.8…+1.1) and unchanged on frames 1, 8, 11. Mechanism (measured on one row): the intra row predictor (a + 2b + c + 2) >> 2 rounds half up (+0.125 code per row on average), the vertical chain carries it; the correcting residual stays inside the dead zone of the coarse band (coarse step 8 at Qf 6.5), so the level settles ~+1 code high; inter rows inherit it (zero-residual copy). No downward ramp within the slice. base: Y last row −0.45…−0.62 (dng720), −0.50 (dng1080 row 15), frame mean −0.1…−0.2. codec: Y first row +0.25…+0.36, last row −0.06…−0.40; chroma first row +0.38…+0.55 (the seam rows). The +1-code offset is BELOW smudgegroups' block threshold (6) and not a grouping → the tool does not report it; it is a global colour shift = the COLOUR-CAST class the owner caught once (+1-code cast from lifting rounding) → G6 disqualifier until fixed (sign-symmetric rounding).
- Renders (smudge/renders/, per plane): frames 8 and 11 (dng) and 8 and 12 (spot offline; codec spot 8 and 11): level maps ±20 codes, grid variants, |decode − source|; 720p ×2 copies.
- Render/arm details (smudge_SM.txt): gridmap grid = slice boundaries AND every 64 columns (tools used as given, nothing re-implemented); the codec arm = today's real decodes already in the tree (SA7_legality_v2/out/DM/*_a0.d.yuv), NOT encoded by SA11; 720p level maps also as _x2.png (nearest ×2) because a block row is 2 px at slice height 8, 4 px at ×2.
- Coordinator vs 3-of-4 (S5.358): seam ✓; legality ✓; exactness ≈ (E2qK1: all natural cells exact at g2 except 1 index on spot @1.0 which settles at g3; cost 3–7× today); smudges: passes the owner's tool on steady state (fewer groups than today's codec) but carries a uniform +1-code cast on all planes (fixable, disqualifying until fixed); compression (deferred) −0.5…−3.9 dB chroma on spot. SA11 idle. (Chronology: the owner had already started the first independent from-scratch designer SA12 at S5.355, between rounds S and V/E; SA13 and SA14 followed at S5.359/S5.360. No later ledger entry in S5.343–S5.388 runs further vertical-causal tests.)

## §6 SA12 "CLP-1" — canonical, legal-by-construction pyramid (Opus 5.5; 5 rounds, 2026-09-27/28; stopped at consolidation)

Brief (S5.355): design (not code) a full codec from scratch; read PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS in full; told OMC and HVBC are prior efforts that both FAILED and why (§1.7); may read the whole tree, change nothing outside `Subagents/SA12_design/`; small token-conservative code tests on the folder's footage; coordinator adversarially reviews each finished design (grepping its ideas against the records), it rethinks; if stuck, restart; loop until a design passes. Relayed: accomplish all constraints (clause-by-clause map); later S5.358: NEG primary; S5.363: goals 1–4 order.
All SA12 numbers are numpy MODELS: round 1 zeroth-order entropy per band (SA11 struct_s estimator); round 2 entropy; rounds 3–5 "coded" = entropy × a static-tANS overhead model (cost2.py). No codec build. Scripts `SA12_design/notes/` (hf.py, extrema.py, tp.py, art.py, grid.py, permit.py, castcheck.py, loss.py, fmtcheck.py, summ.py, base.py [wraps SA11's baseline model read-only], seq.py, evalseq.py, pitchseq.py, pitch2.py, below.py, cost.py, cost2.py, cfleq.py, anchor.py, render4.py, joincmp.py, intrastep.py; round-6 scripts phase.py ["Round-6 oracle (float, no legality): does starting each slice on a PREDICTED row remove the intra slice-boundary step?"] and rowmaps.py [row-resolution seam renders, coordinator round 6 item 3] — round 6 was never reported, outputs in out/v6/, out/eye_rows/). Results in `SA12_design/out/`.

### 6.0 Early path and the HVBC warning (ledger S5.355 notes)
- SA12's first experiment (notes/hf.py) was a Haar/S-transform pair pyramid with details predicted from the final lowpass — the non-overlapping-synthesis family of HVBC's block grid (CONSOLIDATED_FINDINGS §10/§13; SA10 PROPOSAL_v2 §2.3). The coordinator warned early: prove no block grid (level maps + boundary statistic, per plane, steady state, 0.5 bpp) and zero coding loss, or drop it; the real problem named: overlap/anti-aliasing AND acyclic legality together. Owner: "Don't tell it to stop explicitly…" → awareness only.
- Interim (out/summ1.txt): variant B (2/6 predictor, 2 vertical levels, independent slices) mean vs baseline −0.35/−0.11/−0.14 (0.5), −0.07/−0.06/−0.08 (1.0), +0.10/−0.00/−0.09 (2.0), worst spot −0.73 Y; oor 0; last-row/mean 1.06–1.09 vs base 1.12–1.24 (first row 1.05–1.10). Owner: HVBC too was more efficient than today but had artifacts → efficiency figures are NOT evidence without artifact checks; the coordinator had called it "promising" too early.

### 6.1 Round 1 — DESIGN.md (revision 1; ledger S5.361; md5 94a600fe…; notes/hf.py d034862d…; renders out/art9/)
**Design elements (with the reason given for each).**
- P1 Legality inside the synthesis, acyclic: every transmitted value clamped to the set that keeps its own outputs legal, computed only from final values; no pixel-domain clip (reason: the OMC clip destroyed information; repair, capping, δ-recovery, legaliser, lock all searched for it, S5.212–S5.214).
- P2 Canonical by causality: every decoder value a deterministic function of the coded stream prefix and the one reference frame; the current source never enters a decision; the encoder's only free choice is each coefficient's index, forced on a lattice point (reason: OMC's plan chosen from the source then "rediscovered" by a 50-pass lock).
- P3 Exact pair sums; the half-difference predicted from final neighbouring lowpass (2/10 form) — "the only family I found in which P1 stays acyclic across levels AND the coarse band is anti-aliased rather than decimated" (reason: update makes clamps cyclic, S5.349; predict-only −2.2…−3.7 dB, S5.212, S5.353).
- P4 No slice-boundary machinery: no mirror, blend, un-blend or deferred row; vertical predictor reads true context at the top (lowpass of the decoded rows above), extrapolates linearly at the bottom (reason: seam line = the mirrored last row; smudge = the level-shifting blend, S5.345–S5.348).
- P5 Every per-region parameter a continuous field interpolated from transmitted, canonically derived control values (reason: HVBC 16×16 hard gates; per-slice step jumps).
- Formats: Y′CbCr only; 4:2:0/4:2:2/4:4:4; 8/10/12-bit (16-bit: intermediate words B+9 bits); per-plane lo, hi in the header (full, limited, transport-legal e.g. 1…254 8-bit SDI, 4…1019 10-bit); illegal source samples clamped at the encoder input. Slices full width, sh = 16 (8 at 720p); last slice shorter (1080 = 67×16 + 8) with its own pyramid; NO padding (padding broke generation exactness on 1080-row frames). Exact CBR: slice k has exactly S_k bytes (header, tANS payload, fixed padding pattern), S_k static from pipe rate and sample count. Every decoder-visible decision TRANSMITTED (step-field control values, vectors, α, refresh flag); the encoder derives them canonically (encoder conformance rule).
  [Round-1 detail: BT.709/BT.2020 and SDR/PQ/HLG are code values to the codec (no transfer-function processing, so no colour error from it); σ from a 16-bit LFSR advanced once per pair and seeded per row by the stage and the absolute row index; the decoder's ×m (quarter-octave step mantissa) is shift-add, the encoder's ÷m uses 3 fixed reciprocal shift-add networks; Exp-Golomb k per band class; Lemma 3 boundary case: when an interior target rounded past hi′, "hi′ ∈ ((q − 7/16)Δ, qΔ)" so K gives the gen-1 index; a degenerate interval takes the first branch at every generation.]
- Pair map per 1-D stage with a sign σ ∈ {+1, −1} from a fixed pseudo-random sequence of (stage, absolute slice index, pair index, cross index): forward s = a + b, d' = (a − b − σ·(s mod 2))/2; inverse d = 2·d' + σ·(s mod 2), a = (s + d)/2, b = a − d — an exact bijection Z² ↔ Z²; lowpass grows one bit per stage (B+8 after 8 stages).
- Pyramid per slice and plane: nv = min(3, log2(rows)) vertical levels (3 for 16- and 8-row slices, 2 for 4-row 4:2:0 chroma), 5 horizontal levels total. 2-D level ℓ ≤ nv: H-split A → (Lc, dH); V-split Lc → (LL, dL); e = dH − P_H(Lc); V-split e → (HL, dE); transmitted LH = dL − p_V(LL), HL (minus temporal), HH = dE − p_V(HL). H-only levels: H = dH − P_H(Lc). Coarsest LL minus its temporal term.
- Spatial predictor (2/10 in sum form): p = (22·(s[i−1] − s[i+1]) + 3·(s[i+2] − s[i−2]) + 128) >> 8, exact on linear ramps, shift-add; horizontal linear extrapolation beyond frame edges; vertical: above the slice true data (the lowpass of the decoded rows above), below linear extrapolation, first slice extrapolates at the top too.
- Legal intervals: pixel boxes [lo, hi]; lowpass box = sum of children's boxes; e-boxes data-dependent; given final s, m = (s + σ·(s mod 2))/2, f = s − m: d'lo = max(la − m, f − ub), d'hi = min(ua − m, f − lb), never empty (Lemma 1). Decoder reconstructs d' = clamp(q·Δ + p, d'lo, d'hi); coarsest LL clamp(q·Δ + p, box). Synthesis order coarsest first; within a 2-D level LL final ⇒ LH interval ⇒ clamp LH ⇒ merge to Lc; Lc final ⇒ horizontal intervals ⇒ e-boxes; HL box = pair-sum of e-boxes ⇒ clamp HL; HH interval ⇒ clamp HH ⇒ merge to e; dH = e + P_H(Lc) in its interval by construction. No cycle, iteration or search.
- Temporal: prediction of each band = same band of the forward pyramid of the MC reference, as a residual of the spatial prediction (LH: p = p_V(LL) + α·(dL_ref − p_V(LL_ref)); HL: α·HL_ref; HH: p_V(HL) + α·(dE_ref − p_V(HL_ref)); H: α·e_ref; LL: α·LL_ref). MC reference in the pixel domain from the decoded previous frame; vectors transmitted per block (16×16 coarse levels, 8×8 level 1); half-pel bilinear; OBMC with linear tapers; chroma uses scaled luma vectors with bilinear interpolation (the probe's floored chroma vectors cost ≈ 1 dB chroma). α per block in eighths as a continuous bilinear field; α = 0 is intra. Clamp unchanged.
- Pair rounding variants measured on the way (§2.6): floor mean (S-transform) −¼-code bias per stage where details are dead-zoned = colour cast (−1.3/−1.4 codes Cb/Cr at 0.19 bpp); checkerboard parity → periodic pattern (PER up to 3.8); per-stage alternating parity → rate-dependent residual cast (+0.5 code); exact sums with the extra unit always to the first sample → consistent-phase Nyquist pattern (PER up to 18); **exact sums + pseudo-random unit (design)** → cast ≤ 0.06 code, PER at or below today's. Hardware: 16-bit LFSR advanced once per pair, seeded per row. Encoder rounding offset ½ (not 7/16) on the coarsest DC band (avoids the dead zone's −Δ/16 darkening of an always-positive band).
- Step fields: Δ = m·2^e, m ∈ {4,5,6,7}/4 (quarter octaves); per-format band-class offset o_b; control value bilinear between the previous slice's bottom control points and this slice's, control points every 256 columns, evaluated at the coefficient's vertical centre → adjacent slices meet with identical steps.
- Entropy: static tANS, normative tables per band class; 8 contexts (quantised magnitudes of left and above indices + parent); escape at |q| ≥ 15 Exp-Golomb; signs raw; coarse levels first, band-sequential, level 1 interleaved per 256-column block; four interleaved tANS lanes, fixed chunks, no length fields.
- Refresh: rolling intra, slices with (t − k·N_f/N_s) mod N_f = 0 coded α = 0; barrier (refreshed slices' vectors reference only refreshed rows). Loss: concealed from the MC reference with slice-above vectors.
- Output: one legal picture at display geometry for both hops; optional 2×/4× conversion a separate stage (integer polyphase, ≤ 4-tap vertical), outside the exactness domain.
- Encoder: forward pairs as rows arrive; closed-loop quantisation coarse to fine, one pass: interval [lo′, hi′] = [d′lo − p, d′hi − p]; target t = source half-difference − p; r = hi′ if t ≥ hi′, lo′ if t ≤ lo′, else q0 = choice(t), r = clamp(q0·Δ, lo′, hi′); index q = K(r) = ceil(hi′/Δ) if r ≥ hi′, floor(lo′/Δ) if r ≤ lo′, else r/Δ. Lemma 3 (indices idempotent). An earlier draft reconstructed out-of-interval targets at the nearest lattice point and broke gen 2 on graphics — fixed and re-verified. Encoder freedoms on off-lattice targets: dead-zone 7/16 details, ½ DC; ECSQ two-candidate (CLG 1989, cleared); texture-energy term; temporal dead zone for still areas.
- Rate control (round 1): inputs S_k, the previous frame's record for slice k (bytes per level group and control values), bytes spent, slice-above control values; start from the record via log-linear LUT limited to ±4 quarter-octaves from the slice above; after each level group a LUT function of (bytes so far − planned); **Guard**: when the remaining budget cannot hold the model's minimum, remaining level-1 blocks take the coarsest control value and as a last resort are coded EMPTY (a flat strip — the forbidden "rung"; to be measured zero, T4); fixed padding.
  [Level groups (§3.5): "LL + levels 5–3, then level 2, then each 256-column level-1 block"; after each group the next control offset = LUT(bytes so far − planned bytes so far).]
- Canonical motion vectors and α (encoder-side, transmitted): from the current slice's decoded coarse bands, decoded t−1 and t−2 (encoder keeps two, decoder one), previous-frame and slice-above transmitted vectors; candidates zero, co-located previous, slice-above, t−1/t−2 block matching projected; SAD on the decoded level-2 lowpass, refine ±1 at level 2, at level 1 ±1 + 8 half-pel on the decoded level-1 lowpass, ties by (|v|₁, raster); α = LUT of (best SAD ÷ block activity). No cut detector; frame 0 intra, frame 1 inter, both fit S_k.
- Proofs: Lemma 1 (non-empty interval), Theorem 1 (every decoded sample in [lo, hi] for any bitstream; `dint` raises on an empty interval — never raised), Lemma 2 (analysis recovers synthesis), Theorem 2 (E(y) = E(x) byte for byte from a common start). Not covered: re-encoding at a different rate; a downstream encoder starting mid-stream (first frame intra where upstream was inter; "mid-sequence baseband start exact only from g3", S5.236) — open R7, candidate cure (untested) anchor refresh phase to hard cuts.
**Measurements (round 1).**
- Checks (§4.3): 216/216 synthetic rail cases (8/10/12-bit; full/limited/SDI; 8- and 16-row; Qf up to 12, steps ≫ window): 0 oob, decoder = encoder, gen-2 identity (notes/extrema.py); cf_gfx 4 rates × 3 planes all hold; dng 444/12 full range Qf 6 and 9: 0 / True / True; dng 422/8 SDI 1…254 Qf 2 and 5: same; dng720 422/10 limited 64…940: same (3,916 and 7,204 clamp-site index changes on luma); temporal probe (in-band MC, oracle and derived vectors, three motion cells, floor-pair variant) gen-2 identity incl. re-derived vectors holds; 1080-row frames with padding: gen-2 FAILS (why padding is banned), passes with the short last slice.

At Qf 5 (~1 bpp) α = 1 is worse than intra on floorball (1.20 vs 1.03 bpp) — reference noise in fine bands → α must be a field. Canonical vectors recover ~⅓–½ of the oracle saving and lose ≈ 0.4 dB luma. No NEG for the probe; the probe's decodes were not written out. What the probe lacked vs the design (§8.2): "The design rule (§3.6) adds history candidates, half-pel, overlapped blocks, per-block α, and correct chroma interpolation — expected to close part of the gap; not measured." A1 expectation (§8.3): the intra core at parity or slightly better than today's structure; removing OMC's lock, repair, fill and blend may return some rate, amount unknown; "Expected: roughly today's equivalence ratio (≈ 1.3–1.5× VMAF-NEG-equivalent), not 2×"; levers, each with a decisive test: (1) canonical motion with history candidates; (2) a per-band α field; (3) band weights tuned by eye (PSNR diagnostic only under rev. 6), chroma especially; (4) texture-energy index choice; (5) a 4th vertical level at 16-row slices — "None of these is a known route to 2×." Step allocation left untuned in §8.1 because "both structures use the same power-of-two step rule" (round 2 traced the chroma-flatness regression to this rule).
