- Work derivation detail: canonical motion ≈ 14 ops per luma sample = 45 candidates × 2 ops on N_Y/16 + 17 × 2 on N_Y/4; LUTs ≈ 25 per pipelined op × 2.5 for control ≈ 53 k, + ≈ 10 tANS lanes (15 k) + DDR/IO (15 k); "Every stage runs once per slice. There is no re-decision, lock, legaliser or search pass."
- Work (§7.1, closed formula): decoder per sample entropy 6 + dequantise/clamp 7 + interval 8 + spatial prediction 7 + temporal 11 + pair merge 4 + MC fetch/OBMC/half-pel/reference pyramid 10 = 53 → D ≈ 53·N; encoder decoder path 53 + forward 4 + quantise 10 + rate LUT 2 + ECSQ 6 per sample + canonical motion ≈ 14 + reference box pyramid 4 + t−1/t−2 field 3 per luma sample → E ≈ 75·N + 21·N_Y ≈ 86 ops/sample at 4:2:2. JPEG XS estimate (not measured) ≈ 25 encode / 18 decode → ≈ 3.4× / 2.9× XS. ZU7EV (§7.2): 8K60 4:2:2 3.98 G samples/s → ≈ 342 G ops/s ≈ 855 ops/clock at 400 MHz; ≈ 53 k LUTs + tANS 15 k + DDR/IO 15 k ≈ 83 k of 230 k (≈ 36 %), on DSP48E2 ALUs ≈ 25 %; 4K60 ≈ 9–10 %, 1080p60 ≈ 3 %; decoder ≈ 60 % of encoder; memory ≈ 20 Mbit on-chip at 8K, 1 frame DDR at decoder, 2 at encoder ("memory not a constraint" per the then owner ruling); margin at 8K60 "real but not large" (R5). Latency (§7.3): L = sh capture + sh transmission + 1 first-byte + 1 last-block synthesis + ≤ 4 conversion: 720p50 sh 8 18 lines 0.48 ms / 0.59 ms with conversion; 720p60 0.40 / 0.49; 1080p50 sh 16 34 lines 0.60 / 0.68; 1080p60 0.50 / 0.56; 2160p50 0.30 / 0.34; 4320p60 0.13 / 0.14 (OMC 34 lines at 1080p, XS ≈ 32); encoder must run ≥ 1.2× pixel rate. Word widths: coarsest band B+8 bits (18 at 10-bit, 20 at 12, 24 at 16); intervals/predictions ≤ B+9.
- Encoder timing and cuts (DESIGN.md §3.6–3.7, §7.3): "the encoder must run at ≥ 1.2× the pixel rate so its bytes stay ahead of the channel. The canonical level-2 and level-1 searches must finish before their bands' bytes are due." "No cut detector is needed. At a cut the SAD grows, α → 0 follows, and the rate feedback adapts from the record within the slice."
- Decisive tests as defined in §9: T1 = 24-frame model with canonical motion + α, steady-state f8–15, NEG vs XS@2R per plane, 4 motion cells + dng + gfx, 4:2:2 and 4:4:4; T2 = the owner's eye on out/art9 (if visible: bottom context from the MC reference's rows below in inter slices, canonical and legal, then row phase); T3 = chroma band weights + texture-energy index choice, flatplane/texstat/smudgegroups per plane + renders; T4 = count guard-terminal events over the corpus + cut24 + extrema at 0.3–2 bpp, bar 0; T5 = 24-frame still-area test, frame-difference maps, refresh slices at a higher budget share; T6 = RTL-level estimate of the closed-loop datapath and SAD array; T7 = eye at 8-bit on smooth gradients (alternative σ from the local predicted slope sign, hash only where the slope is 0); T8 = refresh phase anchored to hard cuts, measure convergence after a join.
- Own risk list (§9): R0 A1 not reachable (T1); R1 last-row excess 1.05–1.23 (T2, eye; if visible: bottom context from the MC reference in inter slices); R2 chroma flatness 6/16 (T3); R3 refresh "twinkle" in still areas (T5); R4 guard terminal flat strip fires (T4, bar 0); R5 8K60 encoder 25–36 % logic (T6); R6 σ pseudo-random half-code split a fixed ±½-code pattern in odd-sum areas (T7, eye at 8-bit; alternative σ from the local predicted slope sign); R7 mid-stream join not exact (T8); R8 IP of canonical motion and vertical context. IP provenance table (§10): pair map = integer Haar/S-transform family (Blume & Fand 1989); σ LFSR own work; detail predicted from lowpass (S+P Said & Pearlman 1996; TS/CREW Zandi et al. 1995; Calderbank–Daubechies–Sweldens–Yeo 1998; Adams & Kossentini 2000), not the 5/3 or 9/7; interval clamp own work; closed-loop quantisation Cutler DPCM 1952; dead zone, ECSQ CLG 1989 cleared; static tANS (Duda) allowed; contexts 1990s; block matching Jain & Jain 1981; OBMC Orchard & Sullivan 1994 / H.263 Annex F 1996; hybrid MC per subband 1980s; rolling refresh H.261-era 1990; buffer-feedback rate control 1980s; encoder-side motion Netravali–Robbins 1979 (vectors transmitted, no decoder-side derivation). Not used: 5/3, 9/7, GCLI, bit-plane (JPEG XS); CABAC; rANS; decoder-side motion derivation; fill. Prior failures checked (§11): predict-only (has an update: the pair sum is a Haar update); one-sided update (pair-local update clamps the pair via Lemma 1, acyclic at lag 0); causal vertical (retested −1.0…−1.5 dB + 4-row pattern, rejected); continuous vertical transform (not used); mirror/XSL/blend (none); capping/repair/δ/legaliser/lock/R1 (nothing destroyed); ECR bit-plane (not used); escalation ladder (not used); odd-dyadic points (not used; plan transmitted); lattice lock (not used); decoder-derived vectors (transmitted); per-region vectors without fallback (zero and previous candidates always present); half-pel variants (bilinear only); fill (none); rounding cast (removed at root); per-block mode switching (α a continuous field); padding at 1080 (short last slice); HVBC grid (measured per plane).
- Constraint map status (§6): A1 NOT MET/not shown (expected ≈ today's 1.33×; weakest point); A1 pipe met by construction (guard must measure zero); A2 met (model), 34 lines at 1080p vs XS ≈ 32; A3 R1/R2/R3 eye pending; A4 met from a common start, mid-stream open (R7); A5 met (design + probe); B1–B4 met, 8K120 not budgeted; C1 subject to legal review; C2 met; C3 met (model), 8K60 encoder 25–36 % logic; C4 met; C5 met; C8 to be written; G1–G6 eye pending.
**Coordinator review round 1 (S5.361, sent):** holds — legality/exactness by construction, horizontal grid, intra NEG, loss, latency; fails/unproven — (1) guard terminal flat strip = rung (disqualifier, must be impossible by construction); (2) chroma flatness regression 6/16 (disqualifier); (3) residual seam v16 1.03–1.50; (4) all artifact evidence intra-only (smudges/ants/twinkle on steady-state inter frames and a repeated-frame still input required); (5) mid-stream join exactness (rate state from the previous frame's record; hops do not know the frame index, S5.235); (6) A1 route + comparison vs today's REAL decodes and XS@2R; (7) 8K margin; (8) grep records on canonical motion and refresh-barrier drift. ### 6.2 Round 2 — DESIGN_v2.md (ledger S5.364; md5 ac8b6a61…)
New evidence: sequence model notes/seq.py (final legal pyramid + temporal prediction from one reference + canonical transmitted vectors + rolling refresh), steady-state INTER frames 2–11 vs today's REAL decodes (SA7 out/DM/*_a0.d.yuv, exact CBR) and JPEG XS (SVT build Agents/Agent3/tools/svtxs/Bin/Release, `--quantization 1 --rc 2 --slice-height 32`). Caveats: entropy rates; fixed step per sequence (not CBR packets); no half-pel, no OBMC, no α field, no refresh barrier.
- **Temporal-predictor DEFECT found and fixed:** revision 1's temporal predictor on a truly still input (dng720 f0 ×12) changed 73–84 % of samples every frame (as bad as today, 60–77 %). Cause: the temporal term subtracted a spatial prediction computed with extrapolation for the reference but with decoded-row context for the current slice → a zero residual did not reproduce the reference. Fix (normative): the reference's spatial prediction uses the reference's own rows above (seq.py R['ctx']).
- Still input after the fix (out/v2/seq/fix_still_q6.*): 0.000 % Y/Cb/Cr changed every pair f1→f11 incl. through refresh slices (1 in 8 slices refreshed per frame); ants 880/99/390 qualifying blocks, tail 0.00 %, boil 0.000 every plane (source 0.00); inter slices ≈ 0 bits, 0.146 bpp per frame = the refresh slices; gen 2 picture, indices and vectors identical. Why refresh does not twinkle: with identical steps a still region's inter reconstruction never leaves the intra lattice; intra re-coding of the same source with the same steps and context reproduces the same picture; condition: steps must not change between refreshes (made canonical by reading the step from the picture with a tie rule).
- dng720 (8-row; source changes 96 % of samples per frame, mean |Δ| 10–14), steady f2–11 — bpp, NEG, PSNR mean, PSNR worst, flat % f8, COR f8, ants tail Y/Cb (source 19.9/24.8), smudge f8: today real 0.500 (CBR), 90.16, 35.22/36.10/37.14, 34.69/35.86/36.98, 41.9/82.8/83.3, .865/.290/.358, 10.3/9.9, 0/0/0; CLP Qf 6 0.464 (max 0.62), 91.70, 37.46/36.38/37.55, 37.36/36.35/37.54, 37.0/55.9/70.8, .922/.355/.425, 4.7/1.6, 0/0/0; CLP Qf 6.5 0.422 (max 0.57), 90.59, 36.82/36.30/37.45, 36.71/36.27/37.44, 36.7/57.3/70.0, .908/.347/.415, 3.3/2.2, 0/0/0. XS on the same 12 frames 72.11 @0.5, 89.31 @1.0. Only 5 flat-and-static blocks qualify for ants (weak). smudgegroups finds 0 groups on today's real decode too → not discriminating on these cells; smudge freedom argued from mechanism.
- spotrobotL (16-row), steady f2–11 — bpp, NEG, PSNR mean, PSNR worst Y, flat f8, COR f8, ants tail Y/Cb/Cr (source 26.5/22.1/–): today real 0.500, 93.76, 39.54/42.80/46.31, 36.51, 48.0/65.1/69.0, .849/.508/.442, 5.2/6.7/4.5; CLP Qf 6 0.383 (max 0.47), 92.93, 39.55/41.84/45.17, 39.29, 37.2/53.2/29.5, .775/.407/.318, 3.6/2.8/3.0; CLP Qf 5.5 0.800, 96.66, 42.60/43.63/46.96, 42.38; CLP at 0.5 interpolated in log₂ bpp ≈ 94.3, ≈ 40.6/42.5/45.8. XS 87.73 @0.5, 96.64 @1.0. SA12's reading: NEG equal or better than today at the same or fewer bits on both cells; luma better, worst frame much better (spot worst-frame luma 39.3 at 0.38 bpp vs today 36.5 at 0.5); chroma PSNR dng720 better, spot ≈ −0.3/−0.5 dB Cb/Cr at 0.5 (interpolated) — "the chroma allocation"; chroma flatness and COR better on dng720; on spot flatness better while COR is lower at 38 % fewer bits; nothing yet at exact CBR with real tANS and headers (T1). Refresh twinkle on moving content was NOT measured separately: argued from the still-input result plus the steady-state rows (spot worst frame 0.3 dB below its mean vs today's 3 dB).
- Seams: (a) last-row excess (intra, spot/hwy, Qf 5 and 6; out/v2/below_*.log), last-row ÷ slice mean: linear extrapolation 1.07–1.20; oracle (true rows below) 0.89–1.02; proxy (co-located rows of the previous frame) 1.02–1.07 → cause = missing context below; CHANGE (normative): in inter slices the two samples below come from the lowpass of the MC reference's rows below; intra keeps extrapolation; inter dng720 (below-context not yet in seq.py) last-row 1.05–1.08 vs today 1.08–1.20. (b) Error step across the boundary (mean |e(y) − e(y−1)| at rows ≡ 0 mod 16 vs all other rows; inter f2–11; notes/pitchseq.py): spot CLP boundary 1.18/1.28/1.47, internal coarse-pair boundary (p16+8) 0.97/1.09/1.15; today 1.16/1.27/1.53 and 1.00/1.12/1.21; XS pitch16 = pitch32 = 1.02/1.12/1.22 (the dyadic floor of any wavelet codec). dng720 inter v8: CLP 1.06/1.04/1.04 vs today 1.04/1.03/1.04. Excess over internal: CLP +0.21/+0.19/+0.32, today +0.16/+0.15/+0.32 → CLP removes the mirror/blend mechanism but keeps a step of today's size. Cause by elimination (round-2 hypothesis): oracle below-context does not remove it; finer coarse steps reduce it only at large cost (Y 1.18 → 0.95, Cr 1.47 → 1.26 at 2.3× rate, ll_k5c2.log); "each slice's coarsest band quantised independently" [later FALSIFIED in round 3]. Proposed root remedy (unmeasured): leaky cross-slice prediction of the coarsest band (weight ½ cross-slice, ½ co-located temporal/zero; a lost slice decays ×½ per slice), or an encoder-only boundary-continuity term in the two-candidate choice.
- Chroma flatness (out/v2/cfl/; HF_CL1 = 0.5 chroma level-1 step halved; intra f8), flat % and COR Cb/Cr, today / CLP r1 / CLP ½ step / rate cost vs today: dng 0.5 82.9/85.7 COR .241/.296 / 89.9/88.2 / 19.1/50.4 COR .526/.491 / +27 %; hwy 1.0 58.7/45.1 / 60.9/49.9 [RESOLVED from the files: 60.9 is the Cb flat % of the EARLIER B7 variant's battery (out/art7/battery7_table.txt: hwy1.0 B7 flat 23.57/60.90/49.07); the round-1 FINAL battery (out/art9, hwy1.0 final flat 24.51/51.23/49.86) gives 51.2 — DESIGN_v2's "CLP revision 1" column quotes B7's Cb figure] / 44.3/8.5 COR .632/.740 / +10 %; hwy 0.5 79.6/82.5 / 69.3/84.4 / 67.6/65.6 / +7 %; floor 1.0 41.8/46.7 / 42.2/48.6 / 7.0/9.2 / +21 %. Cause: the step rule (synthesis-energy MSE weights) starves chroma level-1 in both structures — an allocation choice, not the pair highpass or σ. On inter frames CLP chroma flatness already below today's. Caveat (v2): "The rate cost is only indicative: the model's power-of-two steps move in octaves, so the rate bisection could not land on target"; the trade (rate vs luma NEG) was to be decided at matched real CBR (T1) without breaking the no-more-bits guard (the coordinator rejected the fix on these +7/+10/+21/+27 % figures). Change: o_b set by eye and flatness instruments, chroma level-1 one octave finer than MSE (−4 quarter-octaves) to start.
- CBR guard rewritten: "coded empty" terminal removed; coarse groups (levels ≥ 2, LL) use step control p chosen by open-loop cost lanes (parallel LUT sums); level 1 quantised closed-loop in parallel lanes δ ∈ {0, +2, +4, +6, +8, +12} quarter-octaves (all lanes share intervals and predictions → each lane's cost exact), finest fitting lane emitted (the lanes are "the same kind of computation as XS's own budget tables"); δ a control point of the continuous field; second pass if the coarse groups overrun their share beyond what the coarsest lane absorbs (re-coded once at p + LUT(overrun); worst case one extra coarse pass ≈ ¼ of samples; count = T4). Stated plainly: for any fixed-rate codec some inputs exceed the pipe (e.g. independent 12-bit noise at 0.5 bpp); guarantees: no discrete fallback mode, any loss is the same continuous field, bounded work; coarsest lane never needed on real content at ≥ 0.5 bpp is measured, not constructed.
- Mid-stream join (design): "The first encoder may use anything to choose. Every decoder-visible parameter it emits must be readable from the decoded picture, and the emitted value is the one the reading rule returns on the encoder's own reconstruction." Steps p, δ: coarsest-reproducing read (among values within one octave of the slice above, the coarsest for which every non-boundary coefficient lies on its lattice; boundary values detectable; divisibility by m·2^e via ctz + residues mod 3/5/7 LUT → mask → AND → max, one pass; revision 1's nested-lattice argument shows that emitting the read value reproduces the reconstruction exactly and never costs more bits); tie for all-zero residual slices: keep the slice-above value; slice 0 fixed default range; no state across frames. Intra/inter: description reproducing the slice with fewest bits, ties → intra (makes refresh slices detectable → refresh phase). Vectors and α from decoded pictures only (t−1, t−2, current slice's decoded coarse bands); previous-frame vectors only as a candidate, reset in refreshed slices; window restricted to the refreshed region during a cycle. Convergence argued within one refresh cycle + 2 frames (not measured, T8).
- XS anchors (12 frames incl. frames 0–1): dng720 XS 72.11/89.31/94.84 at 0.5/1.0/2.0, today real 88.86 @0.5, 93.83 @1.0; spot XS 87.73/96.64/98.84, today real 93.47 @0.5, 97.55 @1.0. (These "today" figures are over 12 frames incl. 0–1; the steady-state f2–11 figures above are 90.16 and 93.76.)
- 8K60 (design accounting): DSP48E2 ALU mode (TWO24/FOUR12) 1,728 DSPs ≥ 3,456 adds/clock; encoder ≈ 855 ops/clock at 400 MHz ≈ 25 % of DSP adder capacity; LUTs ≈ 45–55 k of 230 k (≈ 20–24 %). Canonical motion hierarchical: ±2 at quarter resolution (25 candidates on N_Y/16) then ±1 full (9): ≈ 21 ops per luma sample; reading rule ≈ 6 ops/sample. 4K60 ≈ 6 % DSPs / 7 % LUTs; 1080p < 2 %; 8K120 ≈ ½ of the part. (The model's ±8 full-res 289-candidate search is a model convenience.)
- Records checked: decoder-derived motion (S5.29) — CLP transmits vectors, the encoder rule's only cross-frame state is reset by refresh; canonical motion weaker than source search ("the vector's resolution is the information", S5.29 Design C), measured 1/3–1/2 of the oracle gain; refresh-barrier drift (XSL.md) — CLP's cross-slice term is the context rows above; top-down refresh guarantees clean context; loss probe 0 from slice +3.
- Exactness after the changes: 0 oob every run; gen 2 identical (picture, every index, every derived vector) on still input (12 frames through refresh), graphics sequence (6 frames), real moving dng720 (6 frames; fix_d720_g2).
- Next tests planned (v2 §4): T0 seam root remedy — leaky cross-slice prediction of the coarsest band, target "the p16+0 step equal to the internal p16+8 step, every plane, on inter frames", plus the encoder-only boundary-continuity index choice, renders; T1 exact-CBR sequence (real tANS, headers, guard lanes) vs today real at 0.5/1.0, 4 motion cells + dng + gfx, 4:2:2 and 4:4:4, NEG first every plane, chroma offset by eye; T4 guard coarsest-lane / second-pass counts on the corpus, cut24 and extrema at 0.3–2 bpp; T8 "a hop starting at frame 5 of a gen-1 decode reaches byte identity within one refresh cycle + 2 frames"; T9 multi-frame loss/recovery with the top-down refresh and barrier. Information-limit statement for the guard: "JPEG XS loses bit-planes the same way"; intra and inter are evaluated in the same pass with a divisibility check.
**Coordinator review round 2 (S5.364, sent):** holds — still-area flicker solved by design, g2 identity incl. derived vectors, legality, NEG above today's real decodes on dng720 at fewer bits; fails/open — (1) seam step = today's (priority #1; pointed to S5.362 bracketed slices as a record); (2) chroma-flatness fix +7–27 % bits violates the no-noticeable-increase guard; (3) entropy vs real bits unfair → add tANS/packing overhead; (4) smudge test must use the owner's marked material (S3.12, S3.19 87 boxes, S3.24 grille boxes, 720p cell); (5) mid-stream join T8 unmeasured.

### 6.3 Round 3 — DESIGN_v3.md (ledger S5.365 SA12 paragraph; md5 e681d26e…)
Evidence: seq.py with OBMC; steady-state inter f2–11; owner's instruments; vs today real; rates = entropy × coder-overhead factor (§3 of v3).
- DESIGN_v3 §3 1.0-bpp line: "dng720 at 1.0 bpp: CLP at Qf 5 is 1.72 bpp (NEG 95.28), against today at 1.0 (NEG 93.83). The 1.0-bpp point was not bracketed; test T1." Window note: 93.83 is today's 12-frame figure (frames 0–1 included; DESIGN_v2 XS-anchor table "12 frames, including frames 0–1"), while the 0.5 row of the same v3 table uses the f2–11 figure (90.16); today's f2–11 NEG at 1.0 is 94.05 (SA14 today_runs / DESIGN_v2) — mixed windows, as with the v2 XS anchors.
- **Seam cause located by elimination** (statistic: mean |e(y) − e(y−1)| at the slice boundary and at the internal half-height boundary, each relative to all other rows; excess = boundary − internal; JPEG XS ≈ 0). Intra spot f8 (out/v2/band/, out/v3/tall.log): any coarse band lossless (LL, levels 5, 4, 3, level-3 LH) +0.09…+0.10 in every case → round-2 diagnosis FALSIFIED; oracle rows below: does not remove it; **one tall slice (no split) +0.01 (Y and chroma) → the whole step is the causal slice split**; 16-row slices +0.16/+0.18. Interpretation: inside a slice a coarse sample's error spreads over pairs on both sides through the detail predictors; at a boundary the bottom of slice k−1 was finalised before slice k existed, so the spread is cut (a vertical block-boundary effect).
- Inter frames had a second, model-caused component: plain 16×16 motion blocks (the design specifies OBMC) made a block grid in both directions (horizontal 16-pitch excess +0.162 Y) — a G1-type artifact of the model.
- Inter f2–11 spotrobotL (16-row), coded bpp / PSNR / slice-boundary excess Y/Cb/Cr / horizontal 16-pitch excess Y: today real 0.50 / 39.54/42.80/46.31 / +0.15/+0.14/+0.30 / −0.01; model plain blocks (r2) 0.44 / 39.56/41.89/45.24 / +0.18/+0.16/+0.26 / +0.162; + MC context below 0.44 / — / +0.18/+0.16/+0.26; + context above also from the reference 0.41 / 40.00/42.19/45.63 / +0.10/+0.09/+0.17 (worse than OBMC alone, dropped); **OBMC (design) 0.405 / 40.05/42.20/45.64 / +0.07/+0.06/+0.11 / −0.020**; **OBMC + encoder error continuation 0.407 / 39.98/42.13/45.50 / +0.04/+0.03/+0.06**. dng720 (8-row) OBMC +0.05/+0.05/+0.04 vs today +0.05/+0.04/+0.05 (0.5) and +0.05/+0.06/+0.06 (1.0).
- New encoder element — error continuation (encoder-only): the encoder adds the previous slice's low-frequency reconstruction error (mean of its last 2 rows, box-smoothed over 16 columns, fading linearly over 8 rows) to the target of this slice's top rows; cost −0.07/−0.08/−0.14 dB PSNR, no bits; exactness untouched (only changes which lattice point is picked; at gen 2 the previous error is 0); gen-2 identity checked with it on (final: OBMC + continuation + MC below, out/v3/final_g2) and without OBMC (d720c8).
- Remaining: inter +0.04/+0.03/+0.06 on spot (today +0.15/+0.14/+0.30); intra slices +0.14 luma (frame 0, refresh slices, cuts) — neither below-context nor continuation applied there (would make refresh depend on the reference).
- Bracketed slices (SA13, S5.362) judged: attacks the right cause (a shared decoded anchor row); costs: legality (SA13's interior 5/3 with update not legal by construction here; a legal version = anchor as known sample, last pair's detail derived, pair-sum box anchor + [lo, hi], acyclic, interior coding gain unmeasured) and rate (a 1-D coded row per slice). Not built; next test T0 on intra slices only. A5: bracketing uses one anchor row of the slice above → damage stops after one slice.
- **Chroma flatness at no more bits:** reallocating within chroma cannot pay for finer level-1 steps (chroma coarse bands hold too few bits; out/v3/cfleq_*.log). Reshape: chroma level-1 step ×√2 (normative band offset) + encoder rounding offset 11/16 (encoder-only dead-zone, lattice-safe) (out/v3/cfth_*.log); luma untouched. dng 0.5: today(model) 0.522 NEG 83.69 PSNR 34.71/34.22/35.47 flat 60.8/82.9/85.7 COR .241/.296; CLP r2 0.479 84.35 34.65/34.24/35.47 59.3/89.9/88.2 .198/.275; **reshape 0.505 84.35 34.65/33.73/35.02 59.3/65.7/80.9 .280/.316**. hwy 1.0: today(model) 0.942 92.84 44.16/49.01/45.78 24.5/58.7/45.1 .531/.601; r2 0.852 93.41 44.06/48.96/45.68 24.3/57.0/50.3 .521/.575; **reshape 0.883 93.41 44.06/48.40/44.50 24.3/56.2/40.1 .524/.590**. Price: chroma PSNR −0.5 Cb / −0.45 Cr on dng, −1.3 dB Cr on hwy. Scope: intra only (ramp, cut, refresh slices); off on inter until measured.
- **Coder fairness** (notes/cost2.py; normative family of 64 tables per band (P(0) × geometric decay), encoder picks one per band per slice for 6 bits, no contexts (conservative), Exp-Golomb escapes, slice header 8 bytes + 4 lane flushes × 2 bytes; tANS within ≈ 0.5 % of ideal): dng720 Qf 6 entropy 0.450 × table mismatch 1.059 × total 1.134 → 0.510; Qf 6.5 0.409 × 1.077 → 1.159 → 0.474; Qf 5 1.648 × 1.022 → 1.043 → 1.718; spot Qf 6 0.352 × 1.119 → 1.151 → 0.405; Qf 5.5 0.736 × 1.054 → 1.070 → 0.787.

**SA12 successes / failures, and what caused each (from the records above).**
- Legality + exactness by construction ← per-pair interval clamp from final values in an exact-sum pair pyramid (Lemma 1/Theorem 1/2); 216/216 rails g2.
- Still input 0.000 % change ← temporal term using the reference's own spatial prediction (defect fix) + identical steps (readable) so inter and intra refresh share one lattice.
- Cast ≤ 0.06 code, PER at/below today ← exact sums + pseudo-random σ + DC offset ½ (each earlier rounding produced a cast or a pattern).
- Inter seam +0.04/+0.03/+0.06 ← OBMC (removed a model-made 16×16 grid) + encoder error continuation.
- Intra seam ≈ today ← the causal slice cut (tall slice +0.01); not removable by below-context, anchor bracketing, 8-row slices, continuation; the only full remedy (crossing transform) breaks latency parity/A5.
- Join byte-exact from frame 15 ← contiguous sweep + clean-region rules + wrap rule + gen-1 self-read (interleaved refresh failed).
- Efficiency at/above today on dng720, spot, hwy (coded-rate ESTIMATES; the hwy point needed cell-specific ×0.6 step multipliers) — never at real code lengths.
- Chroma flatness regression ← MSE step allocation starving chroma level 1; reshape fixed it at a chroma PSNR price (intra only).

Method (script header): float, NO legality; separable 5/3 lifting per 16/8-row slice, 3 vertical × 5 horizontal levels (V1,H1,V2,H2,V3,H3,H4,H5); sequential closed loop, context = the previous slice's DECODED rows at each level; dead-zone 9/16, steps from synthesis weights (LL ×½ per the script), zeroth-order entropy per band; Qf 6; one intra frame. Statistic "excess" = step at the slice boundary minus step at the half-slice, relative to the other rows. Arms: 'std' = rows split even/odd from row 0 (row 0 = low), predict of the last odd row one-sided (extrapolated), update of the first low row reads the previous slice's last detail at that level; 'shift' = row 0 PREDICTED (odd) from the previous slice's final low row (context) and row 1, update of the LAST low row one-sided (= SA14's shifted causal slice). ## §7 SA13 "BSC-1" — Bracketed-Slice Codec (Opus 5.5; 2 rounds, 2026-09-27/28; stopped at consolidation)

Brief (S5.359): a second parallel from-scratch designer, design only, same instructions as SA12 (all constraints, VMAF-NEG primary, efficiency meaningless without artifact checks), NOT told SA12's path (to stay independent); the owner: if SA13 goes down SA12's path, shut it down. Sandbox `Subagents/SA13_design/`. Evidence: numpy, intra (round 1) / 12-frame inter model (round 2), full frames, every plane, zeroth-order entropy; scripts t1_structure.py, t2_slices.py, t4_legal.py, t5_rlayer.py, t6_rext.py, t7_rext_pocs.py, t8_inter.py, t9_railfb.py, t10_fixpt.py, eval_dec.py; run8*.sh; results t*_results.txt; renders/.

### 7.0 First path (NOTES.md first entry; ledger S5.359 note 19:07) — FALSIFIED by its own T1
- Chosen path: closed-loop PIXEL-OWNED structure — every output sample = clamp(prediction from already-final samples + its own quantised innovation); consequences claimed: legality by construction (one compare-and-clamp per sample), exactness by a per-sample rule (canonical rail index = the smallest index whose reconstruction reaches the rail), step and mode readable from the picture (odd-dyadic reconstruction points, per-sample ctz), ties by fewest bits, L∞-bounded error (texture above ~one step cannot be flattened), no block/slice structure. SA13 stated it KNEW this was the predict-only family (DESIGN3 S5.212; S5.349; S5.353 S2/S5b) and pursued it only because (a) those verdicts were PSNR verdicts and NEG is now primary, (b) the tested forms were separable 2-tap; it tested a non-separable quincunx-order (diagonal-first) interpolation from four final neighbours and a directional variant, (c) against the same artifact checks. Coordinator: DIFFERENT from SA12's path → continue.
- **T1** (t1_structure.py; intra, full frame; 5/3 5-level baseline "b53" (with clip) vs closed-loop hierarchical "sep" (separable control), "qx" (quincunx order), "qxd" (directional); uniform (ramp 1.0) or ramped (0.8) steps; D = 8/14/24/40/64; PSNR Y/Cb/Cr + NEG at matched bits; oob 0 everywhere). ### 7.1 Round 1 — DESIGN.md (ledger S5.362)
**Design elements and the reason for each.**
- §1.1 Pigeonhole: any map f from a larger set onto the legal set is many-to-one; so the next encoder can recover the stream from y alone only if y is a point the stream describes (analysis of y in the transmitted cells) = the in-cell principle (agrees with expert B and S5.338).
- **Bracketed slices** (root fix of the seam): each slice codes its LAST row first as a 1-D anchor; the other rows are coded by a vertical 5/3 lifting on the closed interval [anchor of the slice above … own anchor], both ends fixed decoded values, never mirrored, updated or blended; log2 S levels; no row below ever needed (grep: no prior record). Slice k covers rows kS…kS+S−1; anchor row kS+S−1; bracket positions 0…S (0 = slice k−1's decoded anchor; for k = 0 a virtual anchor = row 0 coded once more as a 1-D row); S = 16 above 720p, 8 at 720p (header field); frame padded to a multiple of S by replicating the last row. Horizontal: 5 levels 5/3, whole-sample symmetric extension at frame left/right edges only. Vertical bracket fine → coarse: odd positions X[p] −= P(X[p−h], X[p+h]), interior even positions (never 0 or S) X[e] += U(X[e−h], X[e+h]); S = 16 → coefficient rows level 1 (8), 2 (4), 3 (2), 4 (1). Rounding (normative, sign-symmetric): P(a,b) = (a + b + [a+b<0]) >> 1, U(a,b) = (a + b + 2 − [a+b<0]) >> 2 (T2 used plain floors; its level maps show a mild one-signed bias). Order per slice: anchor row → interior (vertical bracket then horizontal on each of the S−1 coefficient rows).
- Why legality needs its own rail rule (§1.4): the in-cell reconstruction is found by alternating projection (legal set ↔ cells); on natural content it converges in ≤ 1 round; on the full-range rail arms (cut24 256×64, ext_10_422_l0 512×128, values 0/1023) it does not converge in 200 rounds even on 1-D anchor rows (t4b) — "the clip residual on a plate is one-signed, its low-pass part is large, and detail-band moves cannot lower a plate's mean. REXT removes the one-signed part: pixels exactly on a rail are pushed M codes outside it before the transform, so the reconstruction of a plate lands beyond the rail and the clip brings it back to the rail exactly, while ringing inside the plate becomes zero-mean."
- Formats (§2.1): planes coded independently as delivered (Y, Cb, Cr; or R, G, B for 4:4:4 graphics — no colour transform, so legality stays per plane); 4:0:0 = one plane; B ∈ {8, 10, 12} (16 parametric: all arithmetic widths B + 6 bits); legal range per plane [lo, hi] from a normative table indexed by (B, range flag, plane) (full [0, 2^B − 1] unless the transport restricts it, 10-bit SDI [4, 1019]; limited = SDI legal extremes); SDR/HDR (BT.709/BT.2020, PQ, HLG) only change what codes mean — transfer-agnostic, so no colour error from assuming a matrix (B3); 4:2:0 chroma slice = S/2 rows with its own anchor.
- **REXT (rail extension, new, grep: no record):** every encoder maps u = x + M·[x = hi] − M·[x = lo] (M = band-1 step of the plane) before the transform; decoder: w = R(q), z = S(w) (bracket endpoints in the extended domain); rail set Rs = {z ≥ hi} ∪ {z ≤ lo} from the first synthesis only; up to K_max rounds: target t = hi + M on top rails, lo − M on bottom rails, clip(z, lo+1, hi−1) elsewhere; stop if t = z; else w ← clamp(T(t), restricted cells), z = S(w); output y = clip(z, lo, hi). K_max = 1 (normative); on natural content the loop does 0 rounds on > 99 % of units and ≤ 1 elsewhere (T7: dng720 543 units, 4 needed 1 round). If the first encoder sees a unit not converge within K_max it switches that unit to **lossless** (all Δ = 1, flag) if it fits the budget; else keeps lossy indices and the final clip fires — legal but not generation-exact for that unit.
- Quantisation: Δ_b = 2^{e_b}; q = sgn(c)·⌊|c|/Δ_b⌋ (cell 0 = (−Δ, Δ)); nominal reconstruction R(q) = sgn(q)·⌊(|q| + 3/8)·Δ_b + ½⌋. Encoder freedom = idempotent index choice: any RD rule (two-candidate ECSQ, zeroing) provided every decision threshold lies at or below the nominal reconstruction point of the cell (e.g. write |q|−1 if |c| < (|q| + τ)Δ, 0 ≤ τ ≤ 3/8); τ a continuous per-position field. The projection may move a nonzero-index coefficient only within the upper part of its cell [(|q| + 3/8)Δ, (|q|+1)Δ) and a zero-index anywhere in the dead zone.
- (Inter mask rule: "finest bands go intra on noisy content" — the "reference noise in level-1 bands" finding.)
- Temporal: reference = previous decoded frame; prediction in the pixel domain before the transform (bracket endpoints taken as decoded anchor − pred). Motion vectors derived (not searched on the source): anchor-row field = previous frame's co-located field refined ±1 half-pel by matching the previous slice's decoded anchor; interior vector per 32-wide block by a fixed search (±R integer then half-pel) minimising |difference| between the two decoded anchors bracketing the block and the reference; fixed scan, tie-break smallest |v| then raster; transmitted (decoder never searches). Half-pel = two-tap average. Per-band inter mask from the previous frame's co-located statistics. Clean-region rule for refresh (vectors of refreshed slices stay above the wave front).
- Canonical decisions: plan from bit history + bank + decoded anchors; indices by idempotent rule; in-cell values by the decoder projection; vectors from decoded anchors + reference + previous field; inter mask from previous-frame statistics; intra refresh frame index mod P from stream start; scene-cut intra from the decoded anchor's prediction error; lossless unit from "does not converge in K_max and lossless fits" on the unit's input; padding leftover bytes. Mid-stream hop: refresh phase read from the picture (a slice whose intra analysis lies exactly on nominal points is the upstream refresh slice).
- Rate control / exact CBR: CBR frame = frame_bytes; slices variable with the causal prefix bound (slice k complete by (k+1) slice periods), bank ≤ ½ slice, canonical padding at frame end. Plan e_b = base_b + ⌊(k + φ_b)/4⌋, one integer k per slice interior, staggered φ_b (one step of k changes one band, ≈ 1.5 dB aggregate); anchor k = previous slice's k; interior k = f(bank, anchor bits, activity of the two decoded anchors, co-located slice bits previous frame) — the anchor is the rate controller's probe. Band exponent changes between slices softened by τ. Last resort: finest-level coefficients dropped after the last one that fits (end-of-band codes) — "the only non-smooth mechanism … exact and legal but visible".
- Entropy: static tANS tables per band class × context (3 magnitude classes of left/upper neighbours), zero-run symbol, sign raw, refinement bits raw; per plane anchor bands coarse→fine then interior bands coarse→fine; end-of-band codes. Vectors coded as difference to the previous frame's co-located vector.
- Loss: packet = slice; lost slice concealed by the MC reference; reach = the lost slice + the upper part of the next (slice k+1's anchor independent of k); refresh one wave per P frames, bound P + 1 frames (P = 8 → 9 frames).
- Resolution conversion outside the codec (≈ 0.3 ms at 720p50 accounted).
- Proof sketch §3: legal (final clip always); rt = 0; picture fixed point if every unit converged or is lossless (REXT(y₁) = the projection's final target; T(t) = w in the cells; idempotent rule returns q; byte-identical from gen 2); CBR hop same; fails on a unit that neither converges nor fits losslessly (cut24 shape).
**Round-1 measurements.**

- Mirror penalty (§1.3, sl2 = OMC shape): last row +20…+30 % ("Y 12.84 vs 10.65 mid, dng1080" at D40).
- **T4 in-cell alternating projection (POCS) on the bracketed structure** (t4_legal.py; arms "pocs" = plain alternating projection, "cf" = coarse-first: each round the correction is applied level by level coarse → fine, re-synthesising between levels; unit = anchor row or slice interior, cut24 90 + 72 = 162 units): t4_results.txt used [4,1019] on 0..1023 arms = infeasible → superseded by t4b. t4b (unconverged@200 of units; anchor n / interior n; need > 0, > 4, > 16 rounds): cut24 pocs D14 oob_pre 47,666, unconverged 88 (anch 90 of 90 need > 0, 28 > 4, 28 > 16; int 72/72, 72, 60); D40 59,296, 105 (anch 43/41 > 4/> 16; int 72, 64); cut24 cf D14 49,423, 130; D40 58,454, 111. ext10 pocs D14 241,387, 80 (anch need > 0 10, int 88, 76 > 16); D40 51,137, 20; ext10 cf D14 241,780, 81; D40 50,353, 18. gfx pocs/cf D14 0 oob, 0 unconverged; D40 2 oob, 0 unconverged (int need > 0 2, max 1). (t4 original: cut24 162/162 unconverged; ext10 194 at D14, 36 at D40; gfx 0; d720 pocs 0, d720 cf D14 1 unit to 200.) Natural and cf_gfx converge in ≤ 1–3 rounds; cut24 and ext10 do NOT converge in 200 rounds, even on the 1-D anchor rows.
- **T5 fixed-work "clip + next-encoder re-read + lossless pixel residual r"** (t5_rlayer.py): cut24 D14 bpp_q 2.807, bpp_r 3.7601 (r share 57.26 %), flips 1,772, r nonzero px 76,788, PSNR 53.74/52.69/52.69; D40 2.284, 4.6901 (67.25 %), 1,040, 79,584, 45.81/43.24/43.24; D64 2.037, 6.0036 (74.67 %), 925, 97,944, 41.51/40.32/40.32. ext10 D14 3.278, 2.4029 (42.30 %), 17,917, 93,417, 54.91/56.48/56.48; D40 3.026, 1.2846 (29.80 %), 1,824, 38,589, 44.16/44.27/44.27; D64 3.009, 1.0602 (26.05 %), 2,604, 27,627, 40.61/37.21/37.21. gfx D14 5.010, 0.0073 (0.15 %), 1, 126, 44.38/44.23/44.29; D40/D64 0. d720 D14 3.812, 0.0013 (0.04 %), 1, 77; D40/D64 0. spot D14 1.483, 0.0670 (4.32 %), 149, 11,653, 45.70/46.24/48.93; D40 0.481, 0.0267 (5.26 %), 26, 3,861, 41.19/42.42/45.44. Exact by construction, 0–5 % bits on natural, 30–75 % on cut24/ext10 → dead.
- **T6 REXT** (t6_rext.py; M = D): cut24 +4 dB (rails exact), bits slightly lower (DESIGN: PSNR Y 45.8 → 49.7 dB at D = 40, 2.28 → 2.13 bpp; T5 averaged 6 rail frames, T6 3 rail frames), but flips from rail-straddling coefficients remain.
  - [T6 has a script (t6_rext.py) but NO results file on disk: its figures (cut24 45.8 → 49.7 dB at D 40, 2.28 → 2.13 bpp, "flips remain") exist only in DESIGN.md/NOTES text.]
- **T7 REXT + in-cell projection in the EXTENDED domain** (t7_rext_pocs.py; rail pixels pinned at ±M, others in [lo+1, hi−1]): cut24 M14 D14 81 units, need > 0 81, max 64, > 2 74, > 4 74, > 8 66, unconverged@64 65, railset mismatch 4, PSNR 55.76/53.26/53.26; M40 D40 need > 0 81, > 2 63, > 4 54, > 8 54, unconverged 54, mismatch 180, 50.00/48.71/48.71. ext10 M14 102 units, need > 0 96, > 2 5, unconverged 5, mismatch 28, 61.25/inf/inf; M40 same 5 unconverged, mismatch 154, 51.76/inf/inf. gfx M14 0 need; M40 need 2, max 3, 0 unconverged. d720 M14 543 units, need 3, max 1; M40 need 4, max 1, mismatch 1. → natural 100 % within 1 round; cf_gfx max 3; ext10 95 % within 2 rounds (5/102 unconverged at 64); cut24 fails. Lossless cost of rail frames (zeroth order): cut24 1.9 bpp (DESIGN: 1.88), ext10 1.7 bpp (natural 9.6).
- Artifact table (§4): seam — no mirror, flat row phase (T2); grid smudges — no blend; still-area flicker — residual on a still input inside the dead zone → held, intra-masked bands re-code to the same indices, plan hysteresis ("flicker can only come from a plan change"); clip — only on the last-resort unit; colour cast — sign-symmetric rounding (to be verified); HVBC grid — no tiles, overlapping 5/3 synthesis; waxy flat patches — NOT solved by structure (flatplane above; remedies = allocation/temporal, unmeasured); banding — LL steps finest, REXT keeps plates exact (unmeasured on sky); pixelation — no per-sample decisions; hard edges — none; strip-level quality step — τ softening (eye). No temporal checks possible without a build in round 1.
- Work (§5.1, closed form; c_T ≈ 24 per full analysis or synthesis pass (OMC 2V×5H ≈ 20), c_Q ≈ 3, c_E ≈ 1 tANS lookup + 4, c_P ≈ 4, c_ME ≈ (2R+1)²·2/S): decoder W = N·[c_E + c_Q + c_T·(1 + 2·K_max) + c_P·K_max] → ≈ 84 N at K_max = 1; encoder ≈ 110 N + c_ME·N; JPEG XS ≈ N·(14 + 3 + 5) ≈ 22 N each end → ≈ 4× XS decoder, ≈ 5× encoder, fixed ("K_max = 0 would make it 1×, and is exactly what the rails prevent"); "three transform passes where XS has one … the owner may judge it too much". ZU7EV (8K60 4:2:2, ≈ 4.0 Gsample/s): transform 3 passes × 24 ops × 4.0 G ≈ 290 Gops/s → at 300 MHz ≈ 970 adders ≈ 20 k LUTs (≈ 9 %) decoder, encoder ≈ 13 %; tANS ≈ 13 lanes; buffers slice with bracket at 8K 4:2:2 ≈ 7680 × 17 × 2 × 18 bit ≈ 4.7 Mbit × 3 ≈ 14 Mbit of 27 Mbit URAM; URAM tight at 8K. Latency T = 3S lines (capture S + wire S + decode S at 3 passes): 720p50 S8 24 lines 0.64 ms (+0.3 conversion = 0.94); 720p60 0.53; 1080p50 S16/8 48/24 lines 0.85/0.43 ms (XS ≈ 32 lines ≈ 0.57); 1080p60 0.71/0.36 (XS ≈ 0.47); 2160p60 S16 48 lines 0.36; 4320p60 0.18. XS parity forces S = 8 at ≤ 1080p.
- Efficiency (§6; SA13's caveat: "intra, zeroth-order entropy, 5/3 only, one frame per cell, not tANS, not temporal — diagnostics, not verdicts"): the idempotent index choice lets the first encoder use full two-candidate RDO ("a lever OMC's lock could not use freely"); unmeasured and possibly negative: derived motion vectors (from two decoded rows) vs a free source search, and the anchor row's 1-D coding in intra/refresh slices; levers left open: per-band inter masks tuned on noisy content, RDO/ECSQ at gen 1, context-modelled tANS, texture-protecting τ, chroma allocation. Intra T2 bracketed = OMC-shape at equal NEG (dng1080 91.00 vs 90.83 at 1.215 vs 1.195; spot 95.28 vs 95.20 at 1.228 vs 1.359; dng720 90.07 @1.098 vs 90.46 @1.218), PSNR within ±0.2 dB, within 0–14 % of continuous ff; net expectation ≈ today's OMC, ≈ 1.33× short of half-rate.
- IP (§7): 5/3 (Le Gall & Tabatabai 1988; lifting Sweldens 1996; JPEG 2000 Part 1 royalty-free baseline); bracketed interval transform, anchors-first, idempotent threshold, REXT own work; dead-zone quantiser textbook; ECSQ CLG 1989 cleared; POCS (Youla 1978, Gubin et al. 1967); lossless fallback = JPEG 2000 Part 1 integer 5/3; tANS; block MC and two-tap half-pel 1970s–80s; encoder-derived transmitted vectors own work; rolling refresh 1990s; contexts 1980s–90s. "Not the XS architecture (XS has a continuous vertical transform across precincts)".
- Prior failures checked (§8): predict-only (re-tested T1 on NEG, failed); vertical-causal (not used: A5 chain and −0.4…−1.2 dB); continuous vertical (not used); XSL + blend (replaced by fixed endpoint); repair/capping/δ/lock (none); in-cell projection (used in the REXT domain, K_max = 1 + lossless fallback; rails reduced on ext10, not removed on cut24); signatures/odd-dyadic (not needed); embedded bit-plane (not used, +1…17 %); per-block mode (not used); decoder-derived motion (vectors transmitted); per-slice plan steps (τ softening); lossless-if-fits (new); HVBC texture-kill (τ continuous); HVBC tiles (none).
- Decisive next tests (§9): (i) T7 with the restricted cells and K_max = 1 + lossless-fit accounting on all rail arms at 0.5/1.0/2.0 bpp; (ii) T2 at 1080p with S = 8; (iii) a two-frame inter model (anchor-derived vectors vs source search) on the four motion cells, NEG + per-plane PSNR + row phase; (iv) the symmetric-rounding level maps per plane. Round 2 did (ii) and (iii) (and replaced sign-symmetric by half-to-even rounding); (i) was NEVER run, so convergence with the restricted cells (risk 9) was never measured.
- Own risks: (1) rails (T7: 65/81 units at D14, 54/81 at D40 unconverged at 64; lossless ≈ 2 bpp; candidates: finer rail set, M per unit, context-modelled lossless coder); (2) half-rate not shown; (3) latency parity needs S = 8 at ≤ 1080p (unmeasured efficiency); (4) work 4–5× XS; (5) derived vectors may cost on motion cells; (6) last-resort truncation visible; (7) band-exponent change between slices must be invisible after τ; (8) flatness; (9) restricted cells remove the feasibility guarantee (convergence measured with full cells only).
  - Two further statements of DESIGN.md: (a) §9 risk 1 — the lossless fallback's ≈ 1.9 bpp (1.88 bpp on cut24 rail frames) is zeroth-order: "a context coder may bring this under 1 bpp — unmeasured"; (b) §5.3 — XS parity holds with S = 8, "then the anchor overhead doubles; its cost at 1080p is not measured — only 720p S = 8, where br ≈ sl2".
**Coordinator review round 1 (S5.362, sent):** holds — bracketed slices (root seam fix), REXT, the NEG extension of the DESIGN3 falsification; fails/unproven — A4 on cut24-shape rails (disqualifier); lossless fallback = per-unit mode step (zero-visible-steps; HVBC); motion vectors if decoder-derived = Design A falsified on A5 (S5.29); latency at S = 8 untested; 4–5× XS work vs "large margin"; all evidence intra (smudges/ants/refresh on inter frames and a repeated-frame still input); A1 route vs today's real decodes and XS@2R [A1 weighting later corrected, S5.363]; flatness bar plan; cast offset. SA13 rethinking → DESIGN_v2. (S5.365: SA13 resumed after a session 429, reset 22:50.)

### 7.2 Round 2 — DESIGN_v2.md (on disk; round 2 never reported — S5.370: "SA13 no new files 23:49 → 04:00")
New evidence: offline 12-frame model t8_inter.py (one reference, rolling refresh P = 8, 5/3 bracketed slices, zeroth-order entropy per band/mode, encoder-derived transmitted vectors, 32-wide blocks, integer-pel). What the inter model is (t8_inter.py header): picture = clip(pred + decoded residual) — NO REXT and NO projection in the inter model ("natural content: the projection is inactive, T7"); interior vectors by full search ±R (x), ±R/2 (y) with R = 8 (= ±8 x / ±4 y) minimising SAD of BOTH decoded bracket anchors against the displaced reference; anchor-row vector = the same block's vector in slice k−1 refined ±1 on the previous slice's decoded anchor; rolling refresh slice s intra when f == 0 or s % P == f % P (interleaved); dead zone: zero threshold dz·step, cells [(q−1+dz)D, (q+dz)D), recon (q−1+dz+0.375)D; options --dz (intra), --dzi (inter; default = --dz), --lam (zero-vector preference), --P, --still, --round. So none of the t8 results tests legality or exactness together with temporal prediction. Configurations: "t8" runs (BSC_dng720_S8_D56/D100, BSC_dng1080_S16_D56; run8.sh) used DC reconstruction β = 3/8 and exposed the cast (D100 −0.614/−0.805/−1.234); "t8b" (run8b.sh, "LL midpoint recon") is the first battery with the anchor-row DC at cell centre (β = ½), half-to-even rounding, dz 1.0, no zero-vector preference — this is what DESIGN_v2 §4 calls "v1"; "t8d" = "v2" = the same + --dzi 1.25 --lam 1 (inter dead zone 1.25Δ + zero-vector cost +1 per sample) at S = 8 (the result headers print "dz=1.0 zeromv=False" although these flags were set). Today's OMC from its REAL decodes (SA7 out/DM/*_a0.d.yuv) with the same instruments (eval_dec.py: negscore.sh over all 12 frames, PSNR frames 2+, level offset frames 8+, smudgegroups.py on frames 8–11, flatplane.py on frame 10).
- **Rails (items 1, 2): NOT MET.** Mechanisms measured on cut24 (0↔1023 plates butting onto 0…895 ramps): in-cell projection plain — not fixed work, zero bits, "0 of 162 units converge in 200 rounds" citing t4b — **[SOURCES DISAGREE]** t4b_results.txt shows 88 (D14) / 105 (D40) of 162 unconverged at 200 rounds for pocs and 130/111 for cf; the all-162-unconverged figure is in t4_results.txt, which NOTES.md marks as superseded (it used the infeasible range [4,1019] on 0..1023 arms); REXT + projection extended domain — 65/81 (D14), 54/81 (D40) unconverged at 64 rounds; stall: a 20-sample run of plate pixels stuck at −41 vs pin −40 (the pinned rail samples leave no in-cell freedom); + rail-locked coefficients (coefficients whose support is all-rail fixed at their pinned value) — 57 of 72 rows converge vs 60 without (worse); clip + re-read + lossless residual — fixed work but residual 30–75 % of bits (t5); **t10 "emit what the next encoder reads", iterated at the first encoder** (t10_fixpt.py), K = 1 or 4 projection rounds, iterations 0–8+: cut24 K1 D14 81 units iters 0 = 43, 1 = 10, 2 = 4, 3–8 = 4, fail 20, H0 bpp first 2.975 emitted 2.962, PSNR 47.28/47.48/47.44; K1 D40 43/2/0/19, fail 17, 2.165 → 2.141, 42.76/42.60/42.59; K1 D80 43/14/0/21, fail 3, 1.864 → 1.837, 38.68/39.06/39.01; ext10 K1 D14 102 units 97/1/0/2, fail 2, 5.453 → 5.454, 60.65/150.20/150.20; D40 97/1/0/0 fail 4, 5.221 → 5.224, 50.35/150.20/150.20; D80 97/2/0/0 fail 3, 4.965 → 4.968, 44.48/150.20/150.20; gfx K1 D14 96/3 fail 0, 5.339; D40 99/0 fail 0, 2.063; cut24 K4 D14 43/14/0/3 fail 21, 2.977 → 2.973, 47.72/47.32/47.29; K4 D40 44/13/3/10 fail 11, 2.156 → 2.158, 45.09/44.21/44.20 → design text: 20/81 (K1 D14), 11/81 (K4 D40) never reach a fixed point in 8 iterations, bits ±1 %; **lossless unit fallback** (t9_railfb.py) — **[SOURCES DISAGREE]** DESIGN_v2: "25/27 units lossless, 3.7–3.8 bpp vs lossy 2.2–2.6"; t9_results.txt: cut24 D14 81 units, 50 lossless, bpp lossy-only 2.290 / with fallback 3.245; D40 50 lossless, 1.563 / 3.034; D80 38 lossless, 1.318 / 3.125; ext10 D14 102 units 5 lossless, 3.506 / 3.573; D40 5, 3.380 / 3.474; D80 5, 3.190 / 3.392. Why hard (SA13): REXT pins every rail sample to one exact value; a plate of pinned samples next to a steep edge is an equality constraint on many samples; the quantised edge coefficients ring Δ/2-scale values into the plate; the in-cell set reproducing the pins is thin (often a single point); alternating projection reaches it only after data-dependent iteration; no proof that no fixed-work mechanism exists. Consequence: A4 met on everything measured except dense full-range rail steps; there the picture is legal but may move at gen 2. Lossless fallback REMOVED (it cost 1.5 bpp over lossy and turned the whole frame lossless). Exact by construction: natural (dng720/1080, spot), cf_gfx, ext10 except 2–5 of 102 units (95–97 % of ext10 units exact); item 1 verdict: "NOT MET. No fixed-work, zero-bit, exact mechanism found; four measured, none closes; not proven impossible". Item 6 compares the still result with today's 60–77 % (S5.335).
- **Structure changes round 2:** rounding half-to-even in every lifting step (unbiased on ties; the sign-symmetric form of round 1 still biases on all-positive pixel data); DC reconstruction: the anchor row's coarsest band (the slice's DC) at cell centre β = 1/2, other bands β = 3/8 (with β = 3/8 on DC a uniform −0.6…−1.2 code offset on all planes at D = 100 from frame 0 — the cast mechanism); inter dead zone 1.25Δ (intra 1.0Δ) + zero-vector preference (+1 per sample in the vector cost) — still areas stop being re-coded; also saves bits (dng720 at 1.0 bpp: +2.6 NEG vs dead zone 1.0) [derivable: t8d V2_dng720 EXTRAPOLATED to 1.0 bpp (93.0*) vs t8b BSC_dng720_S8 interpolated at 1.0 (≈ 90.4); it measures inter dead zone 1.25 AND zero-vector preference together (--dzi 1.25 --lam 1), not the dead zone alone]; anchor step at S = 8: the anchor (last) row +7…+23 % mean |error| over mid rows in inter frames (dng1080 Cb 15.65/14.14/17.42 at D 72) [the t8d file shows these values for V2_dng1080_S8 at D36: Cb first/mid/last 15.65/14.14/17.42]; at S = 16 below the mid rows; AM sweep at S = 8 (t8f; run8f.sh planned arms AM = 4.5 and 6, dng1080 S8 D36, v2 flags --dzi 1.25 --lam 1) — t8f_results.txt is EMPTY; lossless fallback removed.
- **Motion vectors (item 3):** transmitted per 32-wide block, coded as difference to the previous frame's co-located vector; every encoder derives them from decoded data only (anchor rows vs reference; fixed candidate order; smallest |v| on ties); no derived state in the decoder → pixel refresh restores everything (the S5.29 mechanism cannot occur). Vector bits 1.3–9.8 % of the stream in the model (DESIGN_v2 §3) — [SOURCES DISAGREE] with the result files, whose mv shares run from 0.90 % (BSC_gfx_S16_D36, t8b) to 11.92 % (V2_spot_S8_D48, t8d), e.g. 9.81 % (t8 BSC_dng720_S8_D100), 9.32 % (V2_dng1080_S8_D72). SRCMV control (t8e; vectors searched on SOURCE rows, not canonical): dng1080 S8 D36 SRCMV 1.085 bpp (mv 3.47 %) NEG 91.125, PSNR 36.45/34.87/35.69, offset +0.013/+0.010/−0.008, smudge 0/0/0, flat Y 17.28 % Cb 1.49 %; derived (V2_dng1080_S8_D36) 1.080 bpp (mv 3.10 %) NEG 91.475, 36.42/34.81/35.71. spot SRCMV S8 D24 0.552 (mv 5.63 %) NEG 95.307, 41.68/42.65/45.85, flat Y 20.55 % Cb 34.06 %; derived (V2_spot_S8_D24) 0.593 (mv 5.90 %) 95.451, 41.54/42.47/45.61. (SA13 wrote no reading of t8e; NOTES.md says figures would be appended — they were not. As measured, canonical anchor-derived vectors did NOT cost NEG against source-searched ones: dng1080 derived 91.475 @1.080 vs source 91.125 @1.085; spot 95.451 @0.593 vs 95.307 @0.552, derived using +7 % bits.) [Configuration caveat: no run script for t8e exists (run8f.sh only waits on the SRCMV_ processes, then runs AM4.5/AM6); the t8e header prints dz/zeromv but --dzi/--lam cannot show there, so whether SRCMV used the v2 flags (--dzi 1.25 --lam 1) of the V2_* runs it is compared with is UNRECORDED. spot SRCMV offset (f8+) +0.041/+0.023/+0.009; dng1080 +0.013/+0.010/−0.008.]
- **Efficiency vs today's real decodes (items 4, 7), NEG interpolated in log-bpp (* = extrapolated):** dng720 (S8, v2) today @0.5 88.86 / model 84.27 (−4.6); today @1.0 93.83 / model 93.0* (−0.8). [the "(S16, v1)" rows are interpolated from the t8b runs, not from the t8 lines — e.g. spot t8b D24 0.752/96.799 and D48 0.338/91.862 give 94.28 at 0.5; "v1" = t8b rules] dng1080 (S16, v1) 90.50 / 85.3 (−5.1); 93.54 / 89.85 (−3.7). dng1080 (S8, v2) 90.50 / 84.52 (−6.0); 93.54 / 90.78 (−2.8). spot (S16, v1) 93.47 / 94.28 (+0.8); 97.55 / 98.6* (+1.0). spot (S8, v2) 93.47 / 93.78 (+0.3); 97.55 / —. cf_gfx (S8, v2) 81.06 / 82.6* (+1.5); 92.47 / 89.35 (−3.1). Per-plane PSNR at nearest rates: dng1080 today @1.0 36.91/36.04/36.74; model S8 v2 @1.08 36.42/34.81/35.71 (chroma −1.0…−1.2); spot today @0.5 39.54/42.80/46.31; model v2 @0.59 41.54/42.47/45.61. SA13's list of non-structural differences from today: zeroth-order entropy vs context tANS; no RDO; no chroma step tuning; 5/3 at the finest levels (today 9/7-M); integer-pel 32-wide vectors (today half-pel); no per-band inter mask; fixed D, no rate control. [Round 1 estimated ≈ 9 %/13 % LUTs and 14 Mbit URAM; round 2 ≈ 3–4 %/8 % and 10 Mbit — both kept.]

- Weakest (own, in order): (1) cut24 rails legal but not generation-exact; (2) efficiency −3…−6 NEG on dng, chroma weakest; (3) anchor row at S = 8 a seam risk until its step is tuned per plane; (4) flatness bar not met.

All luma or Cb only; there is NO Cr render. Low-severity wording difference: NOTES describes cut24 as "steps 1023→0 butting onto a 0..1023 ramp", DESIGN_v2 as "0↔1023 plates butting onto 0…895 ramps" (the pack uses the latter).
- Coordinator summary of the on-disk round 2 (S5.370): cut24 rails still not exact with fixed work, lossless fallback removed, efficiency −2.8…−6.0 NEG on dng / +0.3…+0.8 on spot, S = 8 anchor row +7…+23 % excess, 3 transform passes, still input ≤ 0.03 %, cast fixed with half-to-even rounding + DC at cell centre. MEMO_PRIOR_DESIGNERS §A: at 8 rows the anchor row +7…+23 % (dng1080 Cb); "the designer proposed tuning the anchor's step per slice height. That is a knob patching a design-created row."; latency 3S lines: S = 8 gives 24 lines. SA13 was also to receive the S5.369 ruling "with its next review" (it was stopped before one).

**SA13 successes / failures and what caused each (from the records).**
- Seam flat at S = 16 in Y/Cb/Cr ← bracketed slices (both ends decoded, no mirror); at S = 8 the anchor row became a special row (+7…+23 %) ← the anchor row's own step (AM tuned at S = 16 only).
- Pixel-owned predict-only falsified on NEG ← aliasing of the subsampled coarse samples.
- Rails not exact in fixed work ← REXT pins; thin in-cell set; alternating projection needs data-dependent iteration; lossless fallback too costly and a per-unit mode step.
- Cast fixed ← half-to-even rounding + DC at cell centre (β = 3/8 on DC produced −0.6…−1.2).
- Efficiency −2.8…−6.0 NEG on dng ← not isolated (SA13 lists non-structural model differences; unresolved).

## §8 SA14 "NEST" — leaf-reading lifting, legal by construction, exact by reading (Opus 5.5; 2 rounds + unreported round 3, 2026-09-27/28; stopped at consolidation)

Brief (S5.360): a THIRD from-scratch designer, same instructions; stop it if it goes down SA12's path (Haar/S-transform pair pyramid with averaging lowpass + predicted details) or SA13's (closed-loop pixel-owned predict-only, quincunx); keep it if it finds another approach. Sandbox `Subagents/SA14_design/`; numpy integer models in notes/, logs out/, renders renders/.
Path check (S5.360 note 20:07, NOTES.md): NEST KEEPS a symmetric update yet claims an acyclic legal synthesis: the update reads the TRANSMITTED (dequantised, unclamped) leaf values, never clamped ones; every detail band a leaf via a non-separable one-level 2-D lifting (eo/oe/oo classes predicted from final samples, one update builds the low band), recursively, then horizontal-only levels; legality by RAIL ESCAPES (a detail sample whose output sits on its window boundary is an escape symbol: output = boundary, update contribution = band prediction) with top-down windows [lo+U, hi+U]; exactness by reading with nested lattices (reconstruction on qΔ) → DIFFERENT from SA12 and SA13 → continue. Record check to apply at its review: escape families (S5.194–S5.199 escapes; S5.215/S5.217 bank-funded escapes cost bits / plan changes), S5.349 cycle argument. SA14's log: constraints read in full; expert memos read; ledger grepped (predict-only S5.212/S5.228, one-sided update S5.349, nested lattices S5.70, vertical causal S5.353–S5.358, SA12/SA13 paths S5.355/S5.359). Own first idea rejected: modular (wrap-around) lifting (black and white become neighbours on the circle → graphics edges would need to be exact).

### 8.1 Round 1 — DESIGN.md (ledger S5.365)
**Design elements and reasons.**
- **Leaf-reading lifting:** every UPDATE step reads transmitted leaf values (a dequantised index + its band prediction, or a rail symbol), never clamped/final samples; every PREDICT step reads only final samples → each predicted sample's legal interval depends only on final values → acyclic synthesis WITH a full symmetric 5/3 update. "The records' finding that 'legality in the synthesis = predict-only in disguise' (S5.349/S5.350, expert A §2.3) holds only when the update reads the clamped value."
- The separable 5/3 rewritten exactly as a non-separable one-level lifting with all four bands leaves (a separable transform makes the horizontal detail an intermediate value). Notation: A = even/even, B = even-row/odd-col, C = odd-row/even-col, D = odd/odd; π = (i + j) & 1 position-parity rounding offset (makes every rounding unbiased; removes the +0.6-code cast measured with plain floor rounding, out/seam_signed.txt). Analysis: dB = B − ⌊(A_left + A_right + π)/2⌋; dC = C − ⌊(A_above + A_below + π)/2⌋; dD = D − (⌊(C_left + C_right + π)/2⌋ + ⌊(B_above + B_below + π)/2⌋ − ⌊(A_ul + A_ur + A_dl + A_dr + 1 + π)/4⌋); leaves HH = dD; HL = dB + ⌊(u_D above + u_D below + 1 + π)/4⌋; LH = dC + ⌊(u_D left + u_D right + 1 + π)/4⌋; L = A + ⌊(4(u_B left + u_B right + u_C above + u_C below) + (u_D at the 4 diagonals) + 7 + π)/16⌋; u terms from the non-existent row below a slice are 0; 2 levels of 2-D then 3 horizontal 1-D levels (matching today's 2V×5H); a 4-tap horizontal predictor (−1, 9, 9, −1)/16 fits (option); a vertical 4-tap excluded (needs a row below). Frame left/right edges: sample and detail replication (= symmetric extension for 5/3).
- Synthesis per slice and plane: entropy-decode all symbols (lattice value q·2^s (+ c_p inter); rail = flag + side); compute update values; windows fine to coarse (pixel window W₀ = format range: full 0…2ⁿ−1; limited 4…1019 at 10-bit, 1…254 at 8-bit, 16…4079 at 12-bit; low band of level ℓ has window W_ℓ(A-position) + U_ℓ); coarsest low band lattice → min(max(v, lo_w), hi_w) (safety clamp), rail → bound; levels coarse to fine A = L − U; B = rail ? bound : clamp(d_B + P_B(A)); C likewise with the causal top; D = rail ? bound : clamp(d_D + P_D(A, B, C)); one pass.
- Quantiser: only the lattice is normative: reconstruction q·2^s per band per slice; encoder rule free (default dead zone z = 9/16 + any texture-preserving RD rule); R∘Q maps every point of any finer lattice to itself for z ≤ 1 → a decoded picture's leaves survive re-quantisation at their own or any finer shift. Today's reconstruction offsets ("texture bias") REMOVED (an offset breaks nesting).
- **Rails:** a detail sample (or the coarsest low sample) whose output equals a bound of its window is a rail sample, coded RAIL±; every lattice sample strictly inside. **Gen-1 encoder closure** (notes/enc3s.py): canonical analysis of the source (source samples on their bound are rails, update value = rail value); quantise lattice samples; repeat {synthesise; every lattice sample whose pre-clamp value reaches/passes its bound: index 0 → RAIL, else one step inward} until none reaches its bound. Terminates (indices only move toward 0; rails never revert). Measured ≤ 6 rounds (e.g. 903 → 227 → 65 → 4 → 1 changes), 0–0.06 % of samples on natural content (out/e3s_final.txt). Rejected closure variants: rail with update value 0 for every overshooting sample (max error 204 vs 65 for the clip; multi-round); rails carrying the exact clamped value (joint fixed point oscillates ±1 two-cycles, HH predictor couples neighbours non-monotonically); one-shot "pessimistic margin" closure (snapped hundreds of thousands of samples; −10…−25 dB); coarsest-first rounds (42–147 rounds); "source rails as lattice samples" (−11…−16 dB vs clip on rail content).
- **Exactness by reading, nested lattices:** the next encoder runs the exact inverse; rails recognised on their bound; every other leaf returns bit-exactly; per band it reads the coarsest shift and cheapest mode whose lattice holds those leaves (OR + count-trailing-zeros, one pass); that description decodes to the same picture and never costs more bits; gen 1 emits the canonical description of its own reconstruction → byte-identical from gen 2 (Lemmas 1–4, Theorem). "Nested lattices fail to identify a plan's provenance (S5.70), but provenance is not needed: a canonical representative is"; rails do not depend on the plan (what broke nesting before).
- **Shifted causal slices:** slice k covers source rows 16k+1 … 16k+16; frame row 0 coded as a one-row head (intra, 1-D horizontal); vertical polyphase aligned so the FIRST row of every slice is an odd (predicted) row and the LAST is an even (low) row (at level 2, L1 rows k·8+0,2,4,6 predicted, 1,3,5,7 low); causal top = the slice above's final even row (decoded pixels at level 1, decoded level-1 low row at level 2); the last even row's update treats the missing detail below as 0 (the only one-sided operation); no mirror, extrapolation at top, blend, lookahead. 4:2:0 chroma S/2 rows, 2 vertical levels at S = 16, 1 at S = 8.
- Temporal: previous decoded frame; MC prediction integer + bilinear half-pel (2-tap average); vectors per 16×S block, vertical ±4 rows; coefficient-domain band prediction c_p = plain analysis of p (with p's own rows as causal top); inter lattice leaf = c_p + q·2^s, inter rail update value = c_p → static content with q = 0 reproduces previous leaves exactly (frozen). Vectors canonical and transmitted: derived only from y(t−1), y(t−2) (encoder keeps one extra decoded frame, quarter resolution enough) and the current frame's final rows above the slice: constant-velocity extrapolation of the block's motion y(t−2) → y(t−1), refined by matching the final rows above; decoder only reads them. Intra/inter per band READ (leaf-value-based cost rule shared by gen 1). Refresh: contiguous band of ⌈N/8⌉ slices per frame moving top to bottom (every slice refreshed within 8 frames; documented recovery bound = 8 frames + the vertical extent of the damage in slices, to be measured in a build); slices above the wavefront use vectors clamped to the refreshed region; static refresh without a pop (static slice keeps the intra lattice values of its last refresh, next refresh re-describes with the same indices); refresh follows the picture (forces intra only where leaves are intra-consistent).
- Rate control: budget = nominal + causal bank of actual unspent bits, capped at 2× nominal; deterministic padding; stage 1 (fresh sources): per-band shifts + dead-zone field via LUT cost, quantise, closure, check fit with the real pack, if overflow one coarser step and a second pack (≤ 2 packs); stage 2 (always): canonical reading of own leaves (coarsest consistent shift, cheapest mode) → bits(stage 2) ≤ bits(stage 1) ≤ budget; E(picture): if the reading of the picture's own leaves fits, emit it (always for a decoded picture), else stage 1 then 2 (fresh sources read at shift 0 = lossless, do not fit). Strip steps: per-band shifts change ≤ one octave between vertically adjacent slices with a continuous dead-zone ramp (eye check).
- Entropy: static tANS per band class or canonical prefix codes with LUT cost; alphabet {index, RAIL+, RAIL−}; contexts: left and upper neighbour class ∈ {0, nonzero, RAIL+, RAIL−} + magnitude classes; **rail sign must be in the context** (without it plates cost ~1 bit/sample, +160…+230 %; with it rail content 55–63 % cheaper than the clip reference, out/ctx_bits_final.txt); slice header per-band shift (4 bits, differential), mode (1 bit), vectors (differential, static); tANS flushed at slice end.
- Loss: one slice per packet; concealment by copy of p; spatial damage = lost slice + ~2 rows at level 1 and 4 at level 2 of the next slice; refresh heals; safety clamps keep legality.
- Formats: 8/10/12-bit (int32-safe for 16-bit); 4:0:0/4:2:0/4:2:2/4:4:4; full/limited; HDR/SDR code values; conversion a separate stage.
**Round-1 measurements** (single frames f8, intra, full frames, all planes; entropy rates; D0 sweeps the rate).
- **E1 transform cost** (out/e1s.txt; NEST-S vs separable integer 5/3, BD-rate over D0 ∈ {12 … 256}): dng1080 +0.06 / +0.19 / +0.12 %; spot +0.05 / +0.18 / −0.47 %; cf_gfx +0.09 / +0.37 / +0.05 %; floor +0.04 / −0.24 / −0.43 % (Y/Cb/Cr). The first non-separable form (HH predicted from its four edge neighbours, one update, no inner updates) LOST 4–28 % (out/e1.txt) → replaced by the exact separable-equivalent.
- **Legal and exact NEST vs the same quantised transform with a plain clip** (out/e3s_final.txt, vmaf_check_final.txt, ctx_bits_final.txt): dng1080 D0 48 bits 1.3283 / 1.3284, NEG 91.249 / 91.248, PSNR 37.702/36.702/37.405 identical; D0 128 0.5278 / 0.5278, 84.100 / 84.102, 35.050/34.345/35.527 vs 35.051/34.345/35.527; spot D0 48 0.4977 / 0.4955 (+0.4 %), 91.741 / 91.777, 41.617/42.884/46.009 vs 41.624/…; D0 128 0.2682 / 0.2656 (+1.0 %), 84.109 / 84.267, 38.432/40.504/42.907 vs 38.459/… (zeroth-order bits; with context model spot +0.3 % / +0.9 %). Synthetic full-range rail picture (Y): D0 48 PSNR 59.7 vs clip 55.7, context bits 0.131 vs 0.356 (−63 %); D0 128 47.7 vs 43.0, 0.124 vs 0.276 (−55 %). Chroma untouched (0 samples differ). Luma differs on near-rail highlights (spot 0.65–1.1 % of pixels, max |Δ| 61–130) → −0.04…−0.16 NEG on the highlight-heavy cell at low rate. 0 oob every run (Lemma 2: every output sample in [lo, hi] for ANY bitstream, including corrupted ones; no pixel clip anywhere). Gen-2 description identical and picture unchanged in 21 of 21 runs of the final policy (7 intra cells incl. 12-bit and limited synthetic rails × 3 rates) and every earlier variant.
- **Artifact table (§3)**: seam line — no row below is read, the last row of every level is a low row, no predict one-sided, row-phase profile ≈ continuous transform; smudges — no blend, parity-unbiased rounding, flat signed mean per row phase; colour cast — π-alternating offsets; illegal picture / clip — legality in the synthesis (Lemma 2); repair/lock — nothing lost (Lemma 3), plan and modes read; still-area flicker — inter q = 0 reproduces leaves exactly (frozen), refresh re-describes static intra lattice values exactly, temporal hysteresis an encoder policy; 16×16/32×32 grid (HVBC) — every synthesis basis is the overlapping 5/3, no block unit, the only per-region parameters are per-slice shifts limited to one octave with a continuous dead-zone ramp; waxy flat patches — "no mode zeroes a block; flattening can only come from the encoder's dead-zone policy, the same risk any wavelet codec has; there is no LL-only rung"; rail ringing/halos — rails reproduce black and white plates exactly, synthetic rail PSNR 4–7 dB above the clip reference.
- **Seams: shifted causal slice** (luma, 16-row, 2V×5H, closed loop; out/seam_test*.txt, seam_signed.txt; mean |error| by row of slice, max/min ratio): dng D0 48 continuous (no slices) 1.18 (intrinsic 4-row pattern); today-like mirror 1.49, last row +27 %; shifted causal 1.36, rows 0–1 +6 %, row 15 = the 4-row pattern. spot D0 128 continuous 1.03; mirror 1.38, last row +29 %; causal 1.05. Bits continuous / mirror / causal: dng 1.007 / 0.996 / 0.961 (PSNR 38.31 / 38.12 / 38.34); spot 0.191 / 0.177 / 0.171 (38.64 / 38.36 / 38.72) → −3…−4 % bits, +0.2…0.4 dB vs mirror. Signed mean error per row phase: causal inside the continuous transform's own spread (±0.3 code). Not done (need a temporal build): smudgegroups/artifactmap on frames 8–15, flatplane/texstat, colour renders.
- Work (§4): analysis ≈ 9 ops/sample; synthesis ≈ 9 + 3; quantisation ≈ 3; canonical reading ≈ 1; MC ≈ 4; tANS ≈ 5 per symbol. W_dec ≈ 21 N_s; W_enc ≈ 29 N_s + ME + 2·(5 N_s) + c_cl·N_near; JPEG XS ≈ 17 N_s. Closure c_cl ≈ 70 ops per change (≈ 12 dependants × 6 ops); measured changes ≤ 0.06 % natural (worst spot lowest rate ≈ 1,200 per 1080p luma frame ≈ 18 per slice), ≤ 0.13 % synthetic rails; proven bound Σ|q| moves + one rail per sample (far above measured). ME on decoded data ±8 H ±4 V, integer then half-pel, 16×S blocks, template on the final rows above ≈ 40 ops/sample. Packs ≤ 2 (today's lock ≈ 50 entropy-pass equivalents). ZU7EV (≈ 230k LUT, 460k FF, 11 Mb BRAM, 27 Mb URAM): 8K60 4:2:2 ≈ 3.98 Gsamples/s ≈ 13.3 samples/clock at 300 MHz; encoder ≈ 90 ops/sample × ~40 LUT per add ≈ 48k LUT (21 %); decoder ≈ 21 ops ≈ 11k LUT (5 %); line buffers slice + ±4-row window ≈ 1 MB at 8K 4:2:2 URAM (30 %); DDR (one reference read and one write per frame + a quarter-resolution y(t−2) at the encoder) ≈ 9 GB/s at 8K60 of ≈ 19 GB/s; 1080p60 ≈ 16× lighter; estimates to be replaced by synthesis results, no parallel hardware beyond the throughput every 8K codec needs. Latency = today's model (T = S capture + one slice period + 2 lines): 720p50 S8 0.500 ms codec-only, 0.806 ms worst conversion; 1080p60 S16 0.50 ms (34 lines); 2160p60 0.25; 4320p60 0.13 — XS parity. Closure must finish inside the slice period (< 1 % at measured load).
- Against today (§5.4, no build): shifted causal vs mirror −3…−4 % bits, +0.2…0.4 dB; today's cross-slice term worth 0.17–0.46 dB (S5.206); no vertical 9/7-M (loss unknown); no texture bias; no lock/repair (the forced re-choice alone was −0.08…−1 dB); vectors from decoded frames = main unknown on motion. Expected parity ±3 % intra natural, better on rail graphics, unknown on motion.
- IP (§6): 5/3 LeGall–Tabatabai 1988, lifting Sweldens 1996, JPEG 2000 Part 1 reversible path; leaf-reading order, windows, rails, closure own; non-separable rewriting = algebraic identity; parity-alternating rounding own/obvious; dead zone textbook; canonical reading own (not expert A's signatures); coefficient-domain temporal, block MC, bilinear half-pel 1970s–80s; vectors from decoded frames = classic block matching on decoder-available data; rolling refresh + vector clamp; tANS (Duda), prefix codes Huffman 1952; bank/CBR/padding today's own.
- Prior failures (§7): DESIGN3 / S5.228 / one-sided update — update here symmetric full-weight, acyclic from what it reads; expert A — cycle only if U reads clamped values; expert A odd-dyadic signatures (S5.349: +5…75 % distortion; Δ = 1 unreadable) — not used, reading nested lattices needs no offset, Δ = 1 reads trivially; S5.70 nested lattices — provenance not needed; odd-dyadic signatures — not used; lock/R1-LOCK — replaced by reading; in-cell projection K = 4 — none; encoder index cap (S5.213) — closure acts on predicted samples, rails absorb step-wider-than-window; blend = smudge — none; continuous vertical transform — not used; vertical-causal DPCM — vertical 5/3 kept inside the slice, only the top causal, cast removed by π; SA12's Haar/S+P — not used; SA13's pixel-owned — not used; modular arithmetic — rejected; decoder-derived vectors — encoder-derived, transmitted; half-pel gating lever — bilinear kept; soccer/officewalk — not used.
- Own risks (§8): 8.1 closure worst-case bound not proven (a demotion pushes neighbours by ≈ 2^s/8; long chains of near-bound samples, e.g. fine near-white stripes, could take many rounds; first build gate: adversarial generator; fallback directions: rail update value = dead-zone reconstruction, or coarse-to-fine Gauss–Seidel worklist); 8.2 motion efficiency (vectors lag a frame; "readable refinement" lever); 8.3 visible checks not done (first-rows excess +3…6 %, strip steps, flattening); 8.4 mid-stream hop exact after own ramp + ≤ one refresh cycle (its first two frames intra may not fit the budget); 8.5 near-rail highlights −0.04…−0.16 NEG (spot low rate); 8.6 intra-only evidence; 8.7 hardware estimates.
**Coordinator review round 1 (S5.365, sent):** holds — leaf-reading lifting ("strongest idea so far"), nested reading, rails, π rounding, shifted causal slices, transmitted vectors; open — (1) iterative closure must become one pass by construction (follow the acyclic synthesis order) or a proven bound ≤ 2 under adversarial rails; (2) dng rows 0–1 +6 %; (3) temporal path + still-input convergence + smudges on the owner's marked material; (4) comparison vs today's real decodes at coded rates; (5) rail-graphics NEG and renders. Ledger summary of the idea: it refutes the premise "legality in synthesis ⇒ predict-only" (S5.349/S5.350/expert A), which assumed the update reads clamped values.

### 8.2 Round 2 — DESIGN_v2.md (ledger S5.368; numpy models, estimated rates = entropy × tANS overhead)
- **Closure REPLACED by injective dequantisation (IDQ):** per predicted sample, inputs raw (prediction from final samples + band prediction, unclamped), window [lo, hi], step Δ = 2^s (s ≥ 1), index q or RAIL±. Top lattice index qhi = ⌊(hi−1 − raw)/Δ⌋, bottom qlo = −⌊(raw − lo−1)/Δ⌋; lattice indices in [qlo, qhi] decode to raw + qΔ; pool = off-lattice values in [lo+1, hi−1] (≥ (M−1)(Δ−1)/Δ for window width M), split T = ⌊pool/2⌋ top, pool − T bottom; index qhi + k (1 ≤ k ≤ T) decodes to the k-th off-lattice value counted down from hi−1 (closed form: within the t₀ values above the top lattice point hi−1−(k−1); otherwise Lt − gΔ − 1 − r with g, r quotient/remainder of k − t₀ − 1 by Δ−1); bottom symmetric; RAIL+ → hi, RAIL− → lo. Update values functions of symbols only (band prediction + q·Δ for lattice and beyond indices, 0 for rails) → acyclic, legal. Hardware: a compare pair, a subtract, the closed form only for beyond indices (near rails), multiply-free reciprocal of Δ−1 per s (small table). Next encoder: on the bound ⇒ RAIL; on the lattice ⇒ q = (v − raw)/Δ; else rank from the top (≤ T → qhi + k) else qlo − (bottom rank) → reproduces every index. Every index set legal and exactly recoverable; encoder = analysis, quantise, one synthesis (no adjustment, no iteration); rails kept as an encoder option (update value 0); exactness from exact index recovery + canonical parameters (causal plan, transmitted vectors, mode = cheapest exact reading); coarser-lattice reading no longer needed → reconstruction offsets usable again. Capacity bound: an index overshoots by more than T steps only if its virtual value is > ≈ M(Δ−1)/2 ≥ M/2 codes outside; virtual error bounded by E = Σ_b (Δ_b/2)·‖ψ_b‖₁ → plans with E ≤ M/2 guaranteed (per-plan table, checked once) — NOT computed in round 2; measured 0 saturations (intra 13 cell/plane/depth/range combinations × 3 rates; temporal 4 runs × 12 frames). Step ≥ 2 rule cost (Δ = 1 has no off-lattice values): +0.2…+1.0 % BD-rate at the highest rates; rail picture +0.18 % (out/minshift.txt).
- Intra measured (out/v3_intra.txt, v3_ctx.txt, v2_vs_clip.txt): dng1080, spot, cf_gfx (Y, Cb, Cr): 0 oob, gen-2 indices identical, 0 samples moved, PSNR = plain clip of the same indices ±0.01 dB, bits identical (spot +0.1…0.9 % from rail symbols, context estimate); synthetic rails 8/10/12-bit full + limited: 0 oob, identical, +4…+9 dB vs the same codec without rails, −38…−65 % context bits (with rail sign).
- **First-row excess (item 2):** explanation — the first predicted row of a causal slice cannot update the (final) row above, so its detail synthesis gain is 7/8 instead of the interior 3/4; its quantisation error lands more on rows 0–1 and less on the row above (redistribution; over the three boundary rows the total changes +1.7 %). Measured (causal slice, bottom update zero; vs the continuous transform at the same absolute rows; out/seam_test_bot0.txt, seam_chroma.txt): dng D0 48 rows 0–1 Y +6 %, Cb +6…+7 % (11.80/11.90 vs 11.11/11.15), Cr +5…+7 % (11.08/11.31 vs 10.46/10.65); last row Y −8 %; dng D0 128 Y +3…+5 %, Cb +4…+7 %, Cr +4…+5 %; spot none. Not below the continuous transform's own 4-row pattern on dng at D0 48. Lever: step Δ/2 on the finest horizontal subband of the first detail row at vertical levels 1 and 2 (a fixed normative per-row step): rows 0–1 −7 % at D0 48, ±0 at D0 128, cost +4…+5 % bits; encoder-side dead-zone changes do not remove it. Status: open.
- **Temporal model (item 3; notes/temporal3.py)**: whole-frame transform, NO slices; inter per band in the coefficient domain; band prediction = previous frame's leaves where the block vector is zero (exact hold), else plain analysis of the MC prediction; vectors 16×16, derived by the encoder from decoded frames only (match y(t−1) → y(t−2), applied forward; ±8 H, ±4 V, integer), transmitted; mode per band per 16-row stripe by cost; refresh one eighth of the rows intra per frame, cycled. Still input dng720 f0 ×10 (out/t3_still_dng720.txt): 0 changed samples in every plane from frame 1; gen 2 identical; 0 oob; refresh re-describes the frozen intra lattice exactly (no pop) (today 60–77 %). Gen 2 on moving content: dng720 (12 frames, 4 rates) and spot (12 frames, 3 rates): indices identical every frame; 0 oob. Mode readability (flip each band-stripe's mode and recover; out/mode_readability.txt): the wrong mode always more expensive, 0 of 93–99 stripes per frame.

  - v4 configuration (notes/nv4.py header, notes/t4_intra.py): shifted causal slices (first row of a slice predicted from the slice above's FINAL row; last row a low row with zero update from below), 2 vertical + horizontal levels inside the slice then 3 horizontal-only; leaf-reading 5/3; IDQ over the FULL legal window with NO rail symbols; encoder: open-loop quantisation, then ONE coarse-to-fine pass that, level by level, picks for every predicted sample the index among {open-loop, nearest in-window, first beyond} whose decoded value is nearest the source value of that level (fixed tie-break); next encoder: exact index recovery from the picture. S = 8 for dng720/graded720, 16 for spot/gfx. graded720 here = luma clip((x − 200)·5/2), chroma clip(512 + (x − 512)·2). - Stop (S5.370): "SA14 progressing (S5.368) but on the same boundary class (first-row excess)"; stopped with TaskStop; sandbox read-only.

**SA14 successes / failures and what caused each (from the records).**
- Acyclic legal synthesis with the full 5/3 update, ±0.5 % BD vs separable 5/3 ← updates read transmitted leaves; the separable 5/3 rewritten so all four bands are leaves.
- One-pass legal + exact ← IDQ (every index maps one-to-one to a legal value); but IDQ moves samples AWAY from the source (107k away vs 300k toward on rail-graded content; round-3 v4 graded720 171k–217k away vs 47k–53k toward) ← a larger overshoot decodes further inside the window.
- Rail content cheaper and better (+4…9 dB, −38…65 % bits synthetic) ← RAIL± symbols with the rail sign in the context; but +10.9 % context bits at D0 128 on rail-graded natural content (round 3: rails-everywhere +17.9…+31.2 % Y at D0 96–160).
- Still input 0 change, no refresh pop ← exact hold (q = 0 reproduces leaves) + refresh re-describing the frozen intra lattice.
- First-row excess +3…7 % ← shifted causal slice: the first predicted row cannot update the final row above (synthesis gain 7/8 vs 3/4) — a design-created special row (S5.369).
- Efficiency parity at estimated rates only with a plan tilt tuned on dng720 (untilted dng720 +48 % bits) ← plan, not structure.

Use it as evidence of what was measured, not as parts to assemble"; records pointer ledger S5.349–S5.369 (grep `^### S5.36`); "All of their numbers come from numpy MODELS: entropy rates, sometimes multiplied by a static-tANS overhead factor of about ×1.13–1.16 at 0.4–0.5 bpp and ×1.04–1.07 at 0.8–1.7 bpp. They are not coded bitstreams."

All three STOPPED (TaskStop) with leftover jobs killed; sandboxes read-only.
