Nobody solved it at that root within latency parity and loss containment"; review lessons: (1) luma-only results (owner: "we never only look at luma", three corrections → every number and render per plane); (2) efficiency without artifact checks ("HVBC was efficient because it let artifacts through"); (3) parts tested separately never combined (SA14's slices, legality and temporal path never run together); (4) per-cell tuned plans (one canonical, causal plan rule); (5) fixes for a design's own side effects are patches; (6) 8×8-mean level maps cannot show a one-row step. S5.370 addendum (owner): if SA15 stalls → memo its full work and results, stop it, and give the exact same assignment (original brief + MEMO_PRIOR_DESIGNERS + SA15 findings memo + PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS) to a FABLE 5.1 designer, from scratch, one time only.
- MEMO_PRIOR_DESIGNERS §B also recorded: "Today's in-cell projection (4 rounds, S5.338–S5.342): exact on natural content, quality = clip, 0 bits. It stalls on dense rails, and 4 rounds are already more work than allowed."

### 9.1 Round 1 — DESIGN.md (ledger S5.371; ~38 KB; notes/, out/ incl. renders/ 27 PNGs; numpy models; rates = ADAPTIVE conditional entropy held ≤ today's coded rate ÷ 1.10 (static-tANS overhead ASSUMED); the model uses one step per frame, not the specified rate control). Status tags MEASURED / ARGUED / ESTIMATED.
**Design elements and reasons.**
Round-1 headline claim (§0, later overturned by real code lengths): "VMAF-NEG ≥ today on every measured cell with the adopted config … Mean PSNR higher on every plane of every point: Y +0.8..+4.0 dB, Cb +0.6..+1.1, Cr +0.4..+1.3". Model method (§2.2): "the model decodes the whole frame as one continuous pyramid and the packetised decoder produces the identical picture" — round 1 never ran real per-slice packets. Formats (§2.1): Y, Cb, Cr coded as three independent planes (no cross-plane transform → legality per plane; RGB out of scope, B2); B = 8/10/12 (16 later), all integer, widths grow ≤ 3 bits over B; [lo, hi] per plane a stream-header parameter (full [0, 2^B−1], SDI-legal [2^(B−8), 2^B−1−2^(B−8)], or any narrower range; nothing else depends on it); 4:4:4 and 4:2:2 chroma use the luma structure at their own width, 4:2:0 chroma Lv = 1 so its look-ahead stays within the luma look-ahead; SDR/HDR (PQ/HLG) transparent.
1. **No transform unit ends at a slice edge.** Vertical transform = Haar-PAIR pyramid (pair mean + predicted pair difference, "2/6 / TS form"), 5H × 2V; its split never straddles a 4-row block; only the difference predictor reads neighbouring blocks' means; every block's neighbour means are sent one step earlier than the block that needs them — for EVERY block, not only at slice edges → a slice edge is an ordinary block edge; slices = packet/rate units only. Block = 2^Lv rows (Lv = 2: 4 rows; 4:2:0 chroma Lv = 1: 2 chroma rows = 4 luma rows). Unit b order for every block: (i) level-1 vertical-lowpass bands (LH1, LL coarse chain) of block b+2; (ii) level-1 vertical-difference bands (HL1, HH1) of block b+1; (iii) level-0 vertical-lowpass band (LH0) of block b+1; (iv) level-0 vertical-difference bands (HL0, HH0) of block b → after unit b block b is final. Slice S = 4 at 720p-class, 8 at 1080p and above (round 1). Look-ahead to emit unit b: source rows to the end of block b+2 = 8 lines (Lv = 2).
2. Pair step (rounding offset r = (i + j) mod 2, i = pair index, j = position across; alternating so the mean is unbiased, T9): analysis m = floor((a + b + r)/2), d = a − b, d' = d − P(m) (only d' and the coarsest means coded); synthesis s = 2m + ((d + r) & 1) − r, a = (s + d)/2, b = (s − d)/2; P(m)_i = floor((16·m[i−1] − 16·m[i+1] + 32)/64) ("2/6" slope predictor, both axes). Per 2-D level synthesis order: horizontal pair synthesis of (LL, LH) → vertical lowpass rows L_v; horizontal of (HL, HH) → predicted vertical differences D_v′; D_v = D_v′ + P_v(L_v); vertical pair synthesis (L_v, D_v). Coarsest band (LL after 5 horizontal levels, one coefficient per 32 × 4 samples) DPCM from its left neighbour (column 0 from mid-range). Picture edges replicate.
3. **Legality in synthesis (Lemmas 1–2)** ("a clip is not an option anyway — it is what breaks exactness"): for boxes [A0,A1], [B0,B1] and offset r, the set of d giving a ∈ [A0,A1], b ∈ [B0,B1] is r = 0: [max(2(A0−m)−1, 2(m−B1)), min(2(A1−m), 2(m−B0)+1)]; r = 1: [max(2(A0−m), 2(m−B1)−1), min(2(A1−m)+1, 2(m−B0))]; non-empty whenever m lies in Lemma 2's interval [floor((A0+B0+r)/2), floor((A1+B1+r)/2)]. Decoder clamps every mean-type coefficient into its Lemma-2 interval, every difference into its Lemma-1 interval, top-down; legal for ANY bit string; intervals depend only on final values → no cycle, one pass, a compare-and-clamp per coefficient. "Why the clamp never works against the picture": the interval is exactly the set of legal outcomes given the fixed mean; the overshooting sample moves toward its box; the pair's lowpass untouched. Honest limit (SA15): "with boxes" vs "clip" decodes differ in samples far from any clamp (closed loop); per-sample ~50/50 better/worse; the claim is zero cost in bits and dB, not per-sample dominance.
4. Quantisation: q = sign(x) floor(|x|/D + 0.45) (0.45 adopted over 1/3, T10), R(q) = round(qD); division = table reciprocal as shift-adds (8 steps per octave) + one compare; D = W_band · 2^(s/8); W_band = synthesis-gain weight (fixed table); s = the **step field**: knots on a grid of regions (block rows × 256-column strips), bilinear interpolation between knot centres (continuous by construction); knots are the only rate-control output.
5. **Lattice-locked (index-domain) temporal prediction:** reconstruction always v = clamp(p + R(q), [vlo, vhi]); inter transmits r = q − h; hint h = canonical index of x_mc (the same quantity on the MC picture with the same reconstructed-mean predictors); encoder choice of q: exact index if x exactly representable (always on a decoded input); else h if |x − v(h)| ≤ (1/2 + 1/4) D (temporal hysteresis: still content keeps its indices); else the dead-zone index. Motion: 16×16 luma blocks, integer-pel, vectors estimated by EVERY encoder from its two previous decoded frames (block match D(t−1) vs D(t−2), constant-motion assumption), TRANSMITTED; OBMC per pixel bilinear blend of the four nearest block vectors, dyadic weights (sum 256). v never depends on the reference → vectors and reference influence BITS only. Hint-free coding (h = 0) for frame 0, scene cuts (per slice: whichever of hinted/hint-free is cheaper — both costs exact in the same pass), refresh units. Measured vs value-domain prediction (T3 vs T3b, same model, matched entropy): dng720 @0.5 f6 38.41/37.01/38.01 at 0.432 bpp vs 38.08/36.79/37.87 at 0.425; @1.0 f7 40.08/38.63/39.27 at 0.830 vs 39.66/38.24/38.94 at 0.862; spot @1.0 f3 45.04/45.70/48.53 vs 44.72/45.38/48.23 → equal or better.
5b. **Generation-exactness argument (§4, ARGUED + MEASURED):** (a) integer invertibility — the pair step is a bijection on integers for any r, so the analysis of a decoded picture returns exactly the reconstructed means and differences (clamped ones included); (b) canonical indices — for an unclamped v, Q(v − p) = q because |R(q) − qD| ≤ ½ < min(0.45, 0.55)·D for D ≥ 2 (enforced); for a clamped v the canonical index is the smallest-magnitude index whose reconstruction reaches that boundary, which the first encoder emits; (c) canonical plan — knots = coarsest candidates under which every coefficient is representable, applied by gen 1 to its own reconstruction; (d) reference independence (lattice lock) — the picture is D(q, knots); hints, vectors, reference and refresh phase enter only r = q − h (bits). Hence baseband hop E(D1) → same q, knots → D2 = D1 whatever D2's reference, vectors or refresh phase; bits identical when histories are identical (by induction from frame 0); CBR hop the same plus the fit (the background-refresh reservation is accounted in full by every encoder, so a different sweep phase cannot push gen 2 over budget); unlimited generations D_n = D_1. Inline test: "the lattice-locked plane codec reproduces a decoded input exactly with a DIFFERENT reference and with NO reference". Round-1 join design: a new encoder's first frame has no reference and must code hint-free (usually over budget at gen 1's knots), so its first frames are a ramp at coarser knots; then a SYNC SWEEP codes one slice per frame top-down at the upstream knots (exact whatever its reference), funded by coding the not-yet-synced slices coarser; bound slices-per-frame + 2 frames; the 2.06× first-frame cost (T7) is what the sweep must fund — the sweep itself not modelled.
6. Entropy: static tANS; symbol q (hint-free) or r (hinted), per band class, 9 contexts from magnitude classes (0, 1, ≥ 2) of the left and upper coded symbols; |symbol| > 15 escapes to Exp-Golomb; tables fixed (trained offline per band class × context × mode); one tANS state per packet, flushed at packet end. Model measured adaptive conditional entropy; static-table cost ASSUMED +10 % (prior designers ×1.04..×1.16).
7. Resolution conversion outside the core (raster-clocked converter, charge reach + 1 lines, reach 6/9/12/18); its negative lobes can leave [lo, hi], the converter clips — after the decoder, not in any generation loop.
8. Rate control (DESIGNED, not modelled): CBR prefix bound (no overdraft); per slice one pass: magnitude histogram per band class from open-loop coefficients; for each candidate knot shift (−2..+2 ladder steps per region, 1/8 octave, continuity-limited) bit estimate = histogram · static code-length table; pick the finest that fits budget + credit − margin; code once; carry the exact difference. **Still-region rule** (needed by lattice lock): the knot of a region whose previous-frame symbols were all hint-kept (r = 0) may only stay or become exactly one octave finer (nested), never coarsened; refinement frames quantise fresh. **Terminal guard** (never flat): if the exact count would break the prefix bound, the rest of the slice coded as r = 0 (hint-kept) = the MC lattice prediction, "not an LL-only rung". Plan recovery: knots of a decoded input = coarsest candidates under which every coefficient is exactly representable (parallel lattice test over ≤ 5 candidates); gen-1 self-read.
Small design facts (§2.5, §2.8, §2.9): prediction from the previous decoded frame only (C2); intra (frame 0, cut) slices "have no hint; they are the ramp and are sized with a larger margin"; one-way links are "detected automatically when no reports arrive"; the unspent remainder of the refresh reservation (< one block's refresh overhead) is padding.
9. Loss: a lost packet loses slice k's units (its rows + look-ahead means of the next 8 rows); conceal with r = 0; damage spreads only through motion (≤ 16 rows/frame). On-demand refresh (back channel; "owner ruling 2026-09-04"): the encoder codes the affected slices (lost slice ± ceil((16 × RTT_frames + 8)/S) slices) hint-free choosing the SAME indices → refreshed region bit-identical immediately, encoder picture unchanged (no pop); recovery = round trip + 1 frame. One-way: bit-metered background sweep (consecutive blocks coded hint-free until R_ref bits are spent; clean-region rule for vectors above the front; frame budget always accounts the full R_ref so any encoder's sweep phase fits); recovery bound N_blocks × (max per-block refresh overhead)/R_ref frames.
**Round-1 measurements (T1–T10).**
- T1 per-sample-clamp family (closed-loop predict-only pyramid; notes/t1_po_vs_53.py, t1b.py; out/t1b_*.txt): −1.4…−5.0 dB Y, chroma to −7.7 dB vs 5/3 at matched entropy → DROPPED (agrees with S5.212/S5.349).
- T2 pair pyramid (2/6) with decoder boxes, intra (notes/tsx.py, t2.py; out/t2_*.txt): oob 0; gen-2 identical; within ±0.3 dB of 5/3 (dng720 −0.11 Y @0.78 bpp, +0.1 @1.43; spot −0.27/−0.10/+0.07 @0.45).
- T3 sequence value-domain inter (notes/cpp.py, seq.py; out/seq_*.txt): oob 0; gen-2 0 differing samples, identical bits (dng720, gfx, both rates). T3b lattice-locked (TMODE=idx; out/seqidx_*.txt): adopted.
- T4 causal vertical predictor / 3 levels (t2.py; out/t4_*.txt, rowstats_dng720_b0.5_v26cL3.txt): +2..9 % intra bits and a period-8 special row (+5..9 % mean |error| on phases 7 and 15) → REJECTED (design-created special row, not patched).
- T5 legality cost vs "no legality + clip" (notes/t5_harm.py): bits ratio 0.999–1.006 (DESIGN §3: 0.998–1.006) at operating rates; PSNR equal within 0.03 dB at 0.3–2.7 bpp on dng720 and cf_gfx; at 0.2 bpp on cf_gfx +5.6 % Y bits (−4 % chroma) from the boundary-preference rule, "which is to be restricted to exact-target cases (canonicality needs only that)" (Risk 6 fix).
- T6 still input (dng720 frame 8 repeated; notes/t678.py still): 0 changed samples / 0.0000 bpp every frame after the first, all planes, at constant step; a one-octave refinement changes the picture toward the source (PSNR +2.8 dB) and without the fresh-quantisation rule settles over two more frames (1.5 % then 0.02 % of samples); a coarsening requantises (why 2.8 forbids coarsening still regions).
- T7 mid-stream join (t678.py join; out/t7_join.txt; dng720 @0.5, no budget cap): joiner (no history) at frame 4: 0 differing samples from its FIRST frame, all planes; bits 2.06× upstream on that frame (hint-free), 0.97× next, identical from the 3rd (vectors equal). Sync sweep (not modelled): bound slices-per-frame + 2 frames.
- T8 packet loss + on-demand refresh (t678.py loss; out/t8_loss.txt, t8_loss_rm3.txt): slice 20 lost at f4 (dng720 @0.5): damage rows 314–341, spreads to 298–357 by f5; refresh at f6 (RTT 2) of slices 17–23 hint-free → decoder == encoder bit-exact from f6 (0/0/0), encoder picture unchanged, refresh frame 1.29× bits; a refresh of only slices 19–21 left 1–4k dirty samples that RE-GREW → refresh must cover lost slice + motion spread × RTT + 8 look-ahead rows.
- T9 DC bias: floor rounding −0.07…−0.21 code; position-alternating rounding −0.01…+0.02 intra, −0.02/+0.02/−0.01 over frames 2–11 (§5.3: floor −0.07/−0.10/−0.14 intra dng720, lattice-locked inter −0.08/−0.08/−0.15; alternating −0.013/+0.021/−0.013 at identical bits and PSNR). Root: floor in m = floor((a+b)/2) loses the odd half-code whenever the difference quantises to an even value.
- T10 NEG levers (DZ = 0.45 / THETA = 0; out/ev_*dz45*.txt, ev_dng720_1.0_th0.txt): offset 0.45 NEG +0.3..+0.5 over 1/3; THETA = 0 (no hysteresis) worse (NEG 93.46); config B adopted.
- Row phases (§5.1; notes/rowstats.py — per plane, for every row phase of a 16-row slice: mean |error|, mean signed error and the row-to-row step mean |e(r+1) − e(r)|; frame 0 = intra, frames 2–11 inter, refresh rows separate; "Value domain and @1.0: same picture (out/rowstats_dng720_b*_val.txt)"; dng720 @0.5 lattice-locked, out/rowstats_dng720_b0.5_idx0.25.txt): mean |error| spread across 16 phases 3.1/1.2/1.9 % inter, 4.6/2.9/2.9 % intra; step spread 2.5/2.7/1.8 % inter, 3.2/2.4/2.9 % intra; phase 15 inside the band every plane; only ±1..2 % period-2 alternation of the step (inside-pair vs across-pair). §5.6 (out/rowstats_block_vs_slice.txt; the L arms' SOURCE chroma has a period-4 row structure, +10..+21 % at phases 3, 7, 11, 15, from their 4:2:0 master → test slice edge (ph15) vs internal 4-row edges (ph3/7/11)): spot @0.5 mean |e| −0.2/−0.3/−0.4 %, row step −0.4/−0.5/−0.5 %, intra within ±0.8 %; spot @1.0 −0.2/−0.2/−0.0 %, −0.4/−0.5/−0.3 %, ±0.8 %; volley @0.5 −0.1/+0.1/+0.9 %, −0.3/−0.5/−0.4 %, intra Cb/Cr mean |e| +1.6/+2.1 % (one frame); floor @0.5 −0.2/−0.0/−0.2 %, −0.2/−0.3/−0.5 %, ±0.4 %; dng1080 @1.0 −0.1/−0.2/−0.1 %, −0.3/−0.4/−0.2 %, ±0.5 %; cf_gfx @0.5 +0.3/+0.4/+0.9 %, +0.3/−0.3/+1.1 %, ±2.1 % (small picture). Today on spot @0.5: Cr row step phase 15 +48 % vs +16..+19 % at 3/7/11 (excess ~+30 %), Cb +24 % vs +9..+11 % (~+13 %). dng1080 (no source period-4 structure) no 4-row block signature (phases 3/7/11/15 at −2..+0.6 %).

- Latency (§6.1, ESTIMATED; vs "JPEG XS reference: ~32 lines end to end"; "Deterministic: every term is a fixed number of lines; no retry can lengthen a slice (one pass)"; T = S (capture) + 8 (look-ahead) + S × total/active lines (one slice period) + 2 (pipeline); conversion = reach + 1 lines): 720p50 18.2 lines, 0.484 ms codec, +3:2 0.751, +2:1 0.831, +3:1 0.991 (OMC today same model 0.489); 720p60 18.2, 0.404, 0.626, 0.693, 0.826 (0.407); 1080p50 26.3, 0.468, 0.646, 0.699, 0.806 (0.616); 1080p60 26.3, 0.390, 0.538, 0.583, 0.672 (0.514); 2160p50 0.234, 0.323, 0.350, 0.403 (0.308); 2160p60 0.195, 0.269, 0.291, 0.336 (0.257); 4320p50 0.117, 0.161, 0.175, 0.201 (0.154). Tightest: 720p50 + 3:1 0.991 ms vs today 0.996.
- Work (§6.2, ESTIMATED): decoder tANS 1 LUT + ~3 A; dequant ~3 A; hint (forward pair step of the MC picture ~4 A + quantise ~5 A); predictor 2 A; box interval 6 A + clamp 2 A; pair synthesis 3 A; OBMC ~11 A → ~40 A + 1 LUT per sample. Encoder: forward analysis source + MC ~8 A; quantise + hint + hysteresis ~12 A; decoder loop ~20 A; rate histogram 1 A; plan-recovery lattice test 10 A; motion search (~⅓ of the encoder; luma only, chroma reuses vectors) on decoded frames ±16 pel at half resolution, 289 candidates per 8×8 half-res block + 9-point refinement ~90 A per full-res luma sample (~45 A per 4:2:2 sample) → ~135 A per sample. JPEG XS (ESTIMATED): ~40 A encode (5/3 2V ~12, quantisation/GCLI/budget ~20, packing ~5), ~25 A decode → CPP decoder ~1.6× XS, encoder ~3.4× XS. ZU7EV (8K60 4:2:2 3.98 Gsample/s → 10 lanes at 400 MHz): encoder ~1350 adders (~20 LUT each) ~27 k + motion SAD trees and control ~20 k + 10 tANS encoders (~1.5 k LUT and 2 BRAM each) ~15 k + rate/plan ~10 k ≈ 70–80 k LUT of 230 k (30–35 %), 0 DSP; decoder ~40 k LUT (17 %); line buffers ~16 rows × 7680 × 2 × 12 bit ~3 Mbit + motion window cache ±16 rows + block: 41 rows ~7.6 Mbit in URAM; reference in DDR: 2 × 3.98 G × 12 bit ~96 Gbit/s at 8K60 ≈ 62 % of one 64-bit DDR4-2400 (154 Gbit/s peak) — "8K60 therefore needs the PL DDR interface in addition to PS DDR"; 4K60 ~24 Gbit/s; ≤ 4K60 everything < 10 % of the part.
- IP (§8): Haar 1910 / S-transform 1980s; 2/6 TS form Zandi et al. CREW (Ricoh 1995), Said & Pearlman S+P 1996, Villasenor et al. 1995 (patents filed 1994–96 expired); lifting Sweldens 1996, Calderbank et al. 1998; position-alternating rounding own; decoder legal intervals own (related to SA12's per-pair clamp); canonical boundary index own (memo 011 §2.2 form); lattice-locked prediction own in this form (motion-compensated context modelling / conditional coding of indices, 1990s; "not an MPEG/AVC/HEVC tool; no coded residual in the value domain"); H.263 "named only as the dating source"; tANS "used by zstd; no rANS, no CABAC"; OBMC Orchard & Sullivan 1994, H.263 Annex F 1996; block matching 1970s–80s; tANS (Duda 2013/14); dead zone, DPCM; line/precinct-based continuous wavelet with slices as coding units: JPEG 2000 Part 1 precincts, Chrysafis & Ortega 1998–2000 — the resemblance to JPEG XS's architecture must go to the legal team BEFORE building (elements predate XS 2019).
- Prior failures (§9): mirror + blend; closed slices (SA12); anchor row (SA13); shifted causal (SA14); continuous 5/3 (S5.349: pair look-ahead 8 lines not 9–21; S = 4 at 720p; 4:2:0 Lv = 1; memory "owner ruling (URAM, no memory constraint)" and ~11 Mbit total; A5 damage = slice + 8 rows, refresh picture-neutral; resemblance → legal team); "slice k's clamps depend on slice k+1" (clamps depend only on already-received units); predict-only (T1); capping/repair/projection/lock (none); IDQ away-moves (no off-lattice values); rail bit cost (no rail symbol; T5 ratio ~1.000); REXT (no iteration); vertical-causal (not used); 16×16 grid, per-block mode (OBMC, no per-block mode); decoder-derived vectors (transmitted); refresh phase not in the image / interleaved refresh (irrelevant under lattice lock); clean-region rule cost 0.2 NEG (only for the one-way sweep); rung/ladder (terminal guard hint-kept, never LL-only); HVBC tiles/waxy (zeroed detail = slope prediction); floor rounding cast (T9).
- Own risks (§10): (1) rate control weakest (still-region rule unmeasured; mixed regions requantise their still part when a knot changes; guard frequency/visibility unmeasured; model used one step per frame); (2) coded rate = entropy × 1.10 assumed; (3) motion integer-pel from decoded history (one frame late), half-pel not modelled, "motion cells measured only in part"; (4) legal: XS-like architecture; (5) flatness bar not met (38–69 % flat at dng720 @0.5 vs today 42–83 %); (6) cf_gfx 0.2 bpp +5.6 % Y bits; (7) 8K60 DDR ~62 % of one DDR4-2400 (needs the PL DDR port); (8) dng1080 @1.0 luma flatness 41 % vs 27 % ("the owner's eye must judge that cell first"); (9) model gaps (legality boxes exercised only at full 10-bit range; scheduled one-slice-per-frame refresh coded with hint-free CHOICES; no 4:2:0/4:4:4/8/12-bit/limited sequence; hwy not run; half-pel absent); (10) refresh must cover the full damage footprint.
**Coordinator review round 1 (S5.371):** coordinator's look at dng720 f8 Cr level maps (CPP vs today) and CPP Cr row profile: noise-like, no slice-line pattern in the profile; level maps saturated, no legend → unreadable for fine steps. Holds: root-level seam removal, legality from final values, reference-independent reconstruction, still 0, smudge tools 0, NEG ≥ today, latency ≤ XS. Round 2 items: (1) model the rate control on all cells + cut + mixed still/moving; guard firing = design-created visible region → must be impossible by construction; intra-doesn't-fit rule; still-region rule must not starve active regions; (2) A5 one-way sweep with loss, no back channel (cite or drop the "2026-09-04 back channel" ruling), damage must not re-enter refreshed rows, bound in frames; (3) join under exact CBR; (4) static tables trained on disjoint footage, real code lengths, restate efficiency; (5) config B everywhere + highwaydriveL + worst frame; (6) dng1080 @1.0 luma flatness regression root cause; (7) legality zero cost at 0.2 bpp and toward/away-from-source counts; (8) 4:2:0 (Lv = 1 row phases), 4:4:4, 8/12-bit, limited range, cut24/ext10 rails; (9) half-pel; (10) legends, ±2-code scale, ×4 crops; (11) C3 margin at 0.991 ms, DDR vs today. ### 9.2 Round 2 — DESIGN_v2.md + RESUME.md (ledger S5.372 snapshot and addenda 1–8, S5.373)
New in round 2: every rate = REAL CODE LENGTH from static tables (tANS-exact within table quantisation: 1/2048 probability resolution, 11-bit state flush per plane per packet, Exp-Golomb escapes, 16-bit slice header, vectors coded); tables trained on footage disjoint from every test cell. Scripts notes/cpp2.py (plane codec v2), seq2.py (sequence codec: rate control, refresh, decoder simulation), run2.py, eval2.py (per-frame NEG cross-checked = negscore.sh 88.858), loss2.py, join2.py, rowstats2.py, render2.py, t12_harm.py, train.py; logs out/r2/log/, results out/r2/.
**Design changes (root causes, per SA15).**
1. **Open-loop symbols** (quantised from the picture's own TS coefficients; the decoder adds the reconstructed predictor). Why: with closed-loop symbols a candidate step's bits depend on its reconstruction → exact CBR needs trial coding or a guard; open-loop makes every candidate knot's exact code length computable in one pass (XS-style budget evaluation). Exactness unchanged (a decoded picture's own predictors ARE the decoder's). Cost (T11, intra, matched rate, out/t11_openloop.txt): −0.07/−0.08/−0.07 dB dng720, −0.02/−0.03/−0.03 spot, −0.08/−0.08/−0.09 cf_gfx.
2. **No per-coefficient state; still content kept by knot stability.** Per-coefficient step memory was BUILT then REJECTED after the 8K ruling (7 bits of state per coefficient = +14 bit/sample DDR traffic and frame-sized storage; notes/*_stepmemory_rejected.py). Root: memory existed only because a knot change requantises still content → a kept value lives on the CURRENT field (v_keep = R(Q(t_mc, D), D)); the rate controller keeps a slice's knot from the previous frame whenever it fits (and is at most half an octave coarser than the finest fitting one). Keep decision: exact reproduction first (decoded input), then keep if |t − v_keep| ≤ 0.75 D or the kept value is closer than the requantised one.
3. **Exact CBR without a guard** (R1; intra slices: "Frames 0–1 are the permitted ramp"): prefix bound, no overdraft; per-slice allowance (1 − ρ) B − (vector bits share) − 49 header/flush bits; ρ = 1/16 refresh reservation; unspent reservation beyond one frame's worth returns to credit at frame end. One knot per slice (1/8-octave units); the step of a coefficient row ramps linearly inside the slice from the previous slice's knot to this slice's knot; ahead data use this slice's field ("band lag": LL/H/LH1 8 rows, HL1/HH1/LH0 4 rows, HL0/HH0 0) → a slice's reconstruction is final after its own packet. Exact lanes, one pass: previous knot + {−64..+64}, 25 candidates (RESUME: "13-knot ladder") [SOURCES DISAGREE: 13 vs 25 candidates (MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS "about 25"); the spacing inside ±64 (1/8-octave units) is given by neither], exact code length from open-loop symbols (static tables, contexts incl. the row above); choice = finest candidate with cost ≤ target (allowance + credit/16), else ≤ hard budget (all credit). Kept coefficients cost the 0-symbol at any knot → the coarsest candidate is also the cheapest, exactly known. "No guard exists": when content needs more than the pipe the knot rises (continuously ramped); in the limit the whole slice is coded at the coarsest step (keeps its MC lattice values, or intra becomes very soft) — "the codec at its rate limit, not a mode"; reported per cell as "overflow" and "jump>+8" (design statement; in fact only the rail runs report overflow, "1–3 slices per run" — no overflow or jump>+8 count was ever reported for the natural, motion, cut or mixed cells, and the round-2 review item "still-region rule must not starve active regions" was never measured). Intra slices: same lanes, ladder allows +8 octaves in one slice, floor cost = LL DPCM at the coarsest step. Measured deviation actual vs lane: 0 to ±50 bits per slice natural/graphics; on rail plates up to ~2 slice budgets over a frame (cut24 @1.0 prefix bound exceeded by up to 2,183 bits; one slice at 1.0 bpp 256 wide = 2,048 bits) — OPEN (lanes ignore clamp relabels); on the final design cut24 @1.0 held (minimum credit +2,469 bits; the earlier build overshot by 3.4k).
   **[SOURCES DISAGREE — stale text]** DESIGN_v2 R1 still says "Still-region rule by construction: step memory (change 2 above)" and the R8 proof lists "the step-memory state" among the reading's inputs, although change 2 REJECTED the per-coefficient step memory (kept values live on the current field; knot stability instead); the R1/R8 wording was not updated.
4. **Canonical plan reading with legal intervals** (R8): the knot of slice s = the coarsest ladder candidate under which every coefficient is reproducible (legal interval from the picture's own pyramid; boundary value reproducible by the reaching index; kept value by its hint) and whose exact cost fits; if every candidate reproduces, the knot is k_{s−1}. Gen 1: code by rate, reconstruct, apply the reading with the same k_{s−1}, emit the returned knot (picture unchanged). Lemma by induction (no slice's reading depends on anything below it; one reading per slice = parallel lattice test over the ladder, 25 comparators per coefficient; at most one re-description). **One-slice BACKTRACK REMOVED** (notes/seq2_backtrack_rejected.py) after coordinator intervention (07:45): a search + rework of an emitted slice; its trigger was a model defect (ahead data used the next slice's field) + a snapshot state-aliasing bug. Model note: whole-frame coding emulates per-slice re-description; "reading_fixes" 0 on every run.
5. **Canonical index = smallest-magnitude index that reproduces the value** (fixes the cf_gfx 0.2 bpp +5.6 %; removes the source-side boundary-preference rule).
6. **Canonical motion vectors** — final: block match of D(t−1) vs D(t−2), computed WHILE frame t−1 is coded (its rows as reconstructed vs the resident t−2 band) → no extra reference read, stored per block. Abandoned variant: search the current picture and re-describe with vectors searched on the reconstruction (emitted-vector costs differ from those the knot was chosen with → extra plan-reading passes). **[SOURCES DISAGREE]** RESUME §3 rejected list: "Motion from D(t−1) vs D(t−2): extra reference read (8K DDR). Replaced by current-picture search + canonical re-description" — ledger S5.373: "Vectors final = D(t−1) vs D(t−2) measured during t−1 (RESUME rejected-list entry stale)"; MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS §1 says the same.
7. **Implementable tables:** 10 band classes (per luma/chroma: LL, horizontal-only levels, level 1, level-0 HH, level-0 LH/HL) × 2 modes (hinted/hint-free) × 3 contexts, L = 1024: 60 tables, 1.1 Mbit (first round-2 set: 720 tables of L = 2048 = 26.5 Mbit, not implementable). Sets: tables_A (all 7 training clips) and tables_B (5 clips, excludes floorballtrainL/highwayviewL) = primary.
- S = 4 at 720p, 8 at 1080p/2160p, 4 at 4320p (R11b).
**Round-2 measurements.**
- **R7 legality cost and harm** (notes/t12_harm.py, out/t12_harm.txt; same open-loop choices decoded with and without the legal intervals, "unconstrained" = no boxes then clipped; all planes pooled): cf_gfx at 3.05 / 1.41 / 0.67 / 0.36 / 0.17 bpp: bits ratio 1.0000 / 1.0000 / 1.0000 / 1.0000 / 1.0004; samples changed by legality 4 / 8 / 20 / 215 / 1,701; toward/away 3/1, 5/3, 8/12, 126/89, 843/856; max excess over clip 11 / 6 / 41 / 43 / 175 codes; MSE equal (< 0.6 %). dng720 at 2.28 / 0.99 / 0.49 / 0.26 / 0.12: 1.0000 / 1.0000 / 1.0000 / 1.0001 / 1.0025; 16 / 13 / 312 / 2,733 / 8,955; 9/7, 7/5, 187/125, 1,473/1,250, 4,507/4,427; 9 / 5 / 72 / 130 / 259; equal. cut24 at 2.23 / 1.63 / 1.09 / 0.60 / 0.30: 1.019 / 1.038 / 1.103 / 1.088 / 1.136; 21k–30k changed; ~3:1 toward; 12 / 35 / 51 / 130 / 213; MSE −1 % / +12 % / +15 % / −1 % / −8 %. ext10 at 0.67 / 0.52 / 0.40 / 0.29 / 0.09: 1.144 / 1.112 / 1.159 / 1.416 / 2.117; 86k–99k; ~2:1 toward; 12 / 27 / 30 / 209 / 395; MSE −17 % / −19 % / −34 % / +4 % / +9 %. Reading: natural + graphics zero bit cost 0.12–3 bpp, equal MSE; the round-1 +5.6 % is gone (root: the source-side "prefer a clamped neighbour index" rule, removed). Rail plates +2..+16 % bits at contribution rates (+40..+110 % at 0.1–0.3 bpp; memo: +112 %); root: a source sample exactly ON a rail sits on its coefficient's interval boundary; the dead-zone index may fall one step short, so the encoder spends the reaching index (value then exact at the rail; MSE 17–34 % LOWER at 0.4–0.7 bpp on ext10). Boundary-snap reconstruction BUILT and REJECTED (notes/cpp2_snap_rejected.py): snaps coarse-band means to the rails, MSE × 7.7 at 0.12 bpp dng720. **"Never away" NOT met:** every clamp keeps the pair mean, so the partner sample moves by the same amount the other way; away when the pair mean itself was quantised toward the rail; ~50/50 on natural (13–312 samples/frame at ≥ 0.5 bpp), ~2–3:1 toward on rail plates; largest excess over a clip 5–72 codes at ≥ 0.5 bpp, 259–395 at 0.1 bpp. SA15: "structural for any mean-preserving (exactness-preserving) legaliser; the only per-sample-harmless legaliser is a clip, and a clip is what breaks exactness (round 1, T1: the clip-compatible family costs 1.4–5 dB)". Coordinator (S5.372): NOT accepting "structural" yet; memo F5: "unproven".
- Ledger S5.372 snapshot (11:30): one-way loss ρ 1/4 (previous build) 0 damaged samples once the sweep front passed (t = 13); first real-table gfx @0.5 frames ≈ today's level (30.4/33.1/33.1 by frame 2 vs today 30.11/33.45/33.41).
- **R11 latency margin and DDR:** SA15 corrected the review's clause: "A2 bar is sub-1 ms including conversion (the coordinator cites C3's margin; C3 is the FPGA-resource margin clause, A2 the latency clause)"; Lv = 1 on the 3:1 leg would be "a per-format transform depth fixed by the format pair, like slice height — not a profile". 720p50 + 3:1 = 18.2 + 19 lines = 0.991 ms, margin 0.009 ms (0.9 %). T13 (out/t13_lv.txt): Lv = 1 at 720p-class (look-ahead 4 lines): 14.2 + 19 lines = 0.885 ms (11.5 % margin) at −0.40/−0.27/−0.26 dB (dng720) and −0.33/−0.27/−0.24 dB (cf_gfx) intra; Lv = 2 with 2:1 or 3:2 conversion 0.831 / 0.751 ms (17 % / 25 %). Recommendation Lv = 2 everywhere; 3:1 leg alone Lv = 1 (not taken; owner accepted 0.991). DDR (13-bit packed store, window cache, read amplification 1.00): today = 26 bit/sample; step-memory build = 26 + 14 (7-bit knot state per coefficient, write + read) + ~3 (search on the previous TWO decoded frames at half resolution) = ~43 bit/sample, 1.65× today; 4320p60 4:2:2 171 Gbit/s vs today 103; two x64 DDR4-3200 (410 Gbit/s peak) at 42 %; 2160p60 43 Gbit/s on one x64 DDR4-2400 (154 peak), 28 %. (Superseded by R11b after the owner's 8K ruling.)
- **R11b** why it fits: "The difference from today is the slice height (S = 4–8 rows instead of 16–32: CPP's slices are rate units only, so they can be short without a seam cost)"; tightest 8K 4:4:4 12-bit at 83 % URAM = 17 % margin; coefficient buffer uses today's single-buffer schedule (hints written first, residual added in place); "8K rows are cheap in time: 4320p60 line = 3.7 µs".
- **R11b on-chip memory and DDR at every format** (ESTIMATED, method of DDR_WINDOW_CACHE §7.3): reference band ring (URAM) = S + 8 look-ahead + 8 analysis support + 2 × 16 vector range + 8 OBMC overlap = S + 56 rows at the store width (depth + 1, the narrowed store); one coefficient buffer S + 8 rows × 16 bit; capture/output line buffers (BRAM) 2 S rows; tANS tables 60 × 1024 × 18 bit = 1.1 Mbit; +0.5 Mbit misc; encoder adds source look-ahead (S + 12 rows) and computes source coefficients twice (lanes pass and coding pass). S = 8 at 1080p/2160p, S = 4 at 4320p (latency 26.3 → 22.1 lines). Table (band / coef / decoder URAM of 27 / decoder BRAM of 11 / encoder URAM / encoder BRAM / today decoder URAM, Mbit): 1080p60 4:4:4 12b S8 4.79 / 1.47 / 6.27 (23 %) / 2.71 (25 %) / 7.76 (29 %) / 2.71 (25 %) / 8.73; 2160p60 4:2:2 10b S8 5.41 / 1.97 / 7.37 (27 %) / 2.83 (26 %) / 8.90 (33 %) / 2.83 / 13.61; 2160p60 4:4:4 12b S8 9.58 / 2.95 / 12.53 (46 %) / 3.82 (35 %) / 15.30 (57 %) / 3.82 / 20.43; 4320p60 4:2:2 10b S4 10.14 / 2.95 / 13.09 (48 %) / 2.83 (26 %) / 15.55 (58 %) / 2.83 / 24.25 (narrowed store); 4320p60 4:4:4 12b S4 17.97 / 4.42 / 22.39 (83 %; "22.4" in MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS) / 3.82 (35 %) / 22.39 (83 %) / 8.24 (75 %, source rows in BRAM) / 40.85 (does not fit). DDR: reference write 1× + read 1× through the same window cache (read amplification 1.00×; motion search reads the same resident band) = 26 bit/sample = today; no state, no second frame; same memory subsystem incl. single-channel 8K (today's margin +38.7 µs at 4320p60). NOT re-simulated with the document's adversarial-locality model (open).
- **Gen-2 on the final code:** cf_gfx @0.5 12 frames: identical pictures and identical bits every frame, reading re-descriptions 0 (out/r2/log/gfx_0.5.txt); cut24 @1.0 12 frames identical. **SA15 never ran generation 3 or 10** (MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS S3: "Not run: g3/g10"; BRIEF_SA18 §3: "g3/g10 never run"). RESUME §4: the g2 check was enabled only on the dng720 0.5, floor 0.5, gfx 0.5, cut and mixed jobs (+ rails and formats); the gfx 0.5/1.0 efficiency jobs were queued but never reported in R5; the today decodes for floor/volley/hwy were produced by SA15 (out/today/{floor,volley,hwy}_b{0.5,1.0}.d.yuv).
- **Rails:** previous build (step memory, before canonical vectors; out/r2/rails_summary.txt): gen-2 identical on cut24 @0.5/@1.0 (12 frames), ext10 full, ext10 limited (64–940/64–960), ext8, ext12 4:2:2, ext12 4:4:4 (4 frames each); 0 oob; prefix bound exceeded on rail plates by 1.6k–3.4k bits (cut24) and much more on the ext extremes. Final design (out/r2/rails_final.txt): all 7 g2-identical, 0 oob, prefix bound held (minimum credit +1.1k..+25k bits); rate limit (coarsest knot, "overflow") in 1–3 slices per run.
- **A5 one-way:** previous build, floor720 (Lanczos 720p floorballgameL), loss of slice 60 at frame 3, ρ = 1/4: damage grew to rows 198–305 through motion, never above the refresh front once passed, decoder == encoder bit-exact from frame 13 (front advanced 20–24 rows/frame), all planes (out/r2/old/loss_rho4.txt). Refresh cost at ρ = 1/16 on floor720 @0.5 (earlier build): NEG 93.61 vs 93.88 without refresh, PSNR −0.25/−0.06/−0.05 (RESUME: "NEG −0.27"). Final design: ρ = 1/16 vs none NEG 94.16 vs 94.53 (−0.37), worst 89.81 vs 90.18, PSNR −0.22/−0.18/−0.15. **Final code, ρ = 1/4, loss of slice 60 at frame 3** (out/r2/log/loss_rho4.txt): the sweep covered rows 0–544 in frame 1 then STALLED at row 544 for the rest of the run (a 4-row block's hint-free extra exceeded one frame's reservation of 115 kbit); damage (rows 116–389 by frame 22) NEVER repaired. Root (R2): refresh is picture-neutral → a refreshed coefficient is sent as its full q instead of q − h → unit refresh cost = intra cost at the chosen knots (content-dependent) vs a reservation (rate-dependent); with a full-width 4-row block unit a detailed block at 1.0 bpp cost more than one frame's reservation (dng720 @1.0: front stuck at row 124 from frame 2). "Save up to 8 frames" rule (added 13:30) only moves the stall into a content-dependent delay → withdrawn as a design element (coordinator intervention; kept in the running queue code only). The final queue was therefore MIXED (RESUME §4–§5): jobs started before 13:30 (incl. the first final-code loss run) ran WITHOUT the save rule (the sweep stalls); jobs (re)started after 13:30 — incl. the 23 unfinished 1080p/format jobs of jobs_final6 restarted 14:15 (after an OBMC memory blow-up fix; 1080p runs had restarted 12:45 after the vector change) — ran WITH the save-up rule in the code after it had been withdrawn as a design element; several R5/R8 figures therefore come from code containing a withdrawn patch. Coordinator ruling (DESIGN_v2 R2 last bullet): "the unit redesign of R2 is to be modelled after the running queue finishes (coordinator: no restart)" — so the R2 refresh-unit redesign, octave-only refinement and join-via-refresh were never modelled (why F3/F4 are ARGUED only). Redesign (ARGUED, not modelled): cap every hint-free symbol at L_max bits (escape of fixed length; L_max = 16 at 10-bit, 18 at 12-bit); refresh unit = run of u consecutive coefficient columns of one 4-row block row, u = floor(R / L_max) with R = ρ × bpp × W × H; each frame ≥ one unit; bound T_recover ≤ 2 c L_max / (ρ bpp) + 1 frames (c = 2 for 4:2:2, 1.5 for 4:2:0, 3 for 4:4:4): 4:2:2 L_max 16: ρ 1/16 → 1025 frames at 1.0 bpp, 2049 at 0.5; ρ 1/4 → 257 / 513. Typical (earlier build, ρ 1/4) cycle ~33 frames at 720p, loss at 3 repaired by 13. Options left to the owner/coordinator: accept, raise ρ, or re-describe a refresh unit at a coarser knot (bounded cycle H/S frames, but a picture change).
- **Mid-stream join under exact CBR** (floor720 @0.5, joiner starts at frame 6 on the upstream decodes, fresh state, same rate; "picture difference shrinking by ~20 slices per frame" over frames 6–10): differing slices 167, 156, 128, 105, 86 of 180 at frames 6–10 (frame 6 = the joiner's intra ramp at its own knots); 24-frame run (out/r2/log/join24_floor720.txt): 167 (f6), 156, 128, 105, 86, 43, 26 (f12), then a plateau of 43–55 of 180 to frame 18 → does NOT converge. Root: a joiner slice whose reference differs has costlier hints, so the reading's "fits" test rejects upstream's knot and the slice is re-coded at another knot, which keeps its reference different. Needed (not modelled): the sweep re-describes refreshed units hint-free AT the read knot → join bound = refresh bound.
- **Mixed still/moving clip** (dng1080 frame 0 repeated + floorball inset rows 272–808 × cols 480–1440, @0.5): gen-2 identical; NEG 92.31 (worst 90.05); PSNR 39.30/38.95/39.55. STILL-AREA FAILURE: 40–60 % of the still background samples change every frame (frames 1–7); background mean |error| falls every frame (12.75 → 8.32 Y) = a refinement ramp; per sample ~52/48 toward/away → visible shimmer. Root: 1/8-octave knot refinement; a non-nested step change requantises every coefficient on a new lattice. Fix designed, not modelled: a knot may decrease only by exactly one octave (nested), the in-slice ramp moves by the same octave at both ends; coarsening stays free.
- **Cut sequence** (floorballgameL f0–5 then volleyballgameL f6–7, 1080p @0.5): gen-2 identical; NEG 91.13 (worst 83.09 = the frame after the cut); artifactmap on the cut frame Y 42 bright / 44 dark regions (one plane), Cb 0, Cr 1 bright region (out/r2/eval_final.txt; not inspected by eye); PSNR mean 40.28/43.78/44.18, worst 37.61/41.04/42.85; flat 35.46 %. Per-band texture-energy retention (dng720 @0.5, out/r2/flatroot_dng720.txt): the loss is in INTER frames (intra retains more fine luma energy than today: HH0 0.64 vs 0.40); level-0 luma bands 0.88–0.89 vs today 0.96–0.99. Tested: ZH (no hysteresis around zero hints — the keep hysteresis widens the dead zone for grain): floor720 flat 26.9 % vs 33.3 % (texture kept) but NEG 93.39 vs 94.16 (−0.77), PSNR −0.5 dB; dng720 @0.5 ZH NEG 88.25 → REJECTED (NEG −0.8…−2.1). SA15's reading: grain is temporally uncorrelated, inter prediction cannot carry it, and "the rate spent re-sending it competes with everything else"; today spends more luma bits on it (lower PSNR, higher NEG); candidate root: MSE-derived band-step weights, not texture-aware. OPEN.

Integer kept. (hwy half-pel still running at hand-in.)

SA15's look at the Cr strips (dng720): noise-like, no row feature.
- Round-2 one-way refresh as built (RESUME §1(g)): a bit-metered top-down sweep of hint-free symbols "incl. the ahead data of refreshed blocks"; clean-region vector rule = "blocks feeding hints above the front read only rows above the front". RESUME §5 open items not stated elsewhere: "One-way refresh cycle is slow at ρ = 1/16 (front 4–24 rows/frame at 720p) → recovery bound = 2 cycles, content dependent"; rails "one overflow on ext12 (earlier build)"; "C2 note: the encoder reads only the reference frame now (issue resolved by change (e))".
- Model bugs found and fixed (RESUME §3; not design): OBMC cached one shifted plane per distinct vector (tens of GB on motion cells, OOM kills); snapshot aliasing (restore reused mutable state); per-frame frame_info retained (OOM); ahead data on the next slice's field (band lag); gen 1 emitting pass-1 state instead of the canonical description's state. Process: queue restarted 4 times (design changes mid-queue; OOM; 7–10 parallel ~2.6 GB jobs); 39-job final queue ~12–15 h started 11:55; 1080p runs 8 frames (steady 2–7), restarted 12:45 after the vector change; jobs_final6 6 parallel after an OBMC memory blow-up; two rounds ≈ 18 h incl. three usage-limit stops.
- Ledger S5.372 addendum 7 (15:25): the Fable package prepared in `Subagents/SA16_design/` (BRIEF_SA16.md + MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS.md), finalised at SA15's hand-in. Addendum 8 (18:13): join under exact CBR NOT MET (24-frame plateau 43–55 of 180); formats 4:2:0, 4:4:4 12b, 4:2:2 8b/12b, limited range: g2 identical, 0 oob, no special row phase; R5 at that time 10 points: NEG ≥ today 3, tie 1, lower 6 (to −1.13), PSNR better 28/30; renders done (out/r2/renders/); half-pel still running; 4 jobs left.
- Coordinator actions during round 2 (ledger): 07:45 intervention removed the backtrack; RESUME.md requested because of quota (S5.372); "save up to 8 frames" withdrawn; SA15 told to keep RESUME.md + DESIGN_v2.md current after every item; S5.372 addendum 6 (no round 3).
**Hand-in and decision (S5.373, 18:50):** holds — no special row on every format/plane (0.4–2.3 % spread, incl. 4:2:0 chroma; renders ±16/±2 legends, ×4 strips); 0 oob everywhere; g2 identical pictures + bits on dng720, cf_gfx, floorball, cut clip, mixed clip, 7 rail extremes, 4:2:0, 4:4:4 12b, 4:2:2 8b/12b, limited range; zero legality cost natural + graphics; memory fits ZU7EV all formats (arithmetic); latency 18–26 lines; tables B ≥ A. FAILS — (1) NEG vs today 12 points: better 3 (dng720 @0.5 90.34 vs 90.16, spot @0.5 93.99 vs 93.25, volley @0.5 92.48 vs 92.29), tie 1 (floor @0.5 95.53 vs 95.55), worse 8 (spot @1.0 −0.15, volley @1.0 −0.50, floor @1.0 −0.84, dng720 @1.0 −1.00, dng1080 @1.0 −1.02, hwy @0.5 −1.02, dng1080 @0.5 −1.13, hwy @1.0 −1.50); worst frames lower on most; PSNR higher 34/36 → inter texture loss; dng1080 @1.0 luma flat 42 vs 27.5 %; (2) mixed clip still shimmer 40–60 %, ~52/48; (3) A5 sweep stalled at row 544, redesigned bound ≈ 1,000 frames at ρ 1/16, 1 bpp; (4) join plateau 43–55/180; (5) rails +2–16 % bits, never-away ~50/50; (6) cut frame 42–44 luma artifactmap regions uninspected. Half-pel no gain. Common root of F1–F4 per the coordinator: lattice-locked temporal prediction (it forces every inter coefficient onto the INTRA quantisation lattice, so an inter frame can never refine below the step or carry sub-step temporal detail; the 0.75Δ keep window holds values up to ¾ of a step off; still content hostage to every step change; refresh = full intra re-send). DECISION (stall rule): SA15 stopped (TaskStop, leftover jobs killed); package: MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS.md (FINAL: design, S1–S10, F1–F7 with how, 11 rejected items, hints) + BRIEF_SA16.md (§3 cross-designer table; all owner rulings; method; deliverable); SA16 spawned on Fable 5.1.
**MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS (coordinator FINAL 18:45) — framing not stated elsewhere:** §1 told the next designer to "check yourself whether vectors are transmitted or derived at the decoder: decoder-derived vectors failed loss recovery long ago (ledger S5.29)" (round 2 says "vectors coded" and "vector bits share", i.e. transmitted, without stating it as a rule). S2 how: "This contradicts the cycle argument (ledger S5.349), which assumed a separate update step" — the pair mean IS the lowpass. S7 how: "short slices become possible once slices are not transform units". S5: "Today has luma regions on 5 cells" — **[SOURCES DISAGREE]** SA15's final R5 names today's regions on 2 final-table cells (16 on dng720 @0.5, 1 on spot @0.5); the memo's 5 counts the round-1 tool table (dng720 @0.5 16, cf_gfx @0.5 35, dng1080 @0.5 6, spot @1.0 3, floor @0.5 1). F1: "PSNR up with NEG down means detail and texture are lost, not noise. The deficit grows with rate, and with the motion cells at 1.0 bpp." F2: the 40–60 % shimmer is "the same 'ants' class as today's codec (60–77 %), which the owner treats as a disqualifier"; the octave-only fix "would coarsen rate control". F3: ≈ 1,000 frames = "20 s or more. A5 requires a small, bounded number of frames"; the earlier typical case (ρ = 1/4, before the stall) repaired in 10 frames. F4: SA15's join-through-refresh fix "inherits F3's refresh problem". F6: the cut-frame regions were not inspected by eye ("the intra ramp may be exempt; see the owner's ramp rule in PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS"). §5 hints tail: lattice lock "bought still-picture stability at a constant step, exactness through joins and loss, and memory simplicity"; "SA12 showed a byte-exact mid-stream join from frame 15 with a contiguous refresh sweep plus clean-region rules"; "A design must show how rail samples cost nothing and how no sample moves away from the source." BRIEF_SA18 §3 (motion row): "integer vectors from decoded history (D(t−1) vs D(t−2), measured during t−1), no extra read".
**SA15 rejected list (RESUME §3 + memo §4):** (1) closed-loop predict-only pyramid (−1.4…−5 dB, chroma worst); (2) causal vertical predictor / 3 levels (every 8th row +5–9 %); (3) boundary-snap on rails (MSE × 7.7); (4) one-slice backtrack; (5) per-coefficient step memory (8K); (6) motion search against a second reference read (8K DDR) — replaced by the history search during t−1; (7) 720 tables L = 2048 (26.5 Mbit); (8) refresh cost charged to the rate lanes (starved slices, kmax); (9) saving the refresh reservation up to 8 frames (patch); (10) ZH keep rule (NEG −0.8…−2.1); (11) half-pel (no gain).
**Memo §5 hints (coordinator):** the two strongest results of all four designers: S1 (a slice seam is a property of the filter support, not of slices) and S2 (legality exact and free when the final synthesis step writes samples from one final value plus one leaf); F1–F4 share one root, lattice-locked prediction; open question: how can temporal prediction keep the decoded picture reproducible from the image alone, and keep refresh and joins cheap and bounded, WITHOUT forcing inter frames onto the intra lattice; value-domain prediction (SA12, today) kept texture better; F5 comes from the pair mean being preserved at a clamp.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

## §10 SA16 "CQP-1" — Clamped Quantised Pyramid, value-domain prediction (Fable 5.1, the owner's one-time trial; 2026-09-28 18:50 → 2026-09-29 04:45, two usage-limit stops; stopped by the stall rule)

Brief: `SA16_design/BRIEF_SA16.md` = the SA15 brief + all owner rulings (identical in substance to BRIEF_SA18 §2 minus §2b; "Loss recovery without a back channel" was then the stated A5 rule) + the cross-designer table (BRIEF_SA18 §3 rows up to SA15) + method + deliverable (same 8 items); reading: PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS, MEMO_PRIOR_DESIGNERS, MEMO_SA15_FINDINGS. "The owner wants to see whether you produce something DIFFERENT and better." SA16 also grepped: v537 TEMPORAL_T5.md 5.1–5.5 (today = coefficient-domain value prediction coef = pred + q·2^s, ONE history vector per slice transmitted, interleaved rolling refresh refresh_r = 8, unclipped biased pixel domain), DDR_WINDOW_CACHE.md 1–2, LATENCY.md, ledger S5.29, S5.369–S5.373. Model `SA16_design/model/` (omc16.py codec, pyr.py/pyr2.py transform, evalcell.py, t_seq.py, t_hh.py, t_53.py, t_var.py, t_rails.py, run_train.sh, run_queue.sh, queue_all.sh); results out/ (eval/, seq/, tables/), out_t_pred*.txt, out_smoke*.txt.

### 10.1 Design (DESIGN.md §0–§4; final state)
- **Geometry:** planes Y/Cb/Cr; depth 8/10/12 (legal range [0, 2^d−1]; limited range a legal subset); 4:2:2, 4:4:4, 4:2:0. Block = 4 rows of a plane; Unit = 32 luma columns × 4 rows (16 chroma columns at 4:2:2) = the support of one coarsest coefficient and its subtree (128 coefficients per plane). Slice S = 4 at 720p (one block), 8 at 1080p/2160p/4320p (two blocks); slices are rate and packet units only. Width multiple of 32 (chroma 16), height multiple of 4.
- **Transform** (pyr2.py; bijection, legality, re-reading tested exact): 1-D pair split d[k] = u[2k] − u[2k+1], m[k] = u[2k] − ((d[k] + s) >> 1); inverse u[2k] = m[k] + ((d[k] + s) >> 1), u[2k+1] = u[2k] − d[k]; rounding phase s = (i + k) & 1 alternating along both coordinates (kills the −0.25-code-per-level cast of a fixed floor); coded e[k] = d[k] − P[k], two-sided predictor P[k] = (m[k−1] − m[k+1] + 2) >> 2 (one-sided (m[k] − m[k+1] + 1) >> 1 at row/picture ends); 2-D level = horizontal split of every row then vertical split of consecutive row pairs of both outputs; 2 levels V+H then 3 horizontal-only; bands LL, H5, H4, H3, LH2, HL2, HH2, LH1, HL1, HH1 (4:2:2 chroma 4 horizontal levels). **HH bands:** HH2 causal across blocks ((HL2[b−1] − HL2[b] + 1) >> 1), HH1 in-block symmetric over the block's two pair rows ((HL1[2b] − HL1[2b+1] + 1) >> 1) — reason latency: with two-sided HH the dependency chain HH1(b) → HL1(b+1) → L1(b+1) → LH1(b+1) → LL1(b+2) → HH2(b+2) → HL2(b+3) → L2(b+3) → LH2(b+3) → LL2(b+4) needs data 4 blocks (16 rows) ahead, beyond XS parity; with the chosen HH the chain ends at LL2(b+2). **Ahead data:** a slice carries the LL-group (LL, H5..H3) of blocks two ahead, the mid-group (LH2, HL2, HH2) one ahead and the fine-group (LH1, HL1, HH1) of its own blocks; capture latency S + 8 rows; rounding phase absolute → any window of whole blocks synthesises identically to the whole frame (tested exact from the third block of a window).
- **Legality** (pyr2.synthesis): coarse to fine, every leaf clamped before use into its legal interval from final values: for a pair with mean m, phase s, ranges e ∈ [A0,A1], o ∈ [B0,B1]: d ∈ [max(2(A0−m)−s, 2(m−B1)+s−1), min(2(A1−m)+1−s, 2(m−B0)+s)]; the pair mean in [(A0+B0−s+1)>>1, (A1+B1−s+1)>>1] (both brute-force verified for every (m, s, ranges)); ranges start at [lo, hi] and propagate upward (level-1 E-row intervals [dlo − P, dhi − P]; HL1 clamped into the mean interval, HH1 into the difference interval, …); never empty ((m, m) always legal). Consequences: any index set decodes legally, no pixel clip; the pair mean of a clamped pair untouched → coarse re-reading exact; a clamped value reproducible by every index at or beyond the boundary → canonical index = smallest reaching one (ceil((ihi−base)/D) or 0 if base already beyond), boundary values reproducible at every plan. "Legality never harms" (SA16's argument): the clamp moves the illegal sample toward the source, mean preserved; encoder pair-level two-candidate choice nearest vs reaching index by pair error (never more bits). The alternative (move only the illegal sample) makes the coarse level unreadable ("SA15's F5 and the ledger's cycle argument agree on this"; the predict-only pyramid that would allow it costs 1.4–5 dB, SA15 rejected #1). The DESIGN says the two-candidate refinement is "MEASURED in §5" — it was never measured (the rails run failed, §10.3).
- **Temporal (value domain, coefficient domain; no lattice lock):** reference = the previous LEGAL picture D(t−1); prediction x_p = bilinear OBMC over 64×S luma blocks (chroma 32×S at 4:2:2, vectors halved by a shift), integer weights (S−fy)(BW−fx) etc., sum >> log2(S·BW), edge-clamped fetch (four fetches, four constant shift-add multiplies per sample). Vectors derived by the encoder from decoded history — block match D(t−1) vs D(t−2) while t−1 is coded (the D(t−2) window is read for prediction anyway), 4:1 subsampled SAD, candidates (0,0) first then a step-4 grid ±16 rows × ±32 columns (153), one step-2 and one step-1 refinement (16), strict < in list order; TRANSMITTED per block (delta to the left block, tANS tables mvy, mvx); the vectors of slice k+1 travel in slice k (OBMC symmetric at every slice edge). c_p = T(x_p); residual index q codes c = c_p + Δ·q, then the leaf clamp. A still unit (all four contributing vectors zero) predicts itself exactly (T(D(t−1)) = its own final coefficients) → q = 0 unchanged at any plan; every change a non-zero residual makes is toward the source (mid-tread quantiser). Mode per unit inter or intra (LL raw + differences), decided at the slice carrying its LL-group, one binary symbol unless implied by position (refresh band).
- **Refresh, loss, join:** cycle N_c = 8; phase φ = t mod 8 refreshes the COLUMN band of units [φ·Bw, (φ+1)·Bw) in every block row (Bw = ceil(U/8), U = W/32) + ONE guard unit to its right (intra) + the LL of the next unit (intra LL) [RESUME 1b: "one guard unit (intra) and one LL-only-intra unit to its right"]; refresh units intra by position; every slice carries the same intra fraction ((Bw+1)/U ≈ 15 % at 1080p, 12.9 % at 8K; today 12.5 %). Clean-region rule: units in regions 0..φ−1 fetch prediction samples only from columns < 32·φ·Bw of D(t−1), per pixel, for all four OBMC contributions. Guard argument (why U0, the last band unit, decodes exactly at φ): cross-unit reads are only the two-sided predictors reading neighbouring means; G1's first level-k mean depends only on G1's LL, its level-5 difference (whose predictor reads G2's LL — intra by the rule) and the first pair of every finer level of G1 → exact; after one full cycle every unit is exact; bound ≤ 2 cycles = 16 frames from a join (same class as today's refresh_r = 8, documented 8–16). Lost slice: concealed as inter with zero residual using the vectors already held; next slice's vectors copied from the row above; damage bounded to the slice rows ± vector reach per frame, repaired by the sweep. **Exact hold of still refresh units** (per group: a static band unit whose inter residual is all zero at the lane plan described as the reference unit itself: intra symbols at the reference unit's own plan Pu, a hold flag per group, EG0(PMAX − Pu) once per unit) — described in DESIGN §1.5 but **REMOVED at 05:55** (RESUME; see 10.3): DESIGN §5 measurements are "no holds"; DESIGN §8 risk 2 ("held units send their LL at full precision ≈ 10 bits per plane per unit, ~3–4 % of a 0.5 bpp pipe on fully still content") refers to the removed mechanism.
- **Plan and rate control (exact CBR, one decision per slice):** per band Δ_b = 2^e, e_b(P) = max(0, (P + off_b)//30 + base_b − 5); base_b + off_b/30 = the band's ideal step exponent relative to LL from the MEASURED per-coefficient synthesis gain g_b: RD-optimal = equal distortion per coefficient in the gain-normalised domain → Δ_b ∝ 1/√g_b. Luma: LL 0, H5 0.98, H4 1.49, H3 2.00, LH2 2.53, HL2 2.47, HH2 3.56, LH1 3.58, HL1 3.52, HH1 4.60 octaves; chroma 4:2:2: LL 0, H4 1.00, H3 1.50, LH2 2.03, HL2 1.97, HH2 3.06, LH1 3.08, HL1 3.02, HH1 4.10; one rule for every plane and cell; consecutive plans differ by one band-octave (30 sub-steps per octave), lattices octave-nested per band; P ∈ [0, 359], P = 0 lossless. Pipe: F = bpp·W·H bits/frame, 32-bit frame header, 80-bit slice header; each slice's nominal share ∝ block-groups it carries (slice 0 carries 1.5 units); credit carries forward inside the frame, never borrowed; padded to F. **Lanes:** for a candidate P the symbols are open-loop (q = Q((c_s − c_p)/Δ) inter, Q(c_s/Δ) intra, Q(r) = sign·floor(|r|/Δ + 0.45), intra LL raw unsigned index); code length exact before coding because contexts are **plan-invariant and clamp-independent**: inter symbols use the class of |c_p| of the same coefficient (thresholds 8 and 32 codes at 10-bit) × the unit's vector class (zero/non-zero); intra symbols the brightness class of the unit's decoded LL; flag bits reserved at worst case; tile significance flags per (unit, band) so zeros cost ~0. 10 octave lanes (P = 30·o), then 30 refinement lanes inside the finest fitting octave = one predetermined re-choice; 40 quantise-and-lookup passes, no coding, no search. **Emission:** the chosen plan synthesised once on a window of whole blocks (two carried blocks + three context blocks above and two below; the reference update); from the final values the encoder reads the emitted plan Pe = the coarsest plan at which every carried unit is reproducible in a single mode (boundary values reproducible at every plan), the mode of each unit = cheaper reproducing mode, (hold flags), canonical indices; emitted bits ≤ lane estimate ≤ budget (smaller-magnitude symbols at plan ≥ lane plan, identical contexts, monotone tables); credit from emitted bits. Coarsest-lane overflow: P = 299 codes only zeros, raw LL at 1 bit, headers ("fits every slice at every rate ≥ 0.25 bpp by arithmetic"; any occurrence counted as a failure).
- **Entropy:** static tANS L = 1024; alphabet q ∈ [−15, 15] direct (31) + escape (EG0 of |q|−16 and sign); tables per (plane class Y/C, band, context 0..8) = 2×10×9 = 180 tables of 32 symbols (≈ 3 Mbit) + mode, hold, mvx, mvy; frequencies quantised to L and made **monotone in |q|** (so any smaller-magnitude re-description is never longer); trained on bosphorus, cityalley, readysetgo, trafficlightsL, winterdriveL at 0.5 and 1.0 bpp, counts of emitted symbols only.
- **Stream:** frame header 32 bits (frame counter mod 256 → refresh phase, format id); slice header 80 bits (P, three plane lengths, CRC); vectors of the next slice's block row; unit flags; per plane symbols in **unit-major order** (a joiner has |c_p| only for clean units = the left part of every row → decodes the clean prefix exactly and discards the rest); one tANS stream per plane per slice (flush ≤ 16 bits).
- **Formats:** (4:2:0 "not a contribution requirement": chroma block = 4 luma rows, unit 16 × 2 chroma, the vertical ahead depth stays inside the luma slice; the Lanczos-3 buffer runs at the input rate inside the latency budget) 4:4:4 chroma 5 horizontal levels; 4:2:2 chroma 4 levels (unit 16 chroma columns); 4:2:0 chroma 4 horizontal + ONE vertical level in 2-row blocks; 8/12-bit same ladder, context thresholds scale. Resolution conversion: separable Lanczos-3 polyphase after the decoder (never a crop), 6 lines of buffer.
- **Why the known artifacts cannot occur (§3, ARGUED — SA16's own §5 later refuted the stills and row-phase claims):** seams/special rows — no filter straddles a slice edge differently from any other block edge; the vertical predictor of every block reads the block above and below alike (ahead data); OBMC symmetric at slice edges (ahead vectors); the step is the same for all rows of a slice and consecutive slices differ by sub-steps only; smudges — the DC of every 32×4 unit is coded (LL) with an unbiased quantiser and position-alternating rounding; no per-block offsets, fill or repair; flattening — no dead-zone knob, the sub-octave ladder coarsens one band at a time, a unit keeps its inter detail (value domain, no lattice lock); flicker in still areas — "a still unit is unchanged at every plan; refresh holds it exactly" [refuted after the hold removal]; blocks/steps — motion is a continuous bilinear blend, no block-wise switching; cast — alternating rounding phase.
- **Exactness proof sketch (§2)** (baseband hop = the same statement with the decoder's output as the source: "the decoder's output is the legal picture, no wide domain, no CDR"): induction over slices: vectors deterministic from D(t−1), D(t−2); Pe, modes, (holds), symbols read from finals by the same functions a gen-2 encoder applies; gen-2 lane at Pe reproduces the input exactly and costs exactly gen 1's emitted bits; credit identical by induction.
- **Latency (§4.1, arithmetic, today's LATENCY.md model; capture S + 8 rows):** 720p50 S4 18 lines 0.480 ms, 0.987 ms with 3:1 up (reach 18+1); 720p60 18, 0.400, 0.822; 1080p50 S8 26, 0.462, 0.693 (2:1 up, reach 12+1 — "today refuses this leg at 16-line slices"); 1080p60 26, 0.385, 0.578; 2160p50 0.231, 0.347; 2160p60 0.193, 0.289; 4320p60 0.096, 0.144. XS ≈ 32 lines.
- **On-chip memory, decoder (§4.2, DDR_WINDOW_CACHE §7 method; store = depth bits):** band ring = (S + 32 + S) rows of reference window (vector reach ±16 + next slice's prefetch); coefficient buffer S + 8 rows × 16 bit; tANS tables 180 × 1024 × 18 bit (BRAM); line buffers 6 rows. Band ring / coef / tables / line / total Mbit / today's total: 1080p 4:4:4 12b 3.32 / 1.47 / 3.32 / 0.41 / 8.5 / 11.83; 2160p 4:2:2 10b 3.69 / 1.97 / 3.32 / 0.46 / 9.4 / 17.40; 2160p 4:4:4 12b 6.64 / 2.95 / 3.32 / 0.83 / 13.7 / 24.86; 4320p 4:2:2 10b 7.37 / 3.93 / 3.32 / 0.92 / 15.5 / 29.23 (narrowed store); 4320p 4:4:4 12b 13.27 / 5.90 / 3.32 / 1.66 / 24.2 / 47.20 (does not fit). URAM ≤ 21 Mbit of 27 at 8K 4:4:4 12-bit; BRAM ≤ 5 of 11; encoder ≈ +4 Mbit at 8K (S + 8 source rows + 10 per-band cost accumulators per unit). (SA15's R11b table gives today's DECODER URAM as 24.25 narrowed / 40.85 at 8K; SA16's "today's total" includes BRAM — different accounting, both kept.)
- **DDR (§4.3):** decoder one reference window read per frame (1.0×) + one write = 2 × depth = 20 bit/sample at 10-bit (today 26 with the 13-bit store); encoder same two streams (D(t−2) window already read for the prediction of t−1; "today's encoder holds a third luma frame").
- (Latency note: "the pipe streams each slice's groups as they become available, so the front-loaded first slice costs no extra period"; deterministic.)
- **Work (§4.4, closed form; "No multiplier, no arithmetic coder, no iteration; one decision per slice"):** decoder OBMC ≈ 17 + prediction forward transform ≈ 15 + dequantise-add 1 + synthesis with clamps ≈ 30 (per level predictor 3, merge 2, interval 6, clamp 2) ≈ 65 per sample ≈ 2× an XS decoder; 8K60 4:2:2 ≈ 260 G add/s ≈ 900 pipelined adders ≈ 15–30 k LUTs (≈ 10 %). Encoder analysis 15 + OBMC 17 + prediction transform 15 + history search (169 candidates on a 4:1 subsample, 2 ops) ≈ 85 + lane costs (octave-nested: symbol at octave o+1 = symbol at o shifted right by one → every plan's cost = sum of 10 per-band octave totals: 10 shifts + 10 lookups per coefficient + 300 prefix sums per slice) ≈ 30 + closed-loop synthesis 30 + emission 10 ≈ 200 per sample ≈ 5× an XS encoder; 8K60 ≈ 800 G op/s ≈ 3000 pipelined units ≈ 25–45 % of the part; 10 lookups per coefficient from 29-kbit code-length ROMs replicated 10× (0.3 Mbit LUTRAM).
- **IP (§6):** S-transform (Haar 1910; Said & Pearlman 1996; Zandi et al. CREW 1995); two-sided predictor from means (S+P 1996; CDF (2,6) Cohen–Daubechies–Feauveau 1992); 2V × 5H line-based decomposition (JPEG 2000 / ISO 15444 family practice, royalty-free); interval clamp own (SA15 evidence); value-domain coefficient prediction (H.261 era 1988–90; today's OMC T5 principle); bilinear OBMC (Orchard & Sullivan 1994; H.263 Annex F 1996); history block match + transmitted vectors (own / today's OMC T5 §5.2); column-band refresh with guard (own; GDR H.263/MPEG-2 era); octave-nested per-band ladder (EZW 1993, SPIHT 1996; ladder own); tANS (Duda 2013); canonical re-description own; Lanczos-3 (1950s / Duchon 1979).
- **Prior failures checked (§7):** slice-boundary step (no slice is a transform unit); SA15 causal variant (causal vertical measured −0.3…−1.4 dB, rejected; HH1 in-block treats both pair rows identically); HVBC grid (no per-block switching); lattice-lock texture loss (value-domain); still shimmer ("a still unit predicts itself exactly … refresh holds it exactly" — later not met, 10.3); refresh stall (fixed fraction per frame); join plateau (guard-unit column sweep); rail cost / partner away ("no reaching index is ever spent unless it is free"); in-cell iteration / REXT (none); decoder-derived vectors (transmitted); 16×16 grid (bilinear OBMC); entropy-estimate lead (real code lengths); trial coding (open-loop lanes, "coordinator intervention on this design's first rate control"); −D/2 LL bias and zeros costing 0.2 bit each in its first model (rounded index-DPCM LL, tile significance flags).

### 10.2 Tests on the way (RESUME, DESIGN; out_t_pred*.txt)
**Clock note [SOURCES DIFFER in clock]:** RESUME.md's event times (e.g. 03:40, 04:20, 04:40, 05:15, 05:55, 06:20, 06:55, 07:05) are not on the ledger's EDT clock: the ledger has SA16 working until 04:45 and handing in at ~04:55, the memo is dated 04:50 EDT, and DESIGN.md/RESUME.md were last written at 04:33 (file times). The ORDER of the RESUME events is right; their times are SA16's own clock.
- Early status (RESUME 23:50): gen-1 encode → decode rt = 0 on every frame tested; gen 2 pictures AND bits identical on dng720 frames 0, 1, 2 ("180/180 slices via the reading path"); the first dng720 evaluation ran on tables_p1 (corrected code), then was discarded (bug 9). smoke3 (8 frames, out/smoke3.log): bits ≤ pipe every frame, no overflow, estimate ≥ emitted; PSNR 30.6 → 34.6/35.1/35.9 dB by frame 7 at 0.5 bpp (today's 12-frame mean 35.2/36.1/37.1).
- **Causal vs two-sided predictors** (training clips only, rate-matched PSNR): out_t_pred.txt (luma, step 4/8/16: bpp, PSNR): bosphorus_19 causal 1.957/47.64, 1.114/42.72, 0.565/37.99; two-sided 1.821/48.18, 1.003/43.36, 0.508/38.70; haar 2.031/48.43, 1.191/43.44, 0.613/38.65. cityalley_19 causal 2.072/47.53, 1.233/42.91, 0.703/38.23; two-sided 1.976/48.04, 1.150/43.44, 0.645/38.87; haar 2.035/48.37, 1.211/43.80, 0.694/39.27. readysetgo_1 causal 2.822/47.30, 1.925/42.22, 1.231/37.20; two-sided 2.707/47.80, 1.815/42.77, 1.144/37.80; haar 2.825/48.12, 1.934/43.04, 1.246/38.10. Summary (DESIGN/RESUME): causal-both −2.83/−0.87/−0.81 dB (bosphorus) and −0.84/−0.31/−0.34 (cityalley); H causal only −1.45; V causal only (both levels) −1.38; each single causal axis 0.3–1.45 dB; closed-loop encoding recovers nothing (−2.70). out_t_pred3.txt (vertical variants vs V1 = two / V2 = two): bosphorus Y V1 in-block V2 causal −1.43, V1 in-block V2 two −0.96, V1 two V2 causal −0.44; Cb −0.87, −0.40, −0.45; Cr −0.81, −0.35, −0.43; cityalley Y −0.84, −0.47, −0.34; Cb −0.31, −0.07, −0.21; Cr −0.34, −0.10, −0.21. out_t_pred2.txt: per-plane open/closed-loop sweeps by H/V predictor type (e.g. cityalley Cr H=two V=two open step 6 0.293 bpp 48.28 dB, closed 0.312 / 48.45). → causal predictors REJECTED as a design-created dB loss (ledger S5.374: −0.8…−2.8 dB luma, chroma −0.3…−0.9 → two-sided predictors).
- **HH predictor choice** (out_t_pred4.txt, rate-matched vs two-sided HH): bosphorus causal −0.156/−0.074/−0.091, in-block HH1 + causal HH2 −0.157/−0.073/−0.089; cityalley causal −0.243/−0.053/−0.080, in-block + causal −0.244/−0.053/−0.077 → DESIGN: −0.16/−0.07/−0.09 and −0.24/−0.05/−0.08, taken for latency parity. NEG restatement (coordinator request; t_hh.py, bosphorus 6 frames 0.5 bpp, tables_p2): hh0 (chosen) NEG 85.06 worst 83.65 PSNR 41.59/45.57/45.43; hh1 (two-sided, 4-block-ahead latency) 85.16 / 83.64, 41.72/45.68/45.53 → +0.10 NEG / +0.1 dB for two-sided; chosen form stands. (Ledger S5.374: "HH causal/in-block accepted at −0.05…−0.24 dB for latency (review point)"; S5.375: hh0 85.06/83.65 vs hh1 85.16/83.64 ≈ equal.)
- **Refresh geometries rejected on paper:** two-sided horizontal predictor + column sweep with no guard (the clean region's right edge reads a dirty mean → a special column during refresh); two-sided predictors + ROW sweep (the top band at each cycle start has no credit under the CBR prefix bound → a coarser band once per cycle; latency cost ∝ band height). First plan used causal predictors, for which "ahead means (SA15) not needed … +4 lines latency avoided" — superseded by the two-sided decision.
- **Coordinator interventions (ledger S5.374, RESUME):** rate control rebuilt as open-loop lanes (10 octave + 30 refinement, one predetermined re-choice) "after the trial-coding intervention" (closed-loop lanes = one full slice coding per candidate plan = trial coding, forbidden); refresh = column band + intra guard unit + LL-only-intra unit (review: "no flat unit"); floor-lane question (00:25): the floor lane (P = 299) codes LL (intra index-DPCM at D = 128, inter LL residual) and no detail symbols — an INTRA slice on it = LL-only 32×4 blocks = a flat rung slice — "a documented failure mode, NOT a guarantee"; claim to measure: at ≥ 0.5 bpp the floor and coarsest octave lanes are never chosen (per-cell plan histograms floor / ≥ 270 / ≥ 240). Status at 22:40 (ledger S5.374): "transform/legality self-tests pass; tables, efficiency, g2, loss, join all TODO". Session 429 at ~20:46, resumed 22:40.
- **Model bugs found and fixed (RESUME, "root causes, no patches"):** (1) rounding phase array-relative (window synthesis ≠ frame synthesis by one code) → absolute phase; (2) HL1's interval depends on its own block's fine means → HL1 moved to the fine group; HH predictors chosen not to read the block below; (3) hold of the "inter result" is on no coarse lattice → hold = the reference unit itself, per group, LL at full precision; (4) neighbour-magnitude contexts are clamp-dependent (the decoder's clamp changes 207 of 1,280 zero-index coefficients in one LH1 band row of dng720) → plan-invariant contexts + tile significance flags; (5) intra LL raw index had a −D/2 bias (floor) → rounded index, index-domain DPCM from the left unit; (6) mode rule: emission reads "inter iff the LL-group finals are inter-reproducible"; (7) degenerate interval (ilo == ihi): canonical index 0 in the lane too; (8) flag bits in the lane made exact (worst-case reservation caused gen-2 read-misses by a few bits); (9) (00:05) a stray `elif` added hold symbol bits twice in the emission → removed; the first dng720 evaluation collapsed from frame 6 (credit negative) and was discarded; (10) floor-lane semantics documented (above; also: an INTER slice on the floor lane = "the prediction + LL correction (prediction texture kept, no new detail)"; "a slice always has ≥ its nominal share (credit only adds); lane costs are content-dependent, so no analytic bound excludes the floor on full-amplitude noise at 0.25 bit/sample"; "the collapses seen so far came from the accounting bugs (9, 11), not from content"); (11) (00:20) a unit's hold plan Pu dragged to ≤ 89 by one off-lattice LL value → near-lossless re-sends (> 1 kbit/unit) → budgets exhausted → floor; fix: LL no longer constrains Pu; hold only if Pu ≥ the previous slice's emitted plan; (12) (05:55) the dead-zone hold test is inconsistent between a source input (gen 1) and a finals input (gen 2): a fresh-coded static group whose finals quantise to a zero residual at Pe is held by the gen-2 lane → picture flip (floor f1 slice 20 unit 14, HL1: gen 1 final 0, gen 2 64); (13) a held group re-sends the reference at the reference's own fine plan → lane reservation inflated estimates 15 % and drove 4 slices to the floor lane (dng720 @0.5 f3) → **HOLD mechanism REMOVED (HOLD = 0)**: refresh units always fresh intra at the slice plan; consequence: still content re-quantised once per 8 frames in the refresh band (pop class) — "documented weakness, not patched". Also (05:15) S = 8 gen-2 flip root: hold decided from the SOURCE residual at gen 1 but from finals at gen 2 (a boundary clamp can return a non-zero residual to the reference value; floor f1 slice 103 unit 14) → holds decided from finals both sides, and "the lane reserves max(hold, fresh) symbol bits for static band units" ('hold_flip' counts lane/emission differences) — that reservation inflated lane estimates by 15 % and drove 4 slices to the floor lane (bug 13), so fixing this gen-2 flip created the cost that led to the hold's removal at 05:55. (05:55) JOIN LEAK: the barrier limited the FETCH, but T(x_p) is computed on the whole prediction picture, whose two-sided predictors read across the barrier column → the last clean units' c_p depend on dirty samples and contamination walks left each frame → clean units take c_p from the transform of a barrier-consistent picture (columns beyond the barrier replicated from the last clean column). - SA16's root choices behind the gap (measured): (1) pair pyramid −0.53/−0.19/−0.19 (dng720) to −0.76/−0.66/−0.63 dB (bosphorus) vs 5/3 at matched entropy; (2) the gain-derived ladder is PSNR-optimal and at 0.5 bpp zeroes the level-1 bands almost entirely (luma flatplane 46 % vs 42 %; artifactmap luma 30 vs 15) — NEG punishes that; (3) refresh fraction (15 % at 1080p vs 12.5 % today). "No per-cell tuning was done and none is proposed; a perceptual tilt of the ladder would be a knob against the metric." (Memo §4.11: a perceptual tilt NOT tried; "today's codec's perceptual allocation is part of its design, not a per-cell knob".)
- **Exactness (§5.2):** generation chain dng720 @0.5, 6 frames × 3 generations: bits and pictures byte-identical (out/seq/gen.log) — measured on the OLD, defective ladder with tables_p2 and holds ON (RESUME 04:40 "old ladder, mechanics only"); on the final design (no holds, gain-derived ladder, tables_p3) the only exactness checks were floorball S = 8 gen 2 over 2 frames (06:20) and the dng720 join; floorball S = 8, 2 frames identical after hold removal; rt = 0 on every frame; join from grey at frame 5 (dng720 @0.5) exact after one cycle, bound ≤ 16 frames (required barrier-consistent c_p); slice loss (slices 20–21 of frame 4, dng720 @0.5): damage rows 48–133, repaired by frame 19 (15 frames; 233-sample residue 4 frames) — measured on the OLD ladder; legality 0 oob every run; rails (cut24/ext10) not measured.

The pyramid has a 4-row block signature (outer rows of a block carry 3–8 % more error than the inner rows, every block alike, no slice row special) — "NOT the identical-statistics bar". SA16's root: the pair split assigns the difference error asymmetrically to the even/odd sample only through the rounding phase; a full fix needs an even split (half-code precision in the synthesis), not built. (Memo F3: likely source the HH causal/in-block predictors and the two-blocks-ahead structure — coordinator's reading.) Still input (old ladder) converges 30.6 → 37.5 dB, 40–57 % change per frame; with holds removed the refresh band re-quantises still units once per cycle; mixed clip 58/42 → still bar NOT met.

Decision: SA16 declared its root choices cannot meet goal 4 → stall rule; SA16 stopped; memo `SA17_design/MEMO_SA16_FINDINGS.md`; brief BRIEF_SA17.md (adapted, + SA16 row); SA17 spawned on Opus 5.5 with PROJECT_CONSTRAINTS + all three memos. Memo summary: better than SA15 on loss recovery, join, memory; worse on efficiency, still areas, row phases; Memo §2 S8 — the coordinator credited Fable's process: (1) rejected ideas on paper before building them (two refresh geometries that would create a special column or band); (2) measured and rejected its own causal-predictor plan (−0.8…−2.8 dB); (3) restated the HH choice in NEG when asked; (4) found the plan-weight defect by per-band error-energy analysis. Memo §5 also: "Only SA15 reached ≥ today on some points (3 of 12)"; "Open: rail-plate bit cost and never away (SA15)". memo §5 hints: seam-freedom WITHOUT a transform price still open; contexts must be clamp-independent; value-domain prediction + column-band refresh + guard + clean-region barrier gives exact join ≤ 16 frames and loss repair in 15 frames, lattice lock cannot; open: still-area stability without an exact hold that breaks gen 2; both SA15 and SA16 lost NEG through texture loss at the finest bands (SA15 lattice lock, SA16 PSNR-optimal allocation); a canonical perceptual allocation (derived by rule, same at both ends) is not a patch and today's codec has one. S5.378 (later): 15-frame recovery is not a win (bar < 4 frames).
**SA16 rejected list (memo §4):** (1) all-causal predictors (−0.8…−2.8 dB luma, −0.3…−0.9 chroma); (2) column sweep without a guard unit (special column); (3) row sweep (coarser band once per cycle); (4) lattice lock; (5) source-searched vectors (not reproducible at gen 2); (6) dead-zone width as the rate knob (DZ 0.5 −0.6 dB); (7) magnitude-based neighbour contexts; (8) closed-loop lanes (trial coding); (9) exact hold of the inter result (off-lattice after a clamp-heavy reference: Pu = 0, symbols at step 1, slice cost 10× the budget); (10) chroma-first priority; (11) perceptual tilt NOT tried. Plus (short pack) a floor lane with no symbols queried as a forbidden flat rung.
