## §11 SA17 "CPV-1" — Continuous Pair pyramid, Value-domain prediction (Opus 5.5; 2026-09-29 ~05:00–13:30; from 12:45 with ideation partner SA17P; stopped by the pair stall rule)

Brief: `SA17_design/BRIEF_SA17.md` = BRIEF_SA16 with "Five earlier designers (SA12–SA15 on Opus, SA16 on Fable 5.1)", MEMO_SA16_FINDINGS added to the reading list and an "SA16 additions" row in the §3 table (true duplicate of BRIEF_SA18 content otherwise). SA17 read all of it plus SA14 DESIGN §1.2–1.5, §2; located the owner tools incl. `shared_tools/rowphase.py`. Model `SA17_design/model/` (pyr.py transform + legality + canonical index; ent.py tANS code lengths; codec.py frame coder, rate control, reading, decoder; motion.py history vectors, OBMC, barrier; seq.py sequence, refresh/heal, still-hold, re-encoder; heal.py; oneway.py; stilltest.py; railtest.py; awaytest.py; join2.py; evalcell.py; metrics.py — NEG verified against the owner's negscore.sh: 88.858 = 88.858). Results out/ (eval_final.log, eval/*.json, dec/, art/, heal_*.log, eval_wave.log, eval_oneway.log, still_*.log, join_v*.log, rail_v1.log, away_*.log, chain_final_*.log, tune*.log). PROXY = entropy proxy (screening only); PAPER = argument.

### 11.1 Design (DESIGN.md, final) and the root-cause chain behind each choice (DESIGN §0)
- Goal 1 seams: root "a slice that is a closed transform unit gives its edge rows one-sided support" → transform continuous over the whole frame; a slice is only a rate/packet unit (evidence: today's rowphase dng720 @0.5, owner tool sh = S: Y 4.6 / Cb 20.5 / Cr 18.1 %). Second order: "a continuous dyadic 5/3 still treats even (updated) and odd (predicted) rows differently" → vertical split = symmetric PAIR (E1 PROXY: dng720 row spread 5/3 2.3/15.4/14.6 % vs pair 0.3/0.5/1.7 %).
- Goals 2+3: "with an UPDATE step, a clipped sample's lost overshoot is read by the lowpass; a re-encoder cannot recover it" → every output pair written LAST from a final mean and a leaf read by nothing else; legality = interval clamp of that leaf; index = smallest magnitude that reproduces (E2: 5/3 + clip + reading: 18–282 index mismatches, 820–24,769 differing pixels on cut24/ext10). Per-sample leaves (predict-only) would make a plain clip exact → rejected (E1d PROXY +12…60 % bits).
- Goal 4 transform: kept pair — at REAL code lengths the pair equals 5/3 (EV-B: 5/3 backend (not legal, not exact) hwy 91.47 / floor 93.88 vs pair 91.69 / 93.65 NEG @0.5).
- Goal 4 entropy: tables pooled over all step sizes → tables keyed by (plane class, band group, inter/intra, step bucket) (ENT1: pooled tables +47.8 % class bits over per-band empirical on a training clip).
- Goal 4 refresh: a rolling wave costs 0.8–1.0 NEG → owner ruling: on-demand heal over the return path primary; a wave only for one-way links (EV-B).
- Still areas: "exact CBR spends every bit; with nothing to code, still content is re-refined every frame; history vectors read refinement as motion" → one refinement per still coefficient then hold (encoder-only), padding; zero-vector tolerance 2 codes/pixel (§7).
- **Transform:** per plane 2 "quad" levels then 3 horizontal pair levels (2V × 5H) over the WHOLE picture; mirror extension only at picture edges. Pair step both axes: S-transform m = (a + b + π) >> 1, d = a − b, position-parity rounding π (unbiased; removes the +1-code cast class); leaf = d − ((m[−1] − m[+1] + 1 + π′) >> 2) (2/6 slope from neighbouring FINAL means). Quad level: vertical pair → mean row VM, difference row VD; horizontal pairs of VM → (LL, HL leaf), of VD → (LH mean-leaf, HH leaf); HH predicted from the vertical slope of HL and the horizontal slope of LH ((ΔHL + ΔLH + 3 + π) >> 3). Synthesis order LL → HL → VM → LH → HH → VD → pixel pairs (all reads final). Every leaf read by nothing but its own pair.
- **Quantiser / plan / rate control:** lattice leaf value = base + q·2^e (base 0 intra; left final LL for intra LL DPCM; the prediction's leaf for inter); nothing else normative; encoder dead zone ρ = 0.35 (floor(|r|/s + 0.35)). Plan ladder: M = 30 (plane, band) exponents; plan k raises exactly one band exponent by 1 per step, order fixed by the synthesis gains + a tilt of 0.25 octave per level toward finer fine bands (tuned on training clips only: traffic @0.5 +1.0 NEG vs no tilt); lattices NESTED (dyadic). One decision per slice: all 16 exponents' code lengths per band computed in parallel from open-loop symbols; the finest plan that fits B + credit − header − 16 taken; the 16-bit reserve covers the canonical emission (measured excess at most 5.5 bits on 1 slice in 180; with it 0 prefix violations in every measured run); unused bits credit; unneeded bits padding. Emission: after synthesis, the canonical READING of its own picture (coarsest consistent plan, smallest-magnitude indices) — one fixed second pass.
- **Entropy:** per leaf magnitude class (0; 1 + ⌊log2|q|⌋) static tANS L = 1024, context = (left + above class) within the slice (above cut at the slice top: packets independent), then raw mantissa bits and a sign bit. Tables: (luma/chroma) × 8 band groups × (intra/inter) × 3 step buckets (e ≤ 2, 3–4, ≥ 5) × 4 contexts; contexts use INDICES only (clamp-independent).
- **Temporal:** one reference D(t−1); P = OBMC (bilinear block-centre weights, 4-bit, shift 8) of D(t−1) with integer vectors per 64 × 8 luma block (4:2:2 chroma half-sample by a 2-tap average). Vectors DERIVED by every encoder from decoded history (block match D(t−1) vs D(t−2), ±16 × ±4, row-wise regularised toward the left vector, zero unless motion explains the block better by > 2 codes per pixel) and TRANSMITTED (Exp-Golomb differences). Value-domain coefficient prediction: inter leaf value = leaf of T(P) + q·2^e (texture at full precision; no lattice lock).
- **Slices/packets:** S rows (4 at 720p, 8 above), one packet, one plan; packet k also carries the level-2 leaves of the next block and the coarse leaves two blocks ahead (PAPER — the model is frame-level).
- **Loss/refresh, two modes:** return path (primary): per-slice loss flags, on-demand heal, NO scheduled refresh; one-way: column wave of cycle 2. Resolution conversion unchanged from today.
- **Still-hold (encoder-only, §7):** a zero-vector coefficient is refined ONCE after it becomes still (or after intra/heal), then held unless the source moves by more than ¾ of the step it was refined at; unused bits padding; vector derivation keeps zero unless motion is better by > 2 codes per pixel (ZTOL 2).
- Other §3.1/§8 claims: "A legal source passes the synthesis with 0 samples changed"; rails run at full and limited range (cut24 / ext10 full / ext10 limited, 0.25–2 bpp); "No multipliers (4-bit shift-add weights)"; latency "constant by construction".
- **Latency/work/memory (PAPER, §8):** latency 2S + 10 lines (capture S + 8, one slice period, ≈ 2 pipeline): 18 lines at 720p (S = 4; with today's 3:1 conversion ≈ 0.99 ms, "the same line count as today's model 8 + 8 + 2"), 26 lines at 1080p–8K (S = 8); XS ≈ 32. Work per sample: decoder ≈ 57 adds/compares (synthesis ≈ 25 incl. clamps, OBMC ≈ 16, prediction analysis ≈ 12, entropy ≈ 4) ≈ 2× XS decode; encoder ≈ 330 (source + prediction analysis, 16 exponent costs in parallel, canonical reading ≈ 48, vector derivation ≈ 150 on a 1:4 decimated match). Memory at 8K 4:2:2 10-bit, decoder: reference ring ≈ 40 rows × 15,360 samples × 10 bit = 6.1 Mbit (samples legal by construction → the ring stores depth bits; a factor in the 20 vs 26 bit/sample DDR figure) + coefficient buffers ≈ 7.9 + output lines 1.2 + tANS tables 384 × 1024 × ≈ 19 bit ≈ 7.5 → ≈ 22.7 Mbit (today, narrowed store: 29.2); 8K 4:4:4 12-bit ≈ 34 Mbit — over the comfortable margin of a ZU7EV-class part (27 + 11) unless tables drop to 2 contexts (−3.7 Mbit). DDR 20 bit/sample (today 26); vector field for frame t derived during t−1 from rows already fetched. Still-hold state one exponent per (band, unit, slice): a few kbit.
- **IP (§9):** S-transform pair, 2/6 slope (Haar / S-transform 1970s–80s; TS/CREW 2/6 family Ricoh mid-1990s, expired); lifting (Sweldens 1996), parity rounding own; leaf-interval legality, canonical reading, nested ladder own (after SA15); OBMC bilinear (H.263 Annex F era 1996); block matching, Exp-Golomb 1978; static tANS (Duda 2009+); on-demand refresh (owner ruling S5.37; generic ARQ-style intra refresh); column-band gradual refresh (GDR, H.263/MPEG-2 era).

### 11.2 Tests in order (RESUME item table + DESIGN)
- E1 (PROXY; intra; entropy proxy + PSNR BD, gain-normalised; "screening only"): V pair (2/6) vs V 5/3 (H 5/3 both): BD +2.0 % dng720, +2.5 % hwy, +6.3 % spot; row-phase spread (sh = 4) dng720 @~1.3 bpp: 5/3 2.3/15.4/14.6 %, pair 0.3/0.5/1.7 %; long L clips have a source chroma row-phase structure (4:2:0 origin) → use dng for row tests. E1b (PROXY): pair + update (both signs, causal), 2/10 predict, (9,7)-M horizontal: none helps (updates ±0.3 % or worse; 2/10 worse; 9/7-M +1.6..+2.3 %). E1d (PROXY): predict-only at level 1 / 1–2 / all: +12..29 % / +37..52 % / +48..60 % bits → per-sample-leaf legality structures dead (confirms S5.349). E2: plain clip + gen-2 nearest-lattice reading, continuous 5/3: cf_gfx 0 misses at every rate; cut24/ext10 18..282 index mismatches, 820..24,769 pixels differ → NOT exact on rails.
- M1 model built: legality stress 0 oob; gen-2 reading dng720 @0.5 5 frames pictures + bits + plans identical; decoder == encoder. T1 intra tables (5 disjoint clips, 3 rounds: tab_intra_pair.pkl, tab_intra_53.pkl); T2 seq tables (tab_seq_pair.pkl, 2 rounds; tab_seq_53 retrained after an LL bug).
- P0 first real eval, untuned, r0 tables, dng720 (frames 2–11 vs today real): @0.5 NEG 89.08 (worst 86.03), PSNR 35.18/36.04/37.17 vs 90.16 (87.22), 35.22/36.10/37.14; @1.0 94.09 (92.56), 38.78/37.23/38.37 vs 94.05 (92.31), 38.19/37.08/37.95. R1 row phase (owner rowphase.py) on P0 dng720 @0.5: sh = 8 spread 1.3/3.6/4.2 % (today 9.8/53.0/50.8); sh = 4 0.6/3.0/3.1 (outer vs inner rows of a 4-row block, chroma); mean |err| by phase (S = 8) spread 3.9/1.5/1.8 % (today 33.9/16.4/13.4). Today's rowphase with sh = 16 dng720 @0.5: Y 11.9 / Cb 57.8 / Cr 52.4 %.
- TU1 (training clips city, traffic): tilt +0.25 better (traffic +1.0 NEG @0.5), −0.25 worse; ρ 0.35 slightly better than 0.42; ρ 0.5 worse. TU2 (city/traffic/winter): best tilt 0.25, ρ 0.35, 64-column blocks, λ 4 (all 6 points ≥ others within 0.3); chroma offsets trade planes; ρ_still 0.2 no gain.
- J1 gen chain + joiner + loss decoder, dng720 @0.5, 24 frames (join at f3, slice 40 lost at f4; wave mode): gen 2 AND gen 3 identical pictures + bits all 24 frames; loss repaired at f13 (9 frames; cycle 7); JOINER NEVER CONVERGES (1.3–1.6 M samples differ through f23; phase lock found at f4). J2 joiner fixes: (a) readable unit = consistent at a plan with every band step ≥ 2 (camera input reads ku = 0, decoded ku ≥ 216 on dng720); forced slice plan = min ku over readable units (NOT capped at own plan — that broke gen 2 by credit divergence); (b) clean-region rule added to VECTOR DERIVATION (clean blocks derive from barrier-replicated D(t−1), D(t−2); zero at cycle start); per-unit plans tried and REJECTED (plan side info ~78 bits/slice, 1.5 %, pushed every slice over budget); gen-2 exact restored (24 f). - **Exactness (§4):** R1 pair analysis of D returns the final (clamped) leaves exactly; R2 canonical reading (smallest |q| with clamp(base + q·2^e) = v exists at every e ≤ e_read (nesting); slice plan read = the coarsest at which all leaves have one; gen 1 emits that reading); R3 vectors functions of decoded pictures; a re-encoder reads the refresh/heal structure from the picture (units that read as intra at a plan with every step ≥ 2 exist only where upstream coded intra; camera input reads 0, decoded dng720 ≥ 216); R4 (CBR) a re-encoder forces every readable unit to the read plan; its cost = gen 1's emitted bits; by induction its credit ≥ gen 1's → fits; gen ≥ 3 identical credits. CH measured (final configuration: on-demand mode, still-hold, reserve): dng720 @0.5 14 frames and spot @0.5 7 frames: gen 2 AND gen 3 pictures and bits identical every frame (out/chain_final_*.log); earlier configurations 24 frames dng720 / 30 frames spot identical (join_v1.log, join_v2*.log); rails 0.25–2 bpp identical. Ledger S5.382: chain logs show a "loss" column with ~27k samples still damaged dng720 f9–f12 (arm unclear — review point). Mid-stream join by an ENCODER on a two-way link: exact once every unit has been coded intra upstream; in the primary (no-wave) mode only heals do that → NOT guaranteed to converge (measured in wave mode only: 1.3–1.6 M samples differing after 20 frames; not fixed). Decoder join: exact after one cycle in the one-way mode. OPEN.
- **"Never away" (§3.2):** the clamp preserves the pair mean (already read by the neighbours' slope predictors and what the coarser level's reading recovers), so the partner moves by the overshoot in the other direction; toward its own source iff the mean's error is ≤ 0 at the upper rail (≥ 0 at the lower). SA17's proof sketch: exactness needs every value read before the clamp (the mean) to be recoverable from the final picture; for a pair the only such functional is the sum, so an exact legaliser preserves the sum and moves the partner by −overshoot; direction = sign of the mean's reconstruction error, unknowable to the decoder; the two escapes measured dead (per-sample leaves +12…60 % bits, E1d; plain clip with a lowpass not exact, E2); two limiter variants did not reduce away-moves (L3). "This is a design failure under the owner's rule, stated plainly." Coordinator and partner do NOT accept the proof (memo F2; §11.3).
- Motion levers with the partner (training clips traffic/winter @0.5, out/tune7.log): vertical search ±8 instead of ±4 (hwy has 11 % of |vy| > 4, others ≤ 3 %): −0.00…−0.02 NEG; ZTOL 0/1 instead of 2: +0.05…+0.13 NEG but 0 re-creates still-area flicker; level-1 ladder shift by the moving-block share (K = 0.5): −0.85 / −0.02 → none closes the gap. ### 12.2 Measurements and decisions so far (RESUME item table, DESIGN §A–§B, PARTNER_NOTES)
- **R0 bench reproduction:** dng720 @0.5 88.97 (88.10) 34.43/36.12/37.22; volley @0.5 91.17 (90.08) 39.57/42.67/45.29 = SA17 exactly (partner asked for this AND one gen-2 identical run before trusting D-numbers; the gen-2 run was never recorded — only the NEG/PSNR reproduction is; gen-2 exactness of ccv and the table bank is argued by construction, not measured). Measurement status labels (DESIGN): PAPER = argument only; PROXY = entropy screen; REAL = real code lengths from static tables trained on clips disjoint from the test cells, exact CBR, full frames, compared with today's real decodes; D8/D12/D13 are MC-prediction luma PSNR, not codec NEG.
- **D1 chroma row signature** (zero-detail synthesis, owner statistic row mod 4): killing the level-1 vertical details (LH1/HH1) 0.3–1.1 %; killing level 2 too (LH2/HH2) 3.4–4.5 % (rows 0, 3 low); + horizontal kills (HL1/HL2) Cb/Cr 10–12 %. Predictor tuning over 108 dyadic 2/4-tap pairs (vertical-only model): best 24 → 16 % → "structural to 2 nested dyadic vertical levels", not a root fix. Partner's root (Dir 3a): at 0.5 bpp chroma details mostly zero → the picture is the pyramid's implicit zero-detail interpolator, not phase-invariant over 4 rows, plus integer S-transform rounding favouring one row. **D1a** chroma 1 vertical level at real code lengths (SA17 tables): zero-detail stress ≤ 1.8 %; dng720 @0.5 86.53 vs 88.97 (−2.4 NEG, luma −1.27 dB), volley @0.5 89.79 (−1.4) → REJECTED (SA17's LV1 −1.8 dB was luma intra). Options SA18 offered after D1: (a) 1 vertical level for chroma only, (b) a perceptual allocation keeping chroma LH2/HH2 alive; SA18P rejected (b) as "content-dependent and not by construction" and asked for (a) with these checks: the stress test with all chroma details killed must fall to ≈ 1 %; real code lengths on all cells at 0.5/1.0 plus 0.25 bpp, and 4:4:4. As run, D1a used only dng720 + volley @0.5 with SA17 tables (stress ≤ 1.8 %); 0.25 bpp, 1.0 bpp, the other cells and 4:4:4 were NOT run — the rejection rests on 2 cells at one rate. SA18P's Dir 3b: "SA15 used 1V for 4:2:0 chroma; 4:2:2 chroma at 1V may cost far less"; SA18P excluded "temporal phase shifting (breaks still areas)" and "per-column phase offsets (separable transform)" — and later itself proposed F1, a column-dependent vertical phase pairing (D10, rejected). After D4b, SA18P sequenced: "D1 parked until the entropy fix lands". Partner: if costly, ask why the second vertical level must be dyadic and nested. **D10** staggered level-2 vertical pairing (partner F1, skewed/quincunx averaging, column-dependent vertical phase → row-uniform by construction; costs the partner stated in advance: "compaction, staircase blocks"): vertical kills rows 4.2 → 0.9 % but columns 0.9 → 5.3 %; with horizontal kills rows 4 → 40 % → REJECTED.
- **D2 never-away kill test** (oracle: best of 18 single ±1 re-choices of the coarse coefficients covering each case, whole-plane check): fixable cases cut24 @0.5 0.1 % (1/682), ext10 0.0 % (1,876 cases), dng720 32.7 % (150 sampled), cf_gfx 56 % (19/34) → two-choice (floor/ceil) coarse re-choice DEAD; "no predetermined re-choice rule can reach 100 %". After D2 SA18 asked whether to measure predict-only at level 1 only; SA18P said NO: "the coarse-grid quarter is still mean-paired, so not never-away; leaf/coarse positions create a phase class like D1"; and asked for "a precise claim, proof and oracle numbers per cell, plus the size of the away moves, for the coordinator (per-sample vs per-plane intent is the owner's call)", finishing dng720/ext10 first.
- **D4 fast motion, per-(plane, band, slice) prediction-weight oracle:** {1, 0}: floor 95.46 (+0.14), hwy 93.13 (+0.17), volley 91.18 (+0.01); {1, ½, 0} volley 91.14 → not the lever (per-band intra/inter mode or prediction shrink rejected).
- **D4b tables vs the frame's own empirical class entropy** (same contexts): volley @0.5 with hold 2.02× (class symbols ~85 % of bits), without hold 1.26×; dng720 with hold 1.47×; traffic (training, in-sample) 1.18× → ROOT (then): SA17 trained its tables WITHOUT the hold and measured WITH it. **T1** hold-trained sequence tables round 1 (out/tab_h_r1.pkl) @0.5: NO gain — dng720 88.93, volley 91.21, hwy 93.00, floor 95.35 (vs SA17 88.97/91.17/92.96/95.32); table/own-entropy ratio volley still 1.87× → the gap is content/slice NON-STATIONARITY, not train/test hold mismatch (ledger S5.388: ±0.05). Round 2 (out/tab_h_r2.pkl) running. SA18P's conditions for the entropy work: no reference-derived contexts (parse dependency breaks A5 and the join); stream-only contexts; "report table Mbit against 8K memory (SA15 #7)" [SA15 rejected #7 = 720 tables of L = 2048, 26.5 Mbit, not implementable]; judge only at real code lengths with disjoint tables trained with the hold.
- **D6 away moves by source type** (overshooting sample's source at a rail or not): natural content ALL "case 3" (source not at a rail = near-rail texture whose error exceeds its distance to the rail): dng720 388/36/2 away samples @0.5/1/2; cf_gfx 71/7/2; cut24 ~50/50; ext10 > 95 % rail-source.
- **DOL (partner's decoupled output layer; Laplacian pyramid, Burt–Adelson 1983; not in ledgers or SA12–17):** coarse pyramid internal only; output = clip(up(coarse) + per-sample residual). Kill test (gen 2 must re-read the same coarse indices from the output): 1-level LP, down = 2×2 mean, up = replicate (left inverse), coarse step 1–8, residual step 8–32: coarse re-read mismatch 11–40 % (dng720), 5–36 % (hwy). Reason (analytic): down(P1) = c + down(residual error) — the re-read sees the UNQUANTISED coarse value plus noise, which falls into another cell with probability ≈ |noise|/step; "the null-space fix = a wavelet again" → FALSIFIED. SA18P's claimed merits for DOL: "the clip is never-away by construction, still = dead-zone residual (no hold), text lives in the per-sample layer"; staged tests: (1) gen-2 re-read share Q_c(down(P1)) ≠ c; (2) the overcomplete price at 0.5 bpp at real code lengths + up() row phases — only (1) was run (it killed DOL); (2) was never reached.
- **IPL (interpolating pyramid with per-sample leaves on a row-uniform lattice, new):** every level splits samples into kept (real output pixels written at their coarsest level, never changed) and predicted (clip(P(final kept neighbours) + leaf)); no update; separable 4-point Deslauriers–Dubuc (−1, 9, 9, −1)/16, shift-add; vertical kept/predicted pattern SKEWED per column group so every row contains every lattice class at the same density; slices rate/packet units, continuous with ahead rows. Pre-set rule: IPL chosen if at real code lengths (tables trained for it) it costs ≤ 0.1 NEG intra and shows no lattice artifact on renders (partner added: Cb/Cr PSNR not lower; G2 banding check). Proxy −2.5…−3 dB luma at matched rate (dng720 40.95 vs 37.86; hwy 43.09 vs 40.33). REAL intra (tables trained identically for both arms): dng720 @0.5 IPL NEG 65.45 (64.50) PSNR 29.99/33.67/34.66 vs AVG 80.55 (79.57) 32.06/35.70/36.72 = −15 NEG, −2.1 dB every plane; INTER proxy (MC residual from real decoded references) volley/hwy ≈ −2.7 dB luma at matched rate — temporal prediction does not remove the predict-only penalty → KILLED. IPL risks/constraints (DESIGN A.2): (1) coding gain — predict-only lowpasses are aliased point samples; SA17's PROXY screen said +12…60 % bits, never measured at real code lengths, and "SA17's other proxy overstated a transform price by 2–6 points"; (2) "at low rate the kept samples keep texture while interpolated ones are smooth → a visible dot/diagonal lattice ('screen door') is possible; must be judged on renders, per plane"; constraints: same line buffers as AVG (4-tap vertical support = 2 rows each side per level), shift-add, one pass; IP interpolating wavelets / lifting (Deslauriers–Dubuc 1989, Sweldens 1996). Planned decisive test (MAIN 13:51): 6 cells @0.5/1.0 + renders + SA18P's G2 banding check; REPORTED: dng720 @0.5 intra only (65.45 vs 80.55) plus the inter proxy (volley/hwy) — no renders, no lattice judgement; the kill rests on one cell/rate + the proxy. IPL-in-INTER screens requested by SA18P, "per cell + worst frame": (1) uncovered/fast-motion residuals are structured; (2) the one-way cycle-2 wave (≈ half the picture per frame) needs refresh predicted from clean regions (SA16 barrier), still < 4 frames; (3) heal/ramp invisible and monotone; note for §A: "prediction as the anti-alias lowpass"; the actual kill used the inter PROXY (≈ −2.7 dB luma at matched rate), not the per-cell > 0.2 NEG criterion. Partner's kill criteria for IPL-in-inter: > 0.2 NEG below AVG-inter on any motion cell @0.5, or one-way not < 4 frames without intra. IPL-in-INTER was SA18's proposal (MAIN 14:01: residuals noise-like, so the predict-only penalty may be small; stated risk "intra heals/wave at −3 dB"); SA18P supplied the screens and kill criteria. SA18P's earlier questions on IPL (PARTNER l.28: "explain why it beats SA15 #1 / SA17 #3; check aliasing/noise on the kept grid") were never answered.

### 12.3 Never-away wide search (DESIGN §A.0, §A.5; ledger S5.385)
- SA18P's opening record check (PARTNER l.4): ledger grepped for "censor", "rail-aware" and "rounding-bias" — nothing found (the evidence behind its "new, not in records" labels). SA18P's rebuttal of SA17's never-away proof (PARTNER l.9): SA17's proof shows only that a MEAN-PRESERVING legaliser must push the partner along (1, −1); "a per-sample clip is always toward the source (source is legal) and touches nothing else; it failed only on gen-2 reading (SA17 rejected #2, tested for update-lifting). Root: exactness is obtained by 're-analysis lands on the lattice'." Dir 2a as proposed: CENSORED canonical reading — a sample at a rail means "internal value ≥ rail" (≤ for the low rail); gen 1 emits the canonical coefficients whose CLIPPED synthesis equals its own output (its own coefficients are a witness, so one exists); gen 2 reads the same; question to test first: can this reading be one-pass for the pair pyramid (children censored → parent mean uncertain), fixed work? Dir 2b: rail-aware two-candidate (ECSQ, legal-cleared) choice of the COARSE index with the descendants' clamp outcome costed for both; "measure the share of clamp cases where one candidate gives zero away-moves before building" (→ SA18's D2 oracle). (Dir 2a's later "dropped" verdict is below.)
- SA18P's root chain for still areas and text (PARTNER l.5): "detail freezes ← hold rule keeps the step a region had when it went still ← refinement of still content = flicker ← a still coefficient's value moves whenever its step changes AND source noise is re-coded each frame. Two separable causes; SA17 fixed both with one freeze." (Both SA18's later events test and the noise-vs-refinement exchange, §12.2, depend on this split.)
- **A.0 dichotomy (SA18):** every current goal except efficiency is decided by one structural choice — is an output sample ever an input to an average that something else reads? If yes (any lowpass with an update/averaging step, incl. 5/3, S-transform pairs, lapped transforms), exactness forces the legaliser to preserve that average (gen 2 re-reads it), pushing the overshoot onto the partner(s) in a direction the decoder cannot know. If no (every output sample written once from final values plus its own leaf), a plain per-sample clip is legal, never-away (moves an overshooting value to the rail, between it and the in-range source), exact (gen 2 reads leaf = sample − prediction(final values); smallest reproducing index canonical) and free = the predict-only family. Proof sketch: per-sample never-away + exactness ⇒ no averaged sample is ever legalised ⇒ predict-only. Owner: A.0 is not an answer (it only bounds the considered space); widen.
- Architectures written (§A): A.1 **AVG** (constraints: latency 2S + 10 lines, 8K memory fits (SA16/17 paper), one pass, royalty-free (Haar/S-transform, 2/6)) (continuous averaging pair pyramid, leaf-interval legality — the SA15–17 family re-derived; seams/smudges met, legal at zero cost, exact, loss < 4 frames measured by SA15–17; never-away fails; chroma row signature per D1/D1a; latency 2S + 10 lines; 8K memory fits (SA16/17 paper); kept as the efficiency reference); A.2 **IPL** (killed, above); A.3 **DOL** (killed, above).
- **A.5 table of ~15 families** ("case 3" = overshooting sample whose source is not at a rail; D6: 100 % of AVG's away moves on natural content): L1 per-sample clip after synthesis, predict-only (IPL) — never-away + exact + free by construction, efficiency dead (−15 NEG intra, ≈ −2.7 dB inter); L2 leaf clamp, mean-preserving (AVG) — exact, free, not never-away (D2, SA17 §3.2); L3 rank-order lowpass (coarse = min/max of a pair) — never-away at the rail on the extreme side, exact; erosion/dilation bias = colour cast (G6), no anti-aliasing (paper); L4 encoder-side closed loop "no overshoot in own streams" — decoder clamp never fires; partner harm relocated into the encoder's constrained choice (b = 2m − a) (paper); L5 injective dequantisation (SA14 IDQ) — moves samples away (107k); L6 rail symbols in the entropy stage (SA14 RAIL±) — +10.9 % bits on a graded frame; L7 fold at the rail (hi + o → hi − o) — never farther than the pre-legal value but a rail source gets dips (white plate → grey speckle) (paper); L8 overshoot-conditional correction symbol — breaks gen 2 (the corrected sample enters gen 2's averages) + bits (paper); L9 lattice-preserving legaliser (sum changes by 2k·s so the mean stays on its lattice) — the mean is synthesised: changing it = changing an ancestor index (sibling/whole block moves, unsigned); also rewrites a mean after neighbours read it (partner, paper); DOL — gen-2 coarse re-read mismatch 5–40 %; RC rail-conditional structure switch keyed on final coarse values — PROPOSED by SA18P as its third architecture ("pairs whose final coarse m and step allow overshoot (|m − rail| < max|d|/2) become IPL leaves, all others AVG"; SA18P's risks: IPL share on cf_gfx, the switch boundary = a per-region parameter, clamp-independent contexts), KILLED by SA18 at MAIN 13:51: the switch is decided top-down from the final m but m's meaning (average vs kept pixel) depends on the switch → m ↔ switch fixed point (trial/iteration); a legaliser-only "clip a, keep b" breaks gen 2; SA18P then agreed to record it as considered (a source-decided transmitted flag mismatches at gen 2 + zero-visible-steps) (paper); F2 switch keyed on causally final data (the decoded reference) — not by construction on cuts/moving rails; parse/structure depends on history (loss, join; S5.29 class) (partner, paper); F3 (SA18P's admissibility condition: "one fixed point, allowed only if provably stable after one re-choice") rail-excluded structure (samples exactly at a rail are known values outside all averages; non-R clamped to [lo+1, hi−1]) — by construction for plates and clipped highlights; does NOT touch case 3 (D6); e_m inward rounding of ancestor means near rails (encoder or decoder side) — a detail moves its two children in opposite directions; the inward margin needed at the top grows with depth (LL forced inward = darkening = harm); conflicts where both rails share a support (partner, paper; D2 oracle 0–56 %). Other families, with proposer and killer: censored canonical reading (proposed by SA18P, DROPPED by SA18P itself) (a rail sample means "internal value ≥ rail") — DROPPED after the coordinator's record check: same as SA13's REXT in-cell projection (no convergence on cut24) and SA17 E2 (18–282 misses); uncertainty climbs every level, no one-pass reading; rail-aware two-candidate coarse rounding (ECSQ; proposed by SA18P, killed by SA18's D2 oracle); a clip-robust overcomplete coarse functional (SA18's question at MAIN 14:09; SA18P answered that none exists) ("exact invariance ⇒ kernel ⇒ critical sampling; a quantised f flips at cell edges"; the aligned-lattice relaxation works only with a per-sample last layer); PREVENT (proposed by SA18P, killed by SA18 at MAIN 14:13; continuous step field from the decoded coarse value's distance to the rail so the worst-case error bound < distance, clamp never fires; plates via F3) — SA18: not by construction (a source can sit at hi − 1 whatever the coarse value → needs lossless in [hi − E, hi); becomes a rail-weighted allocation; near-rail share of test cells chroma 0 %, luma 0–3 % within 40 codes, much more on dark/PQ content; SA18P had asked for bits and NEG at matched rate with the worst-case bound on dng720, cf_gfx and a dark cell, plus the bound's looseness, "only then write §A" — none of it was run; the kill rests on paper plus the near-rail share); clip + nearest/directed lattice reading (SA18P's "aligned-lattice relaxation (gen 2 re-chooses c′)", which SA18P itself judged to work only with a per-sample last layer; costed by SA18 at MAIN 14:13) — guarantee needs overshoot < LL step/2 = L4; SA18P's family list ★1 quantiser-stage inward rounding of the ancestor mean (depends on inequality slack; SA18P asked to "measure: share of away cases with e_m > 0 (theory 100 %), |e_m| per level; frequency and cost of high+low conflict supports", in the order "1's slack, 3 on paper, then the IPL A/B" — none of it was run; the verdict rests on paper (margin grows with depth → darkening) and D2's single-re-choice oracle), 2 decoder-known inward reconstruction offset near rails (same sibling conflict + MSE), 3 (hi, lo, flag) pair description → axis-aligned legal box (open: what the parent reads), 4 rail mask as don't-care (gen-2 mask drift, bits), 5 fixed point of clip∘decode∘encode (ledger ~2437–2643/2821 + SA17 E2 + SA13 → dead). Families SA18 asked SA18P to add (MAIN 13:55): "non-dyadic, non-separable, canonical-adaptive, reading approaches" — only F1 (skewed/quincunx averaging) came back; the others were never costed and remain RESUME routes (b)/(e). Partner's sufficient condition: at a high-rail overshoot (ov ≤ e_a), b moves toward the source iff e_m ≤ (e_a − ov/2)/2; e_m ≤ 0 ⇒ both toward always → root = the ancestor mean's error has the wrong sign at the rail.
- Where the chain ends for case 3 (SA18): an overshooting sample and its partner share a synthesised mean whose inherited error the decoder cannot sign; any exact legaliser must preserve what gen 2 re-reads; a by-construction guarantee needs the sample's LAST write to be private (no averaging) = L1; the open question is L1's price. Pre-set rule (A.4): IPL chosen if at real code lengths it costs ≤ 0.1 NEG intra and shows no lattice artifact; "otherwise the dichotomy of A.0 is reported to the owner as a measured conflict between 'never away (per sample)' and 'efficiency ≥ today', with AVG's away-move sizes (count, max, share of plane MSE vs a clip)". §A.4 choice: IPL failed → legality core = AVG, "re-derived here from A.0/A.5 and D2/D6, not inherited"; never-away per sample reported as the design's failure (partner: "design FAILS never-away per sample", never "structural"). Coordinator (S5.385, S5.388): keep OPEN. RESUME "OPEN goal items": frontier premises P1 "gen 2 re-reads everything the synthesis reads" and P2 "averaging is needed for efficiency" (measured only vs IPL); unexplored around P1: (a) a representation where the synthesis reads values gen 2 gets from somewhere other than the picture analysis (e.g. the reference frame, which gen 2 shares exactly, for inter frames); (b) readings that are many-to-one but canonical; around P2: (c) partially averaging structures (update only where it matters, e.g. coarse levels averaged, finest per-sample with a different geometry); (d) adaptive predict-only with a better interpolator (edge-directed / longer support; IPL used a 4-point linear interpolator only); (e) non-dyadic or non-separable predict-only lattices (quincunx) with less aliased coarse samples; (f) averaging whose update reads only TRANSMITTED leaves (SA14-style) combined with a per-sample final clip, cost/exactness uncosted.

### 12.4 Design so far — "CFV-1" (DESIGN §B; continuous pair pyramid + coarse-first vectors + selectable entropy tables)
- B.1 transform and legality = AVG (2 quad levels + 3 horizontal pair levels over the whole picture; S-transform mean with position-parity rounding; difference predicted from neighbouring FINAL means (2/6 slope), coded as a leaf; slices rate/packet units; leaf clamped into the exact set keeping its pair's two outputs legal, from final values; any bitstream legal; no symbol spent; a legal source passes unchanged). Why not the alternatives: IPL −15 NEG intra / −2.7 dB inter; DOL fails gen 2; continuous 5/3 5–15 % chroma row class (SA17 E1); slice-closed transforms make slice-edge rows (SA12–14); pair vs 5/3 price ≈ 0 (SA17 EV-B). Known failure: never-away per sample (case 3).
- B.2 as of DESIGN 16:12 (supersedes the 15:09 text kept below): two TRANSMITTED fields — V_h history field (D(t−1) vs D(t−2), ±16 × ±4, row-regularised, zero preferred within 2 codes/pixel) predicts the coarse bands LL, H5, H4, H3; coarse-first field V_2: once a slice's coarse bands are decoded, its level-2 LL2(t) (quarter resolution) is known exactly to every encoder, re-encoder and decoder; per 64×8 block among the 25 candidates {V_h + d : d ∈ [−2, 2]²} (fixed order, zero first) take the one whose block-shifted reference has the level-2 LL closest to LL2(t) (SAD per block; each candidate scored as a uniform field, no block coupling); V_2 predicts the level-2 AND level-1 bands; sent as a difference to V_h; its bits capped per slice by a fixed allowance (2.5 % of the slice budget, raster order, excess blocks fall back to V_h — deterministic). Coding order inside a slice: coarse bands → LL2 → V_2 → level-2 and level-1 bands. Why: D8 staleness; D9 source-searched vectors not re-derivable; D14 35.51 / 34.91 (quarter-res), D13c 35.74 / 35.23 (half-res). Rate control per RESUME B2: fine-band plan index kf with canonical reading read2 (replaces the d re-choice).
- B.2 as of DESIGN 15:09 (superseded; both fields TRANSMITTED because "the decoder never derives vectors; S5.29, IP note in the ledger") temporal = value-domain coefficient prediction from D(t−1) with TWO transmitted vector fields: V_h history field (D(t−1) vs D(t−2), 64×8 blocks, ±16 × ±4, regularised) for the coarse bands (LL, H5–H3, level 2); V_c coarse-first field for the level-1 bands (after a slice's coarse bands are decoded, its level-1 LL1(t) at half resolution is known exactly to every encoder, re-encoder and decoder; per 64×8 block among the 18 candidates {V_h + d, V_c2 + d : d ∈ {−1,0,1}²}, V_c2 = match of LL1(t) against the reference's LL1 × 2, take the candidate whose OBMC prediction's LL1 best matches LL1(t) (SAD, uniform-field evaluation, ties to the earlier candidate); sent as a difference to V_h). Why: D8/D9/D13b above. Rate control with V_c: slice plan chosen with level-1 costs under V_h; then ONE predetermined re-choice of the level-1 exponents, pass-1 exponents + d, the finest d ∈ [−4, 8] that fits the remaining budget with V_c's real costs; never over budget; savings carry as credit.
- B.3 entropy: per leaf magnitude class (static tANS L = 1024), raw mantissa, sign; context left + above class (in-slice); tables keyed by (luma/chroma, band group, intra/inter, step bucket), trained on 5 disjoint clips WITH the still-hold on (T1); per (band, slice) the encoder picks the cheapest of T skew variants p_λ(c) ∝ p(c)·2^(−λc) and sends its index (a deterministic function of the indices → re-encoders make the same choice). Why: T1 retraining gained ±0.05 NEG; the 1.87× gap is non-stationarity (D11 −26.0 % volley, −5.4 % dng720, −4.7 % hwy class bits incl. side bits).
- "Numbers for the full design are in §5" — §5 still not written at the 19:35 snapshot (DESIGN.md 19:17 has §A, §B, §4, §6, §7; §12.5). Configuration per RESUME/run_final.sh/DESIGN 19:17: cfv2 vectors + hold1 + texture reconstruction offset ro 2 + tables tab_f1, table bank dropped, tilt 0.25.

### 12.5 DESIGN.md as of 19:17 — "CFV-1: continuous pair pyramid + coarse-first vectors + hold-once still areas" (§A unchanged; §B rewritten; §4, §6, §7 new; §5 numbers not yet written)
- B.1 transform/legality and B.2 coarse-first vectors: as in §12.4 (B.2 = the V_2/LL2 text).
- **B.3 Quantisation and texture reconstruction:** leaf value = base + recon(q); recon(q) = q·2^e for the coarse bands; for the level-1/2 bands of INTER frames recon(q) = q·2^e + sign(q)·2^(e−2) (nonzero indices reconstruct ¼ step outward: a canonical texture reconstruction offset, normative, same at both ends). Encoder dead zone ρ = 0.35. Plan ladder: 30 (plane, band) exponents, one band coarser per plan step, order from the synthesis gains + 0.25-octave tilt per level toward the fine bands (tilt on city @0.5: 0.25 → 93.95, 0.5 → 93.62, 0.75 → 93.50). Canonical index = smallest |q| whose (clamped) reconstruction reproduces the final leaf; the offset keeps this one-to-one ("tested: chain exact with the offset on"). Evidence E8: dng720 89.35 → 89.59, volley 91.72 → 91.86.
- **B.4 Entropy:** per leaf magnitude class (static tANS, L = 1024) + raw mantissa + sign; context = classes of left and above neighbours inside the slice (indices only: independent of the decoder's clamps; packets parse independently); tables keyed by (luma/chroma, band group, intra/inter, step bucket) = 82 tables × 4 contexts, trained on 5 disjoint clips UNDER THE FINAL CONFIGURATION (T2) [MISMATCH, from out/train_f.sh vs out/run_final.sh: tab_f1 was trained WITH the table bank on (`export SA18_BANK=…`, still_hold 1, ccv 2, no recoff) while the final eval and DESIGN B.4 run WITHOUT the bank (`unset SA18_BANK`) and WITH ro2 (E8 came after training) — so DESIGN's "trained … UNDER THE FINAL CONFIGURATION" does not match train_f.sh; the same class as SA17's train-without-hold/measure-with-hold mismatch (§14 item 1: tables must be trained on the design they are measured with)]. Dropped: the per-(band, slice) bank of 8 skew variants — removes 5–26 % of class bits (D11) but only +0.07/+0.11 NEG in the full codec (E10) and cannot be built at 8K (48 Mbit stored, or ~240 table builds per 31 µs slice period).
- **B.5 Still areas — hold once, then update only on change (owner rulings in force):** encoder-only rule: a coefficient of a zero-vector block coded once since it went still (or since an intra/heal) is held (index 0 → value = prediction = previous value) until the SOURCE changes by more than ¾ of the step it was coded at; no scheduled, age-based or pulsing refinement; state per (64×8 block, band): one exponent + the still flag. Measured: frozen input 0 changed samples Y/Cb/Cr from frame 2. Built, measured, rejected: fixed-frame / still-age events (C2: +1.2 volley / +2.4 dng720 NEG but ≈ 70 % of still samples change by ≈ 6 codes per event = refresh-pulse class); error-driven absolute-threshold hold (C3: 33–62 % change every frame, mean 6.5 → 3.4 codes, no stop in 10 frames = ants class; NEG 89.11 < hold1 on dng720); current-plan octave re-coding (= SA17 hold2, 18–71 %/frame). Consequence (open goal-4 item): on static-camera content still areas keep the quality of the frame in which they went still; root chain: still content first coded under a budget shared with motion (or at a cut) → exact CBR per frame (rev. 4, no cross-frame credit) and one reference frame (C2) give a still region at most one frame's bits per visible change → today reaches its higher static quality by changing still samples every frame (its 60–77 % "ants"), which the owner forbids; levers that raise what the one update buys (B.3 offset, tilt) are in.
- **B.6 Rate control:** per slice, code lengths of all 16 exponents of every band computed in parallel from open-loop symbols; plan k = finest ladder plan fitting B + credit − header − V_2 allowance − 16-bit reserve; after the coarse bands are decoded and V_2 known, ONE predetermined re-choice of the fine bands' own plan index k_f (finest that fits with V_2's real costs; coarse exponents stay at k), from the same parallel table; emission = canonical reading of the encoder's own picture (coarse plan = coarsest consistent on the coarse bands, k_f = coarsest consistent on the fine bands, smallest indices); unspent bits carry as credit within the frame.
- **B.7 Slices, packets, loss:** slice = S rows (4 at 720p, 8 above), one packet, one (k, k_f); transform continuous; packet k carries the coarse leaves of the blocks below that slice k's pairs read (a packing order). Two-way: per-slice loss flags; on-demand heal of rows [kS − m, (k+1)S + m], m = 16 + 8(RT + 1), intra in frame t + RT + 1 inside the fixed budget; vectors transmitted so the decoder's prediction state after the heal equals the encoder's ("§3.5" of DESIGN, not yet written). One-way: cycle-2 column wave with the clean-region rule (SA17's mechanism, to be re-measured); the coarse-first field is derived from the current frame's decoded data, clean wherever the slice is clean.
- **B.8 Formats:** transform, legality intervals and canonical reading depth- and range-agnostic (lo/hi from the format; 8/10/12-bit); 4:2:2 chroma half-width planes with vectors halved (2-tap half-sample average); 4:4:4 full planes; 4:2:0 would halve chroma rows (one vertical chroma level fewer); 720p50 3:1 conversion outside the core, as today.
- **§4 Work, latency, memory (PAPER):** decoder ≈ 83 ops/sample (synthesis + leaf clamps ≈ 25; OBMC twice ≈ 2 × 16; forward analysis of the two predictions ≈ 12 + 10; entropy ≈ 4) ≈ 3× a JPEG XS decoder; per slice W·S·(25 + 32 + 22 + 4) per plane sample. Encoder ≈ 415 ops/sample (SA17's ≈ 330 incl. canonical reading ≈ 48 and history vectors ≈ 150; + V_2 scoring ≈ 55: 25 candidates, 4×4 box means by separable running sums ≈ 2 adds per candidate per full-res sample + quarter-res SAD; + second prediction/analysis ≈ 28); one plan decision + one predetermined fine-plan re-choice + one emission pass per slice. Latency 2S + 10 lines (capture S + look-ahead 8 + one slice period + ≈ 2 pipeline) → 18 at 720p (S = 4), 26 at 1080p–8K; JPEG XS ≈ 32. Coarse-first order adds no capture wait but a compute stage (coarse synthesis → LL2 → V_2 → fine bands) that must be pipelined per 64-column block, "otherwise it costs up to S/2 line times; at 720p50 with the 3:1 conversion (0.99 ms today) even 2 lines (53 µs) would break 1 ms. RISK: this pipelining is argued, not built." Memory (vs DDR_WINDOW_CACHE.md §7.3), decoder 8K60 4:2:2 10-bit: reference ring ≈ 44 rows × 15,360 × 10 bit = 6.8 Mbit (window grows by V_2's ±2 rows); coefficient buffers 7.9 + 3.9 (second prediction's fine-band analysis) = 11.8; output lines 1.2; tANS tables 328 distributions × 1024 × 19 bit ≈ 6.4 → ≈ 26.2 Mbit vs today's 29.23 (narrowed store; ZU7EV-class 27 URAM + 11 BRAM); 8K 4:4:4 12-bit ≈ 39 Mbit — over the part, like today's 47.2 ("not solved here"). DDR 20 bit/sample (today 26), both vector fields read from the same on-chip window; encoder-only state still flag + one exponent per (64×8 block, band) ≈ 0.16 bit/sample plus V_h/V_2 fields (a few kbit per slice row).
- **§6 IP provenance (DESIGN's table):** S-transform pair / 2/6 slope (Haar / S-transform 1970s–80s; TS/CREW 2/6 mid-1990s) expired; lifting, position-parity rounding (Sweldens 1996; own) open/own; leaf-interval legality, canonical reading, nested dyadic ladder own (after SA15); texture reconstruction offset (generic reconstruction-point choice, Lloyd-Max 1960s) expired/generic; OBMC bilinear block-centre weights (H.263 Annex F era, 1996) expired; history block matching (textbook) and coarse-first candidate selection (own; vectors transmitted); Exp-Golomb vector differences (1978) expired; static tANS (Duda; no rANS) open; on-demand heal (owner ruling S5.37) and cycle-2 column wave (GDR, H.263/MPEG-2 era) own/expired.
- **§7 Prior failures checked (DESIGN's table):** slice-closed transforms → continuous transform; continuous 5/3 row class → pair vertical; 2-level vertical pair 4-row chroma signature → root found (D1), NOT solved (1 chroma level −1.4…−2.4 NEG, tuning 24 → 16 %, staggering moves it to columns); lattice lock → value-domain prediction; stale history vectors → coarse-first LL2 vectors; source-searched vectors → not used (S5.152, D9); decoder-derived vectors → transmitted (S5.29); per-band intra/inter mode → not used (D4 +0.01…+0.17); entropy-estimate claims → real code lengths; tables trained without the operating state → retrained under the final configuration (T2); table pooling → keyed by step bucket; exact hold not gen-2 consistent (SA16) → hold = encoder choice of zero indices read back canonically; hold freezes still content (SA17 F1a) → "measured as the main remaining gap; every catch-up form tried violates the flicker/pulse rulings"; never away → NOT solved (~15 families, A.5); predict-only legality → IPL −15 NEG; Laplacian output layer → gen-2 mismatch 5–40 %; rail symbols / IDQ / REXT → not used.

C3 flickered because each re-code went to the CURRENT step (partial, 4–5 changes/sample)." The form S5.390 allows: error > T → re-code that region ONCE to a canonical FINAL step, paid from padding only, worst-error regions first, every sample changes once; risk a short sharp/soft mosaic, never measured → measure its duration in frames, changes per sample, renders, NEG volley/dng720. (2) agree: retrain for 1V chroma before rejecting; accept D10. (3) agree: slack + PREVENT numbers. (4) rail18 gen-2 bit differences + 50 overs = correctness blockers, first. ## §13 Cross-designer cause → effect (which combination of choices produced which result; every row cites where it is recorded above)

| Continuous vertical 5/3 across slices (coordinator) | seam-free on paper, but 9–21 lines lookahead, 720p50 4:2:0 misses 1 ms, +3–4 Mbit at 8K, A5 damage across slices, XS resemblance | §4.3 |

| Closed per-slice pyramid, true context above, extrapolation below (SA12) | inter step REDUCED (not removed) with OBMC + encoder error continuation: +0.04/+0.03/+0.06 (r5 +0.03…0.08) vs today +0.15/+0.14/+0.30; INTRA step ≈ today (+0.16/+0.18/+0.32; +0.13/+0.13/+0.24 with the default intra continuation, which was later DROPPED as symptom treatment, S5.369); cause = the causal slice cut (tall slice +0.01) | §6.3–6.5 |

### 13.2 Legality and exactness
| Choice | Result | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Clip after a lossy inverse (today) | destroys information → lock/repair/search families | §1.7, §3 |
| Clamp in synthesis with an update reading clamped values | cyclic; acyclic one-sided updates = predict-only (−2.4…−4.8 dB) | §4.3 |
| Predict-only / pixel-owned closed loop (DESIGN3, SA11 S2/S5b, SA13 T1, SA15 T1, SA17 E1d, SA18 IPL) | legal + exact trivially; −1.4…−5 dB (NEG too: dng1080 85.4 vs 91.1); IPL −15 NEG intra real, −2.7 dB inter | §5, §7.0, §9.1, §11.2, §12.2 |
| Vertical-causal + clamp + own-decode requantisation (SA11 E1q/E2qK1) | exact on natural except spot @1.0 (E1q) / 1 index (E2qK1); rails settle at g3 | §5.3 |
| Per-pair interval clamp in an exact-sum pair pyramid (SA12) | 0 oob; 216/216 rail cases g2 | §6.1 |
| REXT + ≤ 1 in-cell projection round (SA13) | natural exact; cut24 not exact in fixed work; lossless fallback too costly | §7.1, §7.2 |
| Leaf-reading lifting (update reads transmitted leaves) (SA14) | acyclic with full 5/3 update, ±0.5 % BD vs 5/3 | §8.1 |
| Closure (iterate until no overshoot) (SA14 r1) | ≤ 6 rounds measured, no proven bound | §8.1 |
| IDQ (SA14 r2) | one pass, legal, exact; moves samples AWAY (107k vs 300k toward) | §8.2 |
| IDQ + source-faithful one-pass index choice, no rails (SA14 r3 v4) | still moves more samples away than toward on graded720 (171k/217k away vs 47k/53k toward) and −0.59/−0.61 dB Y vs the clip codec with the same indices; natural cells within 0.003 dB | §8.3 |
| Leaf-interval clamp from final values, pair written last (SA15/16/17/18) | 0 oob, exact, zero bits on natural/graphics; never-away fails (~50/50 SA15; ~54 % SA17; up to 75 % ext10) because the pair mean is preserved (SA16 contributes only "0 oob": its legality bit cost, MSE vs clip and toward/away were never measured) | §9.2, §10.1, §11.2, §12.3 |
| Canonical index = smallest reproducing index; plan read from the picture; gen 1 emits the reading (SA15 R8, SA16, SA17) | gen 2 identical pictures + bits (SA15, SA16, SA17); gen 3 identical only in SA16 (3 generations × 6 frames, on SA16's OLD ladder with holds ON — not the final design) and SA17 (gen 2/3); SA15 did NOT run g3/g10 | §9.2, §10.3, §11.2 |
| Pair rounding choice (SA12 r1) | floor mean → −1.3/−1.4 code Cb/Cr cast at 0.19 bpp; checkerboard parity → PER up to 3.8; per-stage alternating → +0.5 code residual cast; exact sums with a fixed odd unit → PER up to 18; exact sums + pseudo-random σ + DC offset ½ → cast ≤ 0.06 code, PER at/below today on 20/24 | §6.1 |
| Rounding and DC reconstruction (SA13) | half-to-even lifting + DC band at cell centre → level offsets ≤ \|0.06\| on 29/36 plane-runs (worst +0.22); floor rounding control −1.5/−1.6/−1.7; DC β = 3/8 → −0.6…−1.2 uniform offset | §7.2 |
| Position-parity rounding π (SA14), position-alternating rounding (SA15), alternating phase (SA16), parity rounding (SA17) | SA14: removes the +0.6-code cast of floor rounding; SA15: −0.07…−0.21 → ±0.02; SA16: claimed, never measured | §8.1, §9.1 |
| Vertical-causal row predictor (a+2b+c+2)>>2, half-up (SA11) | uniform +0.9…+1.2 code cast on all planes | §5.4 |
| SA13 fixed-work rail mechanisms | T5 clip + re-read + lossless pixel residual: exact but residual 30–75 % of bits on cut24/ext10; T10 emit-what-the-next-encoder-reads: 20/81 (K1 D14) and 11/81 (K4 D40) units never reach a fixed point in 8 iterations; REXT alone: +4 dB on cut24 (rails exact) but flips remain; lossless unit fallback: removed (cost) | §7.1, §7.2 |

In progress (19:35 snapshot, 0.5 bpp only): SA18 event arms beat today on dng720 (91.55/91.77 vs 90.16), volley (92.96/92.97 vs 92.37), hwy (93.96 vs 93.73) at the cost of 70 %-sample refinement pulses — withdrawn (S5.390); kept configuration: dng720 89.59, volley 91.86 (hold1 + cfv2 + ro2, E8, tab_h_r1 + bank); hwy 93.84 (bank, C1) / 93.77 (no bank, E10) — hold1 + cfv2 WITHOUT ro2 (E8 ran only dng720 and volley); final 12-point eval killed at the stop (only dng720: @0.5 89.75 vs 90.16, @1.0 94.35 vs 94.05, on mismatched tab_f1 — not decisive) | §11.2, §12.2 |

| SA14 | today's model (34 at 1080p) | ≈ 1 MB line buffers (30 % URAM) | r1: enc ≈ 29 N + ME + packs + closure; dec ≈ 21 N (XS ≈ 17 N); r2 removed the closure (IDQ, one pass) |

| SA18 (DESIGN §4, 19:17, PAPER) | 2S + 10 = 18 / 26; + coarse-first compute stage that must be pipelined per 64-column block or it costs up to S/2 line times (RISK: argued, not built; 2 lines = 53 µs would break 1 ms at 720p50 + 3:1) | ≈ 26.2 Mbit decoder at 8K 4:2:2 10b (today 29.23); 8K 4:4:4 12b ≈ 39 Mbit, over the part (today 47.2); DDR 20 bit/sample; table bank dropped | dec ≈ 83 ops/sample (≈ 3× XS); enc ≈ 415 |

## §14 Open problems (as recorded at compile time, SA18 items at its 19:45 stop; S5.387 short pack §5, MEMO_SA17_FINDINGS §5, SA18 RESUME/DESIGN, ledger S5.385/S5.388)
1. **Efficiency (goal 4):** no finished design meets it at real code lengths (best finished: SA17, ≥ today on 4 of 12). New lever (owner C2 ruling S5.393): multi-frame PREDICTION is permitted if the references are compressed and nothing else is damaged (library still forbidden). At SA18's final snapshot (19:50): SA18's event arms (E3, E5 C2) beat today on dng720/volley/hwy at 0.5 bpp but were withdrawn under the owner's ruling (S5.390); the error-driven C3 was rejected for flicker; the kept configuration (hold1 + cfv2 + ro2) reached dng720 89.59, volley 91.86 (tab_h_r1 + bank); hwy only without ro2: 93.84 (C1 with bank; 93.77 without) vs today 90.16 / 92.37 / 93.73 — SA18 stated a static-camera gap (expected volley −0.5, dng720 −0.6); the final eval was killed at the stop (dng720 @0.5 89.75, @1.0 94.35 on mismatched tab_f1). Untested proposal at the stop (SA18Q/SA18P): region-once refinement to a canonical FINAL step paid from the 60–80 % padding of still inter frames (kill risk: an HVBC-class grid step while refined and held blocks coexist). SA18's conclusion: "STILL AREAS = main efficiency gap" (volley no-hold ceiling 93.50). Recorded causes still open: detail loss on text and textured still content at 0.5 bpp tied to how still content is held (SA17 hold; SA18 D3 decomposition to do); the fast-motion deficit (owner S5.390 addendum 2: motion quality is an important avenue, QUEUED for the three-agent big-picture phase, not now; S5.391: multi-reference prediction queued there too) — SA18 names history-vector STALENESS as the root and adopted coarse-first vectors (exact by construction; E2 at the LL1 stage +0.27/+0.32 NEG, partly measured over budget through the CBR accounting bug; LL2-stage cfv2 in E5, "+0.84 on hwy vs SA17" per MAIN 16:23); table non-stationarity (1.87× own entropy; SA18 bank −5…−26 % class bits, but only +0.07/+0.11 NEG in the full codec (E10) → bank DROPPED as 8K-infeasible: 48 Mbit, or ~240 table builds per 31 µs slice); SA18's partner flagged a conflict with S5.152 (source search −0.8 dB in today's codec) — SA18 states it is explained by committed-SAD gates (explained on paper, not replicated). Today's codec's perceptual allocation and texture rounding bias are recorded as design elements, not knobs (MEMO_SA16/SA17 hints). Tables must be trained on the same design they are measured with.
2. **"Never away" (goal 2) per sample:** unsolved and kept OPEN by the coordinator (S5.385, S5.388); SA18's frontier premises P1 ("gen 2 re-reads everything the synthesis reads") and P2 ("averaging is needed for efficiency", measured only vs IPL) are to be challenged; unexplored routes listed in §12.3 (a)–(f). Natural-content away moves are all "case 3" (near-rail texture, source not at a rail).
3. **Chroma row signature** of 2 nested dyadic vertical pair levels (2.5–7 %; zero-detail 3.4–4.5 %, 10–12 % with horizontal kills); one vertical level costs −1.4…−2.4 NEG (chroma-only; CAVEAT per SA18Q: measured with tables trained for two chroma levels on 2 cells at one rate — a train/measure mismatch, so not yet a valid rejection) or −1.8 dB (all planes); staggered pairing moves it to columns (D10).
4. **Still areas at zero change without an efficiency cost:** SA17's hold works (0 change) but costs up to 1.7 NEG and freezes texture; exact holds broke gen 2 (SA16); octave refinements flicker (SA17 hold2); SA18 E3/E5: lifting the hold gains +1.6…+2.7 NEG (volley no-hold ceiling 93.50) but its scheduled events change 70 % of still samples by ~6 codes per event (frozen input) — owner S5.390: no scheduled/still-age events, update only on change, no pulse; SA18's error-driven hold C3 (absolute per-band threshold) was REJECTED for flicker (33–62 % of still luma samples changing every frame, 4–5+ changes per sample); SA18 kept hold1 and states a static-camera efficiency gap; SA18P's unclosed lever: better one-shot intra coding of the go-still frame.
5. **Encoder mid-stream join in return-path mode** (no scheduled refresh): no convergence mechanism (SA17; SA18Q's question "name what makes it converge" unanswered at SA18's stop); SA12's contiguous-sweep join and SA16's column-band join both relied on a scheduled refresh.
6. **One-way links:** cycle 2 costs 0.41…3.39 NEG against SA17's on-demand mode and −4.05…+0.12 against today (12 points, SA17 DESIGN §6.2 tables; the source's own "1.0–3.4" is a range error) (owner accepts a cost, not trailing damage); long waves are unusable (owner, S5.378 addendum 3 — an open product problem parked until the four goals are solved).
6b. **SA18's final loose ends (stopped 19:45):** rail18 breaks — ext10 @1.0/@2.0 one gen-2 frame with different bits each (exactness), gfx @0.25 50 prefix overs (CBR) — causes never investigated; D1a 1V-chroma rejection used 2-level tables (SA18Q: re-measure); ★1 slack and PREVENT bit-cost measurements never run; coarse-first latency margin at 720p50 + 3:1 (9 µs) never given in lines; train_f2 (final-configuration tables) killed. **SA18's coarse-first decode order** (now coarse bands → LL2 → V_2 → level-2/level-1): 8K memory and latency now assessed on PAPER (DESIGN §4: 26.2 Mbit, 2S + 10 lines) but the per-block pipelining of the coarse-first stage is argued, not built (else up to S/2 line times; 53 µs breaks 1 ms at 720p50); the table bank was DROPPED (E10 +0.07/+0.11 NEG; 48 Mbit or ~240 table builds per 31 µs slice); gen-2 exactness now measured for the kf rate control (B2: dng720 5 frames gen 2/gen 3 pictures and bits identical) but not for cfv2 + bank together (E4 crashed: "no consistent fine re-choice"); the share of slices whose level-1 costs more under V_c and the |Δd| / inter slice-edge row phase of the d re-choice were never reported (d since replaced by kf). Never run as SA18P specified: the fast-motion 4-way discriminator (only MC-prediction PSNR, D8) and the D4 bits-per-slice check on volley; PREVENT's matched-rate bits/NEG with the worst-case bound on dng720/cf_gfx/a dark cell; the S5.152 replication; SA18P's IPL questions (why IPL beats SA15 #1 / SA17 #3; aliasing/noise on the kept grid). Also never run: SA13's restricted-cell rail convergence test (its §9 test (i)); SA18P's requested ★1 slack measurement, Dir 1a change-count test, still-area third-way and blue-noise tests, DOL stage (2), IPL renders/other cells, D1a at 0.25/1.0 bpp, other cells and 4:4:4.
7. **Not yet run end to end for the pair-pyramid family's latest designs:** formats other than 4:2:2 10-bit (SA17; SA15 ran them), rails beyond intra (SA16 not run), adversarial DDR timing model (SA15 not re-run), latency and memory as paper figures (SA16/17), renders with legends for the latest runs.
8. **Other recorded items:** CBR reserve (16 bits) covering the canonical emission is measured (≤ 5.5 bits) but not proven (SA17); cut-frame artifactmap regions (SA15 42–44 luma) never inspected by eye; flatness bar (zero flat textured blocks at ≥ 0.5 bpp) met by no design and not by today; SA18's CBR guard d re-choice per slice could step level-1 sharpness at slice edges (rowphase and |Δd| never reported; d replaced by the kf plan index, B2); JPEG XS architecture resemblance of continuous-transform designs flagged for the legal team (coordinator's action, not the designer's).
